# Implementation specification — connected research, recurring supply and useful reports

Implementation requirements for the approved connected nonvisual programme. Section26 owns the narrow-type/sustainable-research design; section28 owns operating-repair requirements; section29 owns the desktop-control design; [section 30](#30-current-source-routes-after-plan-r10-15-october) owns the current source routes after Plan R10 and supersedes earlier route lists where they differ. P1/P2 and other work blocks are dependencies, not routine approval stops. Current status belongs to [Progress](../../../project/pj-tracker.md); implemented operating repairs, loading and proof belong to the [implementation record](../../implementation_2026-10-11.md). The dated section27 checkpoints are not current running-state or completion claims. Visual redesign, client publication, paid upgrades, supplier outreach and unapproved purchases remain separate.

This is the implementation plan: components, exact changes, dependencies, code/AI ownership, recovery and acceptance. The [product brief](../../../product/india_dropshipping_product_opportunity_radar_product_brief.md) owns service policy; the [cost plan](../../build_activation_and_costs_2026-10-03.md) owns financial arithmetic; the [benchmark record](benchmark_record.md) retains historical measurements; the [review](../../../reviews/2026-10-03_execution_plan_review.md) retains requirement cross-checks. Do not append live status or session narratives here.

## 1. Authority, objective and practical boundaries

Provide an India private-label/dropshipping client with useful product opportunities and supplier leads. Normally research once weekly and prepare three **different** private issues for Tuesday, Thursday and Saturday at 09:00 India time. Daily portal access is not daily research. The goal is good recurring recommendations, not merely a valid PDF or successful API response.

Keep Home and Decor, Baby Products, Home and Kitchen, Travel and Outdoor broad. Exclude Pets. Use moderate brand judgment: exclude recognisable mainstream branded products, not unfamiliar manufacturers or retailers automatically. Use ten active original criteria: IDs 1,2,5,6,7,8,9,11,12,13. Retire high margin, past winners and extremely unique (3,4,10). Six or more Meets is Good fit; five is Decent fit. The four combined fit/source priorities in section 20.9 determine selection order. Unknown is not a negative or a free point. Margin, profit and RTO percentage/cost estimates remain excluded. Product-specific practical inference, including approximate size/weight/handling from known form and mechanism, is allowed and labelled Estimated or Inferred. No measured packed weight, exact demographic share, stopwatch result or certification is required for an ordinary sensible checklist judgment.

Each regular issue needs at least ten products, normally 10–15 and up to twenty when genuinely useful. At least 80% are New to this client's complete published history; at most 20% are supported material updates. A smaller-than-ten shortfall requires discussion, not weak padding or silent publication. No obligation to include updates. Perform a real cursory supplier search for every selected product; finding a confirmed supplier is not an eligibility prerequisite. After real bounded supplier work produces no usable lead—including blocked/failed search routes—an otherwise eligible product may still appear with **“Supplier search did not produce any usable results.”** Keep detailed attempt states internally; do not imply the product itself is unavailable.

Owner reports permission for the approved three-account Apify arrangement and Maps supplier discovery. Do not ask again merely to reconfirm these decisions. Approximate Apify targets are US$4.50 use/US$0.50 buffer per funded account, roughly US$13.50/US$1.50 combined if all three US$5 allowances exist. Minor cents do not need micro-approval. No automatic paid upgrade, credit purchase, paid model fallback, supplier outreach or subscription purchase.

The first two logical published issues have English and colloquial Roman Hinglish PDFs plus one shared workbook each. Later issues use the client's choice. Client email is deferred deliberately; continue owner/internal work without inventing it. The existing NeuraFlow hostname, Worker, private bucket and native cost warning are retained. Functional source panels, all checklist reasons, complete supplier details, real product photos when accessible, clickable downloads and private-file correctness are in this programme; visual redesign and marketing-layout approval are not its completion gates.

Implementation updates do not end work. Inspect, change, verify, repair and proceed to the next dependency-ready item until the nonvisual closure tracker is satisfied or every remaining item has a real external/owner-only blocker. A routine failed route, model batch, unknown optional term, smaller milestone or subjective estimate does not end the programme. Save an exact blocker and proceed with unaffected work. Sections 22–23 define the connected interfaces and comprehensive finish evidence.

## 2. Inspected implementation and preservation map

The audit reread the existing source, actual saved research, frozen report, page images, configuration, operating records and live read-only Cloudflare settings. “Implemented” below does not mean complete recurring-service acceptance.

A preview image is not a live product photograph. No blanket reset, old hash substitution, hidden gate weakening, repository refactor or new workflow framework.

## 3. Delivery, money and yield design

Implement the [cost plan](../../build_activation_and_costs_2026-10-03.md) rather than copying historical low-volume illustrations into forecasts. Current collection cost is US$0.50040006, not the all-in fee. Saved legacy-policy readiness is 11/156=7.05% with 38 unfinished, not measured ten-criterion yield; use 5% planning and 3% stress scenarios without treating them as guaranteed bounds. Account for failed/duplicate collection, pending evidence, read charges, direct-source effort, supplier work and AI usage.

A shared weekly capture can serve three issues only if it actually yields enough distinct inventory. Count 12–14 real monthly release dates and actual provider renewals. Bilingual PDFs do not double research. One account's key change never resets its ledger; old reads remain origin-bound. Read holds are retained, not labelled incurred charges.

**Implemented locally, not additional-account activation:** `accounts.py` now supplies `load_account_registry`, `context_for_account`, `context_for_origin` and `choose_account`. Registry entries live in ignored private config; public config stores safe IDs/references, not keys. A new account can be chosen only for a new logical acquisition. Saved attempts retain their original account/period/generation/build/control and charge evidence. A verified replacement credential for that same account may read/recover the original run under an explicit credential-recovery record; immutable generation provenance does not require keeping an expired secret forever. It must not move the run, reset funds or launch again. `research_costs.collection_costs` counts each saved physical collection once and keeps unresolved holds separate; `yield_forecast` calculates labelled lower-yield scenarios without assuming the pooled allowance will cover them. These functions do not activate accounts or contact providers.

The local `MetaTrial`/`ShortJournal` refactor now separates the exact-attempt guard from short reservation, receipt and uncertainty commits; the existing CLI/batch seams use the account context. Verify original-account recovery and eventual real routing together rather than treating three configured key files as acceptance. Its execution sequence is:

1. Obtain the exact-attempt/logical-request launch guard before taking the money lock. If another process owns it, record busy/recovery work and move to independent work within the finite run; never wait for this guard while holding the financial lock.
2. Under the short journal lock, reread active scope, selected account, actual period/headroom, unknown commitments and equivalent task; record the finite reservation and durable submission intent. Recheck stale pre-lock choices here.
3. Release the global financial lock before network launch/status/read work, retaining the exact-attempt guard and durable intent.
4. Reacquire the short financial lock for receipts, charge/capture commit and uncertainty updates; release it before freeing the attempt guard. Research application uses its separately documented writer/short-commit contract, not a nested financial lock.
5. On restart, acquire the same guard and recover saved receipts/remote identity first. Lost response or possibly running job is reconciliation, never permission to POST again.

Do not assume local timeout stops a remote Actor or caps its charge. Preserve provider monetary/runtime controls and original read allowances. Namespace account-specific unresolved launch/collection checks; count every outstanding liability in aggregate planning. Unknown read fees do not block independent saved analysis or every other funded account. Do not infer a fresh balance from the pooled target.

**Recovery across provider renewal — P4, implemented locally:** `accounts.current_read_funding_context`, `MetaTrial.before_request` and `Journal.reserve_renewed_reads` separate original acquisition provenance from today's read funding. They retain the same provider account, original attempt namespace/period/generation/run/dataset and lifetime request/byte limits. A new potentially billed recovery read declares its own stable operation ID, verified current funding namespace, credential-recovery ref, remaining read exposure and reservation before GET. Renewed funds cover that new read, not rewrite the old acquisition or replenish its lifetime controls. Unresolved old holds remain; authenticated charge attribution links exact operations so one fee is not charged to two periods or double-counted against a provider balance. Funding/attribution uncertainty holds that read, not unrelated funded work. Local account-pool/renewal fixtures pass; actual additional-account activation, genuine renewal and billed-read acceptance remain outstanding. The [cost plan](../../build_activation_and_costs_2026-10-03.md#5-multi-account-financial-implementation-and-remaining-activation) owns the financial treatment.

## 4. Work blocks and order

| Block | Change and reason | Main dependencies | Finish evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| P0 — current plan strengthening | Map ten active criteria, actual brand ownership and action-specific continuation across all lanes. | Actual source/runtime/report contracts and independent read-only reviews. | Consistent current/future docs, exact policy migration and bounded recovery contract; production unchanged. |
| P1 — completed standalone Amazon trial | Preserve the direct India Bestsellers feasibility evidence without repeating the experiment. | Actual section 19 artifacts. | Existing attempt/card/ratio/isolation receipts; they do not prove application integration. |
| P1a — upfront catalogue research | Build substantial source-grounded branch inventory independently of charged dispatch. | P0 current catalogue; permitted public/material reads and bounded C seed mode. | Actual family/phrase/source matrix, grounded examples and consumption scenarios; not synonym padding or a six-month guarantee. |
| P1b — direct Amazon promotion | Connect the demonstrated list route through real scheduled capture/import/dispatcher. | P1 actual results and current whole-program authority; applicability contracts. | Correct inspected list scope, replay and usable rows; no fictitious paid preparation or charge. |
| P2 — practical decision completion | Extend the existing ten-ID contract to four fit/source priorities and full practical review of the 153 non-established working ideas. | Current facts, saved judgments/jobs and sections 20.9/21.16. | Each product has a current completed assessment or exact actual unresolved reason; all consumers agree and legacy packets remain unchanged. |
| P3 — functional report completion | Connect real photographs, typed source numbers, full suppliers, truthful limitations and bilingual artifacts using the existing presentation. | Correctly scoped facts and actual source images. | PDFs/Excel/summary agree; links/photos/download bytes and partial-stage recovery work. Visual redesign is separate. |
| P4 — funded multi-account execution | Add approved profiles and phased controller/locks together. | Finance/migration design and focused controller fixtures. | Original-account recovery, aggregate accounting, overlap/crash tests and bounded actual checks within current funded authority. |
| P5 — durable research advancement | Drive saved backlog and feedback fairly; improve query coverage/catalogue growth. | P2 scoped questions, P4 safe charged dispatch, saved AI contracts. | Restart progresses without duplicate collections; finite post-fill work and no starvation. |
| P6 — all agreed source connections | Connect and test supplier directories/Maps, comparators, QC and the direct Meta alternative through section 22. | Small route-specific access/extraction checks and common capture/scope contracts. | Each route is implemented with actual acceptance or a precise observed access blocker and connected continuation; not a list of future pilots. |
| P7 — distinct issues and private delivery | Prepare enough real inventory for two bilingual issues, then third after language choice; prove private transport/release. | Real eligible inventory, P3, owner session; client access only when email supplied. | At least 10 per actual issue, >=80% New, correct private bytes, approval and server timing. |
| P8 — recurring measurement/operation | Small scheduled-service rehearsal and revised cost/capacity forecast. | Proven dispatch/recovery, report assembly and release contract. | Actual cadence/yield/effort/availability receipts, exact unfinished work and operating handoff. |

These are dependency milestones inside one approved continuous programme, not separate sessions or automatic handback points. Start practical saved-product review and shared priority/query contracts immediately; connect direct Amazon and the other source routes while independent saved work proceeds. Add durable dispatch/account routing before unrestricted scheduled charged work, then finish functional reports, private owner proof and recurring acceptance. Do not wait for visual redesign or a confirmed supplier to continue. Filling one issue does not cancel the working-product queue or remaining source integration.

## 5. Source capability and coverage contracts

Represent each route separately. The current route set, roles and retired routes are in [section 30](#30-current-source-routes-after-plan-r10-15-october): Meta (free read and the paid deeper read, one site), Amazon search/bestseller lists and Flipkart search as the crowding check, signed-in Google Trends, Blinkit, Zepto and Instamart (paid, admitted checks), the three dropshipping platforms (DeoDap, BaapStore, Dropdash), IndiaMART (signed in), Google Maps, TradeIndia, company/product/terms pages and product-image reads. Amazon Movers & Shakers and Google Search supplier discovery are retired. “Google works” does not prove Maps or Trends; known TradeIndia page capture does not prove directory discovery.

Each route has source, purpose, country/location, access mode, parser/build/schema version, bounds, tested outcome/time and safe receipt. Each product question has concept/description hash, missing question, semantic request, route/action/attempt references, state and reopening trigger. A route failure does not create a failed product attempt for every concept.

| State dimension | Allowed meaning | Must not imply |
|---|---|---|
| Route capability | Untested / demonstrated in bounded sample / blocked / degraded, with exact route and date. | Every product was checked; permanent reliability; negative demand. |
| Product work | Not prepared / prepared / running / captured / failed / deferred / cancelled / superseded, with actual attempt. | A prepared check actually fetched data. |
| Interpretation | Pending / accepted / rejected / scope unclear, with job/version. | A captured query names the same product. |
| Evidence result | Positive / Not-positive / Insufficient / Unavailable / stale; method and refs. | Failed access is zero demand, or unknown is a negative. |

Record first-request versus later-request failures in browser experiments: attempt sequence/time, URL/session mode, chart/load/export stage, HTTP status, delay and prior success. Do not call every 429 “too-fast rate limiting” or every block a Codex/browser ban without evidence. Pace bounded retries, retain the observed cause as confirmed or suspected, and use a permitted route. Never bypass CAPTCHA/authentication, disable certificate checks or silently purchase a fallback.

Client source coverage displays actual per-product checked/not checked/insufficient/not applicable states, including zero-fee work. A reviewed relevant quick-commerce result may supply neutral distinct-site support under the current policy, not sales proof or automatic reasonably-positive support. Audit all 156 current ideas against every agreed route; dispatch each applicable route at least once or record genuine applicable saved coverage, actual failure, or a specific not-applicable reason. Relevant shared list/family acquisitions can answer several product questions after separate target relevance review. A shared route outage is not a product attempt. The report does not wait for every inaccessible optional source, and must not claim omitted work happened. Section 22 defines route-specific implementations and section 23 owns the completion accounting.

## 6. Records, scope and migrations

Keep the existing append-only events as authoritative and local indexes as rebuildable derivatives. Add fields through explicit schema versions and adapters, not ad hoc None values in validators requiring a family. Preserve old reports/jobs under their original contract.

Planned request scope is one of `family`, `list`, `concept`. Family has catalogue family/phrase; list has the inspected list URL/node and country; concept has target concept/description/question binding. Shared semantic acquisition identity includes source, purpose, country/location, normalized query or real list URL, filters, bounds, parser/build and relevant options, not an arbitrary caller family ID. Persist actual acquisition ID independently of import time.

An original source item is keyed by source/acquisition/row identity. Re-downloading or remapping one capture creates no fresh observation. A genuinely new collection may return identical bytes yet have a real new date. Changed bytes under one immutable capture ID are a conflict or explicit replacement. Mixed-product A child IDs derive from the saved accepted output and index; reordering a later interpretation cannot silently create new product history.

**Planned product-scope review record:** target concept, current description hash, source/evidence/acquisition IDs, content/query hash, original acquisition date, relationship `exact_product|relevant_family|unrelated|unclear`, reason, method, reviewer/job/prompt version and superseded binding if any. A hash proves unchanged bytes, not relevance. Apply semantic scope review consistently in import, market calculations, candidate summary, questions, selection, facts and reservation fingerprints.

Legacy migration reads old records through their own schema. Backfill only fields justified by actual receipts. Do not label every inherited global source status as a past attempt, replace old dates, recompute historical financial balances or bulk-transform accepted judgments without an explicit correction.

## 7. Identity, eligibility and audit ownership

Code owns source-ID lookup, caches, bounded candidate retrieval, stable IDs/redirects, publication history, policy-bound active-ID fit counts/tiers, evidence arithmetic, selection, money, schedule and output validation. AI owns supplied-material interpretation, semantic identity/relevance, reasonable benefits, supplier phrasing and narration. Hybrid steps use code before and after the bounded AI judgment.

The full detailed identity/search contracts are in section 20. Every stage records included/excluded/deferred/not assessed/duplicate, primary reason and other applicable reasons, supporting refs, rule version and current/older distinction. Preserve all encountered rows within documented capture bounds, including noise and partial pages. Do not claim complete-market coverage.

At the saved legacy-policy checkpoint,107 ideas are set aside and 38 unfinished;145 are not all “rejected.” The 86 legacy lower-checklist ideas have score bins 0:1,1:3,2:1,3:14,4:18,5:19,6:30. These bins are not new five-/six-of-ten readiness counts. Internal candidate HTML/JSON shows the ten active rows plus optional original legacy judgments, clearly labelled by policy, not a second manual audit workbook.

## 8. AI roles and proportional cost

Use existing chosen signed-in GPT-6 Luna/High route and isolated no-tool runner. External content is supplied data, never instructions or executable code. No secrets, account/client records or unrelated files are sent. No automatic paid fallback or second checker model.

| Role | Supplied input and purpose | Output / code-owned action |
|---|---|---|
| A — description/triage | Bounded unfamiliar or meaningfully changed source excerpts and IDs. | Product/service distinction, descriptions/features, category and `generic_or_small`, `established` or `unclear` brand judgment. Code validates and routes the result; unfamiliar names and unclear brands are not automatic exclusions. See section 21.18 for current versus planned corrections. It is not final qualification. |
| B — identity | One described item and useful bounded possible matches, aliases/function/features/history context. | Same/different/unclear, supplied matched IDs, distinguishing features/reason. Code applies safe unprotected associations or saves ambiguity. |
| C — catalogue renewal | Compact real gaps, recent outcomes and coherent new product descriptions. | Up to five distinct proposals: existing-family variant/new family/validation/revisit/none, grounding and overlap explanation. Code deduplicates and admits; AI cannot launch a search. |
| D — visual ambiguity | One target, up to three candidates and four real checked images, with labels/context. | Visible same/different/unclear with image refs and limitations. Missing views cannot prove different. |
| E — checklist/relevance/update | Current product facts and relevant evidence; use a named relevance-only or checklist-only request where that is the missing task. | Exactly the requested original active IDs under the new E fit contract (ten for full assessment), source relationships or material-change reason. Code counts active Meets/derives fit tier and calculates signals/history; legacy E stays readable. |
| F — supplier interpretation | Product plus actual supplier/company/offer/terms excerpts and source refs. | Match/supply arrangement, separate capability/fee/price/MOQ fields and conflicts. Code validates known IDs/units/ranking. |
| G — writing/localisation | Frozen checked facts, limitations, allowed fact IDs and writing slots; English meaning for Hinglish. | Concise supported phrases/prose; code owns numerical panels and validates exact references/language. |

Current production `Jobs.run` permits 1–10 items and a 256 KiB serialised byte bound; `CodexRunner` has a 180-second model deadline. Public config's 90 seconds governs the separate connection probe, not this research runner. Do not describe a character count as a byte bound or admit twelve A records into the existing ten-item contract. Initial planned soft targets are roughly 24,000 supplied characters and small compatible chunks (at most ten short A records, usually up to three disputed B items), below the actual hard bounds. Timeout remains the declared runner's actual limit unless deliberately changed and tested. Split oversized **not-yet-launched** inputs at intact item/claim boundaries; after an intent exists, recover its exact bindings rather than re-chunking to bypass it. Preserve omitted count; no role on every row or trimmed claim presented as complete.

Persist logical job intent, exact relevant input/dependency hashes and execution ID before invocation; save bounded response/usage/exit receipt before temporary cleanup. Execution state, validation state and usage completeness are separate. Accept valid siblings, reject only bad items; no rerun of accepted siblings. Reconcile saved output before another model call. At most one schema repair within the finite allowance; accepted-result policy corrections need the named correction path, not repeated generation until a desired fit tier appears.

G keeps current strict allowed fact-reference IDs/output slots. The original Roman-Hinglish correction remains separately versioned and unchanged. Current G-v2/v3 also has one saved exact-item correction for a completed shape/slot/reference/placeholder/plain-text/checklist failure, including a genuinely missing or duplicated result. Its frozen context and actual rejected rows are preserved; valid siblings are not rewritten. A failed correction is reused, not retried until it gives a preferred answer. Use current price-display-v2 and old-version compatibility unchanged.

Measure roles/calls/items, supplied/reused content, input/output tokens where given, elapsed/timeout and unknown usage. Do not translate subscription tokens into invented dollars. Publish AI judgement as judgement where needed, not proof of sales, supplier capability or identity guarantees.

## 9. Supplier and external-content contracts

Section 21.7 defines the workflow. All external text/model outputs are untrusted. Bound public reads and redirects, allow only appropriate public HTTP(S) destinations, reject credential-bearing/private/local URLs, prevent rebinding on redirects, retain TLS verification, cap bodies and decoded images. Existing HTML-only readers must not pretend to download image bytes successfully.

Escape HTML and spreadsheet formula prefixes. Source text cannot call tools, read files, change policies or spend money. Do not click checkout/forms, message suppliers, create accounts or submit contact details. Credentials live only in ignored private files or private in-memory sessions and never in client PDFs/Excel, logs or manifests.

## 10. Finite advancement and recovery

The implemented `programme.advance_programme` is the small direct dispatcher over existing plans/jobs/questions, not a broker or agent framework; [section25](#25-n22-connected-programme-and-native-schedule) owns its exact interfaces and acceptance boundary. A run has a persisted scope/version and reuses lower-layer action/item/model/spend limits and deadlines. Distinguish **one issue filled** from **run allowance finished**. After filling an issue, freeze its selection and continue remaining budgeted useful work for future issues; do not grant a fresh allowance.

Distinguish restarting that run from a genuinely new scheduled research period. Same-period restart retains its counters and expiry; the next due period gets one explicit finite work envelope under the standing schedule, not new provider money. Admit still-useful unfinished tasks by reference and retain their original executions. Section 21.4 defines the transition, including unselected work, missed starts and cancellation; an exhausted run must neither silently reset nor trap the backlog forever.

Priority: recover exact pending work; finish practical current assessments and already captured material; complete applicable source checks; cursory supplier work; then fresh discovery/catalogue renewal. The initial practical re-review includes all 153 non-established working ideas, including older unknowns and apparent low scores. Subsequent unchanged completed lower-fit judgments need not be regenerated. Age-based fairness admits deferred ready work; one repeatedly unproductive product cannot consume the whole allowance. A flat chart or unmet old growth threshold is not automatically a weak-product exclusion.

Each action commits outcome and dependency refs before next selection. An unrelated catalogue/import change does not reopen a failed question. Deferred work has a precise trigger: new evidence, next relevant batch, route recovery, changed language choice or owner action. Do not poll unchanged deferred items continuously.

Section 21.17 is the required action-level continuation contract. Recovery-first means **trigger-ready** recovery, not revisiting an unchanged uncertain action on every iteration. An unavailable route leaves other eligible items/lanes running. If no safe dependency-ready work exists, save the queue and consumed allowance, return a waiting summary and resume on its actual trigger; do not spin, fabricate progress or reset the allowance.

Failures save action/task/job/attempt, stage, affected concept/issue/source, expected/actual, stable reason, redacted evidence/exception, confirmed/suspected cause, charge/remote-running uncertainty, next action/trigger and independent work available. Group repeated route failures in one operator message with counts. Lack of optional data is not a whole-program exception.

## 11. Output, mistake correction and package integrity

Changes must be made to repeatable source logic, not manually patched PDFs or model answers. Correct visible price labels, scope, units, contradictory source interpretation and missing fields at their owning layer. Add regression cases and rebuild affected **new revisions**. No format validator can guarantee semantic truth; combine code invariants, targeted contradiction checks and actual review.

**Implemented bounded G correction:** `report_narration._correct_contract_item` accepts only an actual completed current-G-v2/v3 rejection whose exact original request, item binding, saved response and allowed writing/reference hints verify. The separately saved `one-item-report-contract-correction-v1` scope supplies one original frozen item plus its rejected row(s), original response hash and failure code. The rules require the complete slots, actual allowed fact references, literal approved numerical placeholders and unchanged protected limitation/source English. Unsupported text is removed, not treated as true or turned into a new benefit. The ordinary strict validator checks the corrected answer. Missing/duplicate/malformed result envelopes can receive one bounded correction per affected supplied item; they are not interpreted as absent demand. No second correction chain is created for a failed correction, including when the caller/batch size changes or a prepared retry exists. Accepted original/corrected items replay without a model. Transport/login/provider/unknown-execution failures, malformed input and explicit unsupported forecast/commercial verdicts are not contract-repair triggers. Genuine G-v1 and the original Roman-repair request shapes remain unchanged. This mechanism corrects bounded output-contract mistakes; it does not claim semantic infallibility or replace the source/price/supplier checks.

`report_selection._facts()` remains the bridge to the report. Its current marked structure carries actual source metrics/outcomes, original photo provenance, all ten active reasons and accepted supplier terms without another report database. `report_workflow.prepare_review_package` freezes the exact issue/facts before G/rendering. All formats derive the same numerical and source facts; section21.6 specifies the new marker and byte/reference contract.

Presentation-only reuse is valid only when exact writing inputs remain unchanged and source packet/manifest/hashes validate. Adding photographs or structured factual fields normally changes frozen facts: create a fact-aware new revision, and reuse individual writing only if its exact dependency inputs still match. Do not remove a photo/metric from fingerprints, copy an old hash or weaken same-facts validation to force reuse.

The renderer supports new and preserved legacy dataset/price contracts explicitly. Existing accepted packet revisions stay immutable. Cross-format validation checks content meaning as well as bytes: amount/quote/basis/pack/MOQ, source date/location, supplier arrangement, signal metric and checklist state must match in English, Hinglish and workbook.

## 12. Private portal: preserve the small supported design

Canonical hostname: `dropshipper.neuraflow.co.in`; root/latest report, archive/report routes and downloads share it. A www alias is optional and not necessary for first delivery; do not mix competing hostnames. Selected private bucket: `neuraflow-dropshipping-mr-mu-ad-product-reports`. Leave other buckets, Stock Planner and other NeuraFlow projects alone.

The direct Cloudflare Worker serves approved private packages independently of the local computer. Whole-hostname Access protects all client/report/download/admin routes. Use the direct Worker's trusted `ctx.access` identity plus configured audience and explicit operator/client allowlists; raw headers are not identity proof. Google sign-in is configured; no Everyone, Bypass, all-Gmail-domain policy, device enrolment, Tunnel or new organisation is needed.

Existing `portal/release-admin.mjs` already uses immutable create-if-absent objects, byte/hash checks, actual R2 ETags and conditional index writes. Preserve and test these, not last-write-wins. Supported references: [Worker Access](https://developers.cloudflare.com/workers/configuration/cloudflare-access/) and [R2 Workers API](https://developers.cloudflare.com/r2/api/workers/workers-api-reference/).

Owner browser login proves guarded access, not local uploader credentials. The supported short-lived owner `cloudflared` session stays private/in-memory; fixed-origin `portal_storage.py` has bounded owned requests, no arbitrary redirects/keys and no blind write retries. Generate one finite login when owner is available, not expired links repeatedly. Connected management automation handles settings it supports; ask the owner only at real named-user or unavailable-capability boundaries.

Native US$1 [budget alert](https://developers.cloudflare.com/billing/manage/budget-alerts/) remains enabled. It monitors account-wide spend and notifies; it is not a project-specific meter, fixed-subscription alert or charge cutoff. Provider test is verified; inbox/real-threshold delivery is not. No new custom polling monitor, analytics store or deliberate spend test.

## 13. Schedule, history, corrections and retention

Use Asia/Kolkata Tuesday/Thursday/Saturday 09:00 =03:30 UTC. Generate actual calendar dates, including partial/fifth weeks. Collection dates never become release dates. The server selects a complete approved active revision only once its release time is reached; no local always-on process is needed for uploaded releases.

Availability begins at the later of approved activation and scheduled time while that revision is active. Local publication history reconciles server-recorded availability intervals, not downloads/current archive alone. Staged objects are not published. Retain reservations until remote replacement/withdrawal is confirmed, and keep any product already available in complete client history.

Corrections are new revisions of the same logical issue, not extra issues or New products. Never overwrite previously frozen or published bytes. Preserve prior package/hash/decision provenance; withdrawn downloads disappear from normal portal access but cannot be recalled from a client's computer.

Check supporting-source freshness against the exact intended release timestamp, not only its date, before final dependent approval/activation. Current allocation uses a date-prefix comparison; replace that seam and include exact valid-until metadata in the new package contract. If affected data expires by Saturday 09:00, refresh only that evidence within the funded scope or record a specific reviewed coverage/freshness exception; preserve original dates and recompute dependent signals. Do not weaken the required eligibility rules, block unrelated issues or launch blanket recollection. A late approval rechecks the actual availability instant. Server activation validates the supplied source-validity/exception contract without itself launching research. This freshness decision concerns intended first availability, not every archive download; historical reports remain readable with their original dates rather than expiring whenever viewed.

Keep source/history/financial evidence until an explicit owner-approved retention policy. Compact indexes retain lifetime novelty without rereading80 reports. Proposed12-month portal archive is a storage/owner decision, not permission to delete raw work. Back up essential records/packages and nonsecret code/settings to owner-selected same-project `data/backups` and prove exact-copy/isolated-restore behaviour. This does not protect against whole-drive loss; no independent-destination choice blocks current research or creates a new cloud release gate.

## 14. Rehearsal and user-only boundaries

Separate offline structural tests, real route acceptance, product semantic review, actual visual/meaning review and private release proof. An HTTP 200, accepted model schema, nonempty PDF, valid manifest or1443 old tests do not prove the full service.

Client email and ongoing language remain future inputs. Do not repeatedly ask for them while independent work is ready. Routine public-source tests, model interpretation and funded-account implementation are authorised in this programme. Exact owner package approval and a finite named-owner uploader sign-in are needed only at their actual boundaries; private owner staging/readback is not client publication. Unapproved paid additions, outreach, purchases and materially new scope require discussion.

The first-delivery date 13 October 2026 is a planning target, not an approved guarantee. Enough inventory for one 11-product draft does not prove two bilingual ten-product issues or a30-product week.

## 15. Verification ownership

Every implementation block finishes its relevant deterministic tests, practical sample/actual output inspection, known-defect repair and regression replay before it is called complete. Use proportionate focused tests while implementing, then the agreed integrated rehearsal. Keep fake fixtures and actual provider tests distinct. Preserve immutable inputs/results when a review fails.

The detailed matrix in section 21.12 covers identity, source scope, account routing, concurrency, reports, schedules and failures. “No known remaining defect in the reviewed block” is the finish condition; never promise an error-free or guaranteed-yield programme.

## 16. Current next action and exclusions

Continue the existing joined nonvisual programme, starting with the confirmed operating repairs in section28, then the dependency order of section4 and closure tracker in section23. Four fit/source priorities, shared family-query profiles, all14 source adapters, the durable controller, funded-account routing, schedule, functional report pipeline and private-owner staging/downloads are connected, but the10October audit found an actual unfinished source-stage walk and incomplete operational visibility. Finish those repairs, current practical reviews, actual applicable source work and interpretation, useful supplier facts/photos, current replacement report versions and recurring recovery proof. Preserve successful original acquisitions and immutable packets rather than build another driver or repeat work to change wording. Review and repair each ready slice, then proceed without another milestone approval. Progress reports state exactly what ran, what remains and the precise blocker, not that a pending answer is a rejection.

Prior P1/P2 results are useful saved evidence, not proof these extensions work. Normal bounded tests do not broaden spending or client-publication authority. A failed source may pause only its affected actions; if genuine access makes a route impossible, retain its tested blocker and alternatives while other work proceeds.

Excluded from this programme: frontend/UI/UX/visual redesign, fully hosted research, client-triggered research, supplier outreach/verification, margin/RTO modelling, automatic purchasing, unapproved paid services, public marketing rebuild and multi-client administration. Functional report photos, numbers, suppliers and downloads are not excluded design work.

## 17. Paid runner contract to preserve during refactor

Before a charged request, prove exact account/period/scope/config/build, supported provider bounds, reservation and durable intent. Include result-read allowance. Use exact saved Actor/run/dataset identity for controls/status/capture. Requested rows/local slices are not provider charge caps. Reconcile potentially running/unknown submissions before a new POST; original run ID/account always win over current active key.

Terminal charges may rise later; append authenticated observations without rewriting old events or double-counting charges already reflected in balance. Paid GETs are not automatically safe retries. Existing known controls/mapping/quality states stay separate. A quality-poor acquisition can still be fully charged and safely accounted.

## 18. Mapping, freshness and scope reuse

Raw transport, mapper acceptance, semantic relevance and market quality are distinct. Replay parsing/mapping from saved captures without collection. Cache identity description by meaning-relevant content; cache market assessment by source/acquisition freshness and exact scope. Independent new observations can reuse unchanged identity after supporting-content review without changing original interpretation date.

Amazon semantic identity currently excludes structured price/status; Meta can still include price/image URL in text. Plan additive component hashes for identity text/features, volatile offer/availability and image content/context. Separate an amount only when the source parser identifies a structured field or clear price/offer span; otherwise preserve ambiguous prose and interpret its changed meaning rather than stripping arbitrary numbers. Unchanged reviewed descriptions can be reused only with justified supporting-content bindings. Do not strip arbitrary numbers from prose: litres, voltage, age and dimensions may be meaningful. Existing saved fingerprints remain readable; new digest versions affect only appropriate future dependencies.

A same query across two products can reuse one fresh controls-checked acquisition. Each target still needs independent relevance/outcome review; do not copy the first product's positive signal. Scope-purpose/country/options changes remain non-equivalent.

## 19. Standalone direct Amazon experiment

**Completed isolated experiment; [measured result and limitations](benchmark_record.md#9-standalone-direct-amazon-india-bestsellers-result) are recorded in the benchmark record.** Six corrected visits across three departments captured180 rows with prices/photo links; five initial price-incomplete visits and one local startup failure remain retained. Production integration is not proved by that result; it is now authorised within the continuous nonvisual programme. Preserve the isolated trial modules/artifacts and implement a separate production adapter rather than altering the original experiment. Small standalone modules: `src/product_radar/amazon_browser_trial.py`, `scripts/trial_amazon_bestsellers.py`, `tests/test_amazon_browser_trial.py`. No new narrative plan.

1. Inspect actual public Amazon India Bestsellers landing/list links. Select 2–4 real list cases across useful client categories where real links exist; never invent node IDs or substitute US lists/search results for Bestsellers.
2. For each case persist trial case ID, requested URL, intended country/list/node, bound of one page/up to100 observed cards, browser mode and attempt sequence. Start a fresh owned public browser context with ordinary access; no account/cart/form actions, proxies or CAPTCHA bypass.
3. Run two finite rounds. Keep the whole-case deadline (initially no more than60 seconds per navigation/capture) separate from request spacing (approximately60 seconds between list attempts), and adapt finite retries to actual failures. Prefer a later-date repeat when practical; a same-day round proves repeatability, not market renewal. A deliberate repeat is a new acquisition; replaying a saved page is not another network attempt.
4. Save requested/final URL, actual scope, time, status/load/challenge stage, bounded HTML/text, screenshot, content hash, elapsed time and complete/partial/error reason. HTTP 200 challenge pages are failures, not success.
5. Parse genuine list cards: ASIN where present, title, product URL, explicit list position, raw price/currency/basis, photo URL and original card ref. Deduplicate ASIN within one acquisition while preserving collisions/variants; missing price/image can coexist with a usable listing. Returned order is not invented sales volume.
6. Validate relevant list/country; separate usable cards from navigation/advertising/noise and save omitted/error counts. Replay exact saved captures offline with zero network. Do not call A/B/E/D or assess recommendation fit in this standalone trial.
7. Report access-success/attempts, correct-list-success/attempts, parsed-card completeness and usable-capture/attempts. Show failures/partial results by case and round. Unique ASIN/title examples measure extraction; do not present them as distinct functional products or qualified report yield.
8. Retain successful and failed artifacts. Their completed isolated-trial evidence informs the production integration now authorised in this programme. Test Movers on real inspected links; if blocked, record the observed access boundary and permitted alternatives without halting other routes.

**Promotion is a connected implementation milestone, not a new approval pause.** The working experimental transport is the connected browser tool with fresh empty contexts; the Python local startup timed out before navigation. Diagnose that startup boundary using existing executables/driver stages and prove an ordinary capture from the environment the scheduled programme will actually use. Reuse an existing working native/managed browser where appropriate; do not assume the interactive Codex connection is automatically unattended application transport or purchase a proxy. This is an operational connection check, not a reliability guarantee.

Current `research_batch._verify_preparation` is Meta-only, `import_saved_attempt` requires a committed paid attempt, and `research_sources.amazon_scope` recognises only the pinned kitchen/baby lists. Add a direct-capture validation/import helper and source-specific dispatch branch; do not fabricate an Actor preparation or charge. The new scoped list mapper requires a positively recognised inspected requested list/node and matching observed country/list identity; two unrecognised URLs both mapping to None are never scope proof. Preserve existing paid import/control recovery and legacy mappings unchanged. Store real direct acquisition/provenance, original list rank/quote/image refs, normal descriptions/meaning review and truthful source feedback. Carry observed location when shown, optional-price/image coverage and inspected extractor identity; preserve same-card modern/legacy quote handling. Test broader real category links, wrong/unknown scope, replay/no charge and same-acquisition reuse before enabling the route in recurring plans.

The trial must not import/instantiate `ResearchMemory`, paid controllers, production journals, catalogue expansion, report allocation or model runners. Protect production financial/research/catalogue/history/report hashes before/after. Tests cover challenge 200, wrong country/list, missing IDs, duplicate cards, contradictory rank/price, pack quote unknowns, legitimate empty versus parser failure, safe timeout/owned-process cleanup, replay and isolation.

No Apify charge was incurred by this trial. Capture elapsed time is measured; total development/review effort is not separately metered. Success means a useful route, not a completed application adapter; the reviewed connected transition is required within current authority.

## 20. Recurring discovery and product memory implementation plan

### 20.1 Objective, scope and proof boundary

Control incremental provider expense and avoid unnecessary new AI work; allow useful overlapping **free local collection** even when repeats are likely. Recognise known records cheaply after collection and reuse their accepted interpretations. Reducing repeats is a paid-efficiency preference, not a universal ban on similar free searches. No system can guarantee all-new results or six-month supply. Section26 specifies the cost-aware selection and renewable discovery mechanism; collection repetition, AI effort and client novelty are three separate decisions.

Use broad client categories with narrow functional families/questions, inspected lists and useful advertiser/product routes. For example, research sink splash control, draining/storage and specific travel packing mechanisms rather than repeatedly “Facebook baby products.” A newly found travel mechanism can become a focused Meta validation question and a distinct catalogue area without qualifying as a recommendation first.

<a id="202-current-catalogue-and-exact-runway-measure"></a>

### 20.2 Catalogue seed baseline and current runway measure

The preserved effective seed checkpoint for revision `2026-10-06-c0-3` contains224 functional families,28 subareas,438 family/phrase pairs and677 source-purpose options. This is a dated effective view, including accepted wording at that checkpoint, not a claim that the literal static JSON alone contains every option. The table and illustrative runway below belong to that baseline, not the current expanded catalogue. These are different denominators. Some phrases are alternatives; do not multiply every family by every phrase or equate source options with paid searches.

The [benchmark's effective view](benchmark_record.md#4-exact-research-catalogue-and-consumption), dated8 October2026,19:32:12UTC, reports240 families,482 family/phrase pairs and722 source-purpose choices. The earlier18:42:51UTC view had233/470/710 and separately verified32/229 mapped Meta discovery choices tried,197 not dispatched. Exact consumption was not refreshed in the19:32 read, so it retains its original date. Accepted additions and separately mapped consumption change that view without rewriting the seed. Unmapped product/source questions are not automatically tried seed choices or additional unused capacity.

| Seed category | Seed families | Seed family/phrase pairs | Seed Meta discovery options | Seed marketplace + Trends validation options | Targeted discovery options tried at baseline | Untouched discovery options at baseline |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Home and Decor | 56 | 110 | 58 | 112 | 6 | 52 |
| Baby Products | 56 | 109 | 57 | 112 | 8 | 49 |
| Home and Kitchen | 56 | 109 | 57 | 112 | 9 | 48 |
| Travel and Outdoor | 56 | 110 | 57 | 112 | 7 | 50 |
| **Total** | **224** | **438** | **229** | **448** | **30** | **199** |

At that seed checkpoint, the30 tried options covered26 families, including4 related alternate phrases. Four named product-validation queries and one legacy broad query were separate from those30. The448 validation options are224 marketplace and224 Trends options, not Meta validation. This baseline did not establish complete marketplace/Trends catalogue-option linkage, so these448 options cannot truthfully all be called tried or untried from this table.

At an illustrative 30 previously untried discovery options per report, 199 remaining options represent about 6.63 such workloads, **not six months**. Queries can renew and successful list routes may cover more ideas, but useful yield and expiry govern actual runway. No family is “exhausted” merely because one phrase was attempted.

The upfront planning display should compare useful available questions with roughly26 weeks/78 issues:780 minimum entries, at least624 New only if the maximum useful updates exist, otherwise780 New. An illustrative30 new discovery choices per issue would need2,340 choices across that period; the seed checkpoint's199 untouched choices cannot be described as that supply. This is a sensitivity case, not a mandatory2,340-term quota: direct lists, different source mix, reuse, real family yield and meaningful renewal change consumption. Use the existing catalogue/coverage exports for the effective matrix and an explicit breadth/shortfall statement; organising the224-family seed alone cannot establish six-month supply.

The existing matrix/coverage export derives from catalogue/history: category, subarea, family, phrase ID/text, source/purpose/location/options, tested capability, distinct attempt/acquisition/result/cost, never-tried/tried/deferred, current yield/repeat rate and next eligible revisit. Print totals by each denominator plus exact overlap, and retain explicit limitations where actual linkage is missing. Record trials separately from production consumption. Do not count URL replay/model remap as another search.

### 20.3 Persistent records and request vocabulary

Reuse catalogue family/phrase, semantic request, collection task/acquisition, observation, described item, concept, scope review, assessment, missing question, supplier company/offer, model job, issue allocation and publication history. Add only the fields needed at the existing seams.

Store first seen/last observed separately from first/last published. A known unpublished product is valid carryover, not an unseen discovery; new seller/colour creates neither a new concept nor client novelty. History includes all published issues and queued allocations, not just the last report.

Record a selected question separately from a physical acquisition. One shared acquisition can answer several logical questions after scope review; count physical cost once and each target's outcome separately.

### 20.4 Product identity and economical matching

Layer 1 deterministic lookup: source item/ad IDs, acquisition rows, accepted product URLs/aliases and current identity digest. Exact same immutable row reuses description/association. URLs/seller names alone are not global functional identity and different URLs do not prove different products.

Layer 2 deterministic retrieval: maintained function/use-case/mechanism/distinctive feature/related-family indexes across categories. Prioritise exact reviewed anchors and distinguishing overlap; penalise explicit contradictions, never missing features. Existing `find_possible_matches` has meaningful token retrieval; full weighted alias/URL features are planned additions, not already proved. Return up to10 useful candidates, source/tie/omitted counts and comparison version.

Layer 3 bounded B semantic judgment: compare the new description with only that shortlist, ignoring ordinary colour/seller/pack differences. Validate supplied IDs and feature/reason support. Supported same links to existing concept; supported functional difference can create a new concept; unclear saves a named ambiguity. If there is genuinely no useful candidate, preserve the existing `no_known_match` route: a coherent in-scope idea may enter provisionally without an empty B call or mandatory photo. Recheck relevant new index entries and history before final New allocation. One widened local retrieval up to20 is allowed if weak/crowded/contradictory, with bounded chunks. If still unresolved or allowance exhausted, do not fabricate New.

Examples: identical manual vegetable choppers under different seller names are one concept; a genuinely heated meal container versus an unheated insulated box may be different. Decorative motif/theme/finish and ordinary material changes do not automatically create another functional concept; retain the implemented bookend-family rule and its examples. A material distinction matters only where the use/mechanism meaningfully changes. Same stock image alone does not prove identity. A/B decisions are best-effort practical judgments, not guarantees.

Accept ordinary supported unprotected judgments; material contradictions or merges affecting published/queued history get targeted review. Normal merges apply only where current protection permits. For held/uploaded/published IDs, section26 specifies a reviewed compatibility/history projection rather than forcing a merge; splits never reset client exposure automatically. Preserve stable IDs/history and old frozen bytes. Recheck affected scope/checklist/supplier/chart/allocation dependencies, not the whole catalogue.

### 20.5 Research catalogue, exploration and source roles

Category -> subarea (optional narrower sub-subarea path) -> functional family -> useful phrase -> source-purpose request. Keep stable family IDs/history when placing a family under a more useful branch; taxonomy movement is not a new product or renewed query. Annotate observed product examples with source/date/ref, plausible extensions separately, meaningful mechanism/benefit distinctions, phrase intent and alternate/revisit policy.

Prepare a substantial upfront six-month-oriented inventory through public category/list/supplier research and bounded AI organisation, not by executing every future paid search. The fixed224-family seed is a start to audit/extend, not evidence that six months are sorted; section20.2 points to the dated expanded effective view. Compute breadth against measured weekly consumption/yield and renewal, not eight families/week by assumption.

Add distinct problems/mechanisms/user contexts with meaningful inquiry. Useful synonyms and similar free searches remain allowed under the same research identity; they do not inflate the number of distinct types or prove fresh inventory. Reuse an unchanged exact request unless there is a recorded refresh/depth/filter or other useful new question. Cross-source lists and advertiser routes expand useful coverage without claiming every page is new. Preserve broad category names and all-category/category/focus modes. Section26 separates renewable local work slices from funded-provider and model admission.

### 20.6 Deterministic planner, feedback and stop/revisit rules

1. Read current capabilities, scoped questions, publication/queued history, catalogue feedback, freshness, funded headroom and exact in-flight work.
2. Recover submitted/uncertain tasks first. Reuse complete fresh equivalent acquisitions and saved interpretations before collection.
3. Admit dependency-ready questions; skip deferred ones until their recorded trigger changes. Adequate evidence completes that validation question, not every optional free exploration question about that type. A new source, grounded alternate wording, inspected child list or due meaningful refresh can still be useful under section26.
4. Rank useful near-ready missing checks, untouched families, actual high-yield follow-ups and due meaningful revisits. Balance categories when useful without equal quotas or weak selections. Use age/fairness so one family does not monopolise work.
5. Select one bounded action or compatible interpretation chunk under the existing run limits. Persist reason, exact inputs, before state and budget.
6. Execute; save actual capture/outcome/cost, interpret as needed and update feedback before choosing the next action. Do not preselect an entire paid batch and describe it as adaptive.
7. End at action/item/model/spend/deadline bound or no dependency-ready work. Filling an issue ends its assembly selection, not all remaining research.

Feedback tracks exact-repeat records, already-known concepts, genuinely new concepts, related-but-unusable output, meaningful validation progress, useful supplier leads, required work unfinished and cost. Repeated low-usefulness changes priority/cooldown or requests one grounded alternative; it does not repeatedly ask C to invent terms until success.

The existing paid planner uses seven-day equivalent-coverage reuse, fourteen-day cooldown after two comparable reviewed repeat-heavy samples, and one diagnostic-root grounded paid alternative. Preserve saved attempts under that original policy. For new planning, scope paid cooldown to the charged route/request class; it must not prohibit independent free source/wording exploration. Free work uses complete request-scope reuse, provider-specific access waits and fairness rather than a family-wide repetition ban. Pending interpretations/source failures are not zero-yield samples. The three-unexplored/one-useful-revisit mix is a preference, not four forced calls. Uncertain charged/unknown-fee execution retains original-only recovery; section26.6 separately permits a bounded known-free read-only recovery after proved old-process exit. Section26 supersedes universal application of old paid defaults without resetting histories/funds.

Default freshness: relevant market/Trends captures up to7 days for shared weekly issues, company terms up to30 days unless contradicted. Deliberate research revisits can use source/date/filter/context changes with recorded reason. No automatic ever-deeper pagination or artificial guarantee of non-repetition.

### 20.7 Reuse, rejection and material updates

Use interpretation-content hashes separately from acquisition time and offer state. Reuse A for unchanged identity, B for unchanged relevant shortlist, F company terms only at their supported scope, G only for exact writing inputs. Changed price/availability recalculates offer/update fields, not every role; changed mechanism/age/function reruns affected description/matching.

A cached “no match” is reconsidered when relevant concepts/aliases/merges enter the index. Model prompt-version change invalidates only that role's decisions. Preserve previous versions, original results and named corrections.

Material update requires a supported change useful to this client: a materially different supplier arrangement, availability/pack/price change with conditions, or comparable strengthened/changed core signal. New seller, colour, same chart replay, different policy/coverage or another unchanged ad is not automatically a useful update. E can describe change; code checks actual refs, comparability and client history.

### 20.8 Browser-first Trends and exact scope

Use the already working browser/chart/export path, without Apify fallback. Freeze target description, intended phrase/geo/date range, exact acquisition and original CSV; validate schema/timestamps/partial flags. Then review whether that **actual phrase/result** means the target product. A recognised concept ID and description hash alone do not prove correct attachment.

The known LL21 solar animal-repeller attachment uses a `bike wheel lights` capture. Append an explicit unrelated/superseding binding for LL21; preserve the incorrect event, original CSV/date/job. Attach the original capture to the bike product only after its own relevance review; do not copy LL21's interpretation or invent a new collection date. This wrong binding currently contributes Insufficient, so it does not qualify LL21 or invalidate the eleven selected products by association.

Reuse stable relationship values exact_product/relevant_family/unrelated/unclear; relevant-family evidence can be useful without identical seller SKU/colour. Relevance-only E uses current description plus actual query/capture excerpts, not a complete new checklist. Every consumer uses the applicable same scope review, including Rising support, summary, selection and report refs.

Within bounded access trials, record chart success separately from export success and import/binding success. Disabled Rising or sparse timeline is missing support, not a browser failure or profitable/negative product. Preserve zero-baseline rules rather than trying repeatedly until a positive chart appears.

### 20.9 Evidence-to-report and finance integration

Compute core group facts from fresh **accepted relevant** observations. The current implementation's numerical growth/ad thresholds below are retained as informative measurements during migration, not universal exclusion rules:

- Meta: >=3 distinct relevant advertisers OR >=5 relevant active ads and oldest relevant active ad >=14 days. Deduplicate genuine parent ad IDs/placements; preserve real advertiser independence. Missing age cannot invalidate an already-satisfied advertiser branch.
- Marketplace: accepted relevant Bestsellers/Movers presence OR relevant matches on Amazon and Flipkart with at least one first-ten returned position. Search order is not sales rank. Current list implementation has Positive/Insufficient, not a complete product-search Not-positive branch.
- Trends: comparable India 12-month timeline, exclude incomplete periods, at least 4 nonzero points; compare last 4 with previous 4. Rising ratio>=1.15 and increase>=5 points. Zero previous average requires at least 2 recent nonzero periods plus supported same-product Rising query; sparse/missing/all-zero stays insufficient.
- Group outcome records collection completion, target relevance, usable information and practical support separately: reasonably positive, steady/mixed, discouraging, insufficient, unavailable or stale, with method, actual measurements and references. An unmet rising threshold is not negative demand. A completed no-match search is completed research but not corroboration; a challenge/parser failure is not an empty result.
- Current `opportunity-priority-v2` counts distinct usable related sites: Meta, Amazon, Flipkart, Google Trends, Blinkit, Zepto and Instamart. All Amazon list/search routes count once. A reviewed related QC listing may give neutral site support, not sales proof or automatic positive support. Supplier sites are not market votes. Historical three-group policy keeps its original meaning.

**Current agreed selection priority, implemented versioned helper; actual whole-pool acceptance remains:**

| Priority | Fit and usable relevant demand evidence | Treatment |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | At least six of ten Matches and at least two usable related market sites. | Good fit with broader confirmation; no mandatory positive-growth gate. |
| 2 | Five Matches and at least two usable related market sites. | Decent fit with broader confirmation; no mandatory positive-growth gate. |
| 3 | At least six Matches and one usable site giving a reasonably positive picture. | Good fit with narrower support; disclose one-source coverage. |
| 4 | Five Matches and one usable site giving a reasonably positive picture. | Decent fit with narrower support; disclose one-source coverage. |

The pure priority helper derives a tier from code-counted fit, separately applicable site records and the declared policy version. It must not trust an AI total or count two Amazon routes twice. Within a tier, use meaningful support, current score, freshness, useful category balance and stable ID; required New share/holds still take precedence. Actual adverse evidence remains visible and can justify a product-specific suitability review, not automatic failure merely because an old growth threshold was missed.

“Reasonably positive” is a bounded practical source interpretation, not a demand for exact sales or the old three-advertiser/five-ad/15%-growth conditions. For one-source cases, use a saved named E source-support judgment when the actual numbers/context do not settle the meaning; return supportive/steady-mixed/discouraging/unclear with a short supplied-evidence reason. Code validates scope, refs and version, then chooses the priority. Steady interest can be useful support where the actual chart and product context justify that judgment. Missing/zero/unrelated data cannot be made positive. Existing numerical calculations remain code-owned and displayed accurately.

Visible report metrics include actual relevant ads/advertisers, active age where known, marketplace list/position and matched listings, Trends comparable averages/change/nonzero points and date range, plus actual QC platform/location/pack availability context. No invented sales, ROAS, revenue, absolute Trends volume or profit.

### 20.10 Operator audit and exceptions

Extend existing candidate summary JSON/HTML: all ten active current-policy rows with criterion state, observed/inferred basis, short reason/refs; every actual source question/attempt and result; primary and secondary blockers; known/unpublished/New distinction; supplier search status; facts needing review; currently applicable versus older decisions.

For the 86 below seven under the legacy policy, preserve score bins and criterion-level reasons, not a new strict rejection rule. Across those assessments 82 negative rows have `basis=not_stated`: plan a named review of meaning, not automatic extra points. Current 30 six-point ideas include 27 assessments using current practical checklist guidance and 3 using older guidance; this is not a description-freshness split. The new ten-criterion migration considers both current/older legacy guidance: reuse applicable supported unaffected rows, review changed audience/time meanings and unsupported negatives, and preserve original results. The three older-guidance cases remain useful diagnostics, not the only candidates for the new policy or a reason for blanket recollection; section 21.16 specifies the finite projection/review.

Separate diagnostics about access, accounting, semantics, freshness and publication. Unknown source data does not become zero. Preserve every captured bounded encounter and safe problem note; use runtime data for detailed drilldown rather than a new narrative document.

### 20.11 Useful missing-market dispatcher

The older `plan_research.market_checks` remains diagnostic; `execute_plan` executes only its supported collection actions, and `validation_targets` selects a narrow marketplace-Positive/Meta-Insufficient subset. The installed `source_coverage` controller and joined `programme` now advance the full scoped working-product/source queue through actual handlers, saved reconciliation and E/F interpretation. Continue their unfinished current tasks rather than describe the diagnostic queue as the executing driver. The old32-product gap is historical, not an admission cap. One-source eligible products still receive other applicable checks; eligibility does not mean research is finished.

Use the existing deterministic question IDs from concept/current description, product-type profile, route, missing dependency and relevant source revisions. Record examined/selected/deferred/skipped/attempted/captured/interpreted/resolved counts. Prefer compatible saved data, then the next useful supported source. If Meta is already usable, do not automatically repeat it. Audit every agreed route once where applicable. Count all Amazon list/search routes as one site; suppliers never add market votes. Under section24's seven-site rule, a reviewed relevant Blinkit, Zepto or Instamart result can provide distinct-site neutral support, but stock or price alone is not positive demand or sales proof.

Use the shared product-type profile in section22 before collection, not only after a full-title query fails. Select a core type and meaningful mechanism/use-case variant; remove irrelevant seller/colour/pack marketing words while retaining functional distinctions. Relevant broader-family evidence stays labelled as context. A grounded free alternative may run in another finite pass even after usable results; it is not a new identity, a paid allowance or an access workaround. Exact pointless repeats remain unnecessary. Section26 owns the separation of validation completion from ongoing exploration.

Saved captured work receives A/B/E scope review normally. An exact query about a target does not prove returned items belong to it. Target-before/after progress can be useful validation without new discovery. Failed/unrelated/partial/pending results remain distinct.

### 20.12 Capacity and finite recurring rehearsal

Measure complete screening and fresh supply across actual batches. One eleven-item draft is 36.7% of a minimum 30-item week; no more than one ten-item issue is presently demonstrated by that pool. Existing unpublished inventory is carryover, not an unlimited supply.

A repeated later trial tests renewal/repeat handling and new aliases; same-day identical capture tests extraction/replay, not fresh market discovery. Keep action counts/runtime/review effort/spend/repeats/ready inventory and full-calendar forecast. No required guarantee of 5% yield or six-month inventory before useful work; record uncertainty honestly and discuss material mismatch.

### 20.13 Verification matrix for discovery

| Test | Expected behaviour |
|---|---|
| Same immutable source item/remap/restart | One observation and stable child/concept/history identity; no extra collection/AI. |
| New acquisition identical content | Real new observation date; identity reuse after scope checks, no fabricated New. |
| Same function different seller/name/colour/category | Useful retrieval and same concept when supported; cross-category aliases prevent novelty reset. |
| Meaningful function/mechanism/age difference | Supported distinct concept; missing details alone do not prove difference. |
| Weak/crowded shortlist/stock image reuse | One bounded widening/optional D; retained ambiguity, not automatic duplicate/New. |
| New relevant concept after cached no-match | Recheck affected B/novelty before allocation; no whole-history AI pass. |
| Duplicate-heavy query | Feedback changes priority/refinement, no page 50 default or identical paid loop. |
| Two concept questions share a query | One physical acquisition; two separate relevance/outcome decisions. |
| Global route failed/no product attempt | Display route issue and Not tried for that product, not a false failed/negative check. |
| Wrong Trends term/hash intact | Explicit unrelated binding, no qualifying points; correct target review preserves original time. |
| Issue fills early | Freeze issue; continue remaining finite run allowance on useful backlog. |
| Six-month-oriented catalogue has fewer useful options than workload | Report actual matrix/runway and renew/extend useful areas; no fake guarantee or padding. |

### 20.14 Complexity limits

Use direct functions, existing event stores and rebuildable local indexes. No new paid DB/vector database, message broker, agent swarm, second checker model, generic dependency engine or custom Cloudflare monitor merely for one client. Model roles are schema/prompt jobs, not autonomous agents. Measure real effort before claiming maintenance savings.

### 20.15 C0 — source-grounded six-month research catalogue

Audit/extend the actual category -> subarea -> family -> phrase map. For each family keep primary/related categories, function/problem/mechanism, observed/plausible status, evidence example(s), phrase/purpose/source roles, overlap explanation and useful revisit conditions. Existing 103 observed and 121 plausible families stay distinguishable.

**Proposed seed-material construction seam:** add `build_catalogue_branch` within the existing catalogue module boundary, separate from `run_expansion(concept_ids)`. Select one thin/underrepresented branch from the current matrix; inspect real public category/list/manufacturer pages; save bounded source URLs/dates/capture hashes and example excerpts as preparation material with stable material IDs, not invented production concepts. Prepare one compact branch batch, initially up to ten short examples plus nearby existing families, within ordinary payload/time limits and the existing ten-item Jobs contract. Use the versioned C seed mode below; validate its supplied references, save accepted/deferred/none decisions once, admit meaningful additions additively, then export the updated matrix and consumption/yield scenarios. Neither recommendation-fit scoring nor paid production attempts are prerequisites for this upfront organisation. Repeat finite branch batches across all four broad categories until useful researched breadth is established; no arbitrary numeric certificate or tiny starter-list substitution.

Build broad upfront inventory through source-grounded category/list exploration, supplier catalogues and useful adjacent needs. A plausible extension may be investigated without proving existence first; classify it as plausible and do not invent a source or product. Prefer real functions, not repeated marketing adjectives/COD variants.

C inputs are compact gaps/outcomes/current family names/features and supported discoveries, not all historical reports. Code rejects invalid refs/categories and duplicate catalogue identities. Useful synonyms can be retained as retrieval alternatives under the same identity; they are not new families or extra funded requests. For differently worded proposals, C judges functional overlap against a bounded nearby-family list; code preserves that family's history. Code alone cannot reliably determine semantic sameness. Admit meaningful new functions with reasons and additive versions. Record accepted/deferred/rejected and why. Catalogue changes alone neither launch paid work nor qualify products; section26 owns free exploration and cost-aware request reuse.

### 20.16 Explicit AI decision ownership and prompt intent

A: “Describe supplied items, their use and distinguishing mechanism; separate facts from inference and name a useful missing question. Do not invent specifications or links.”

B: “Compare only the supplied possible matches. Ignore seller/colour/ordinary pack wording. Explain functional sameness/difference or uncertainty; cite supplied IDs.”

C: “Suggest up to five meaningfully different research questions from these gaps and discoveries. Explain overlap and observed versus plausible basis. Return none if no useful addition.”

D: “Compare only visible labelled real photographs; cite both views. Missing views/specifications remain unknown.”

E scope mode: “Does the actual captured query/listing concern this product or a useful functional family? Choose exact/relevant_family/unrelated/unclear and explain.” E checklist mode: “Judge the ten supplied original active criteria from product context and practical explained benefits. Retired criteria earn no points. Judge likely Indian-consumer positioning and time-saving mechanism loosely and label inference. Missing basis is Unclear, not a negative; do not invent demographic shares, measured time or totals.”

F: “Describe what this actual supplier page states, separately for product match, buying quantity, customer dispatch, packaging, fees and support. Preserve conflicts and unknowns.”

G: “Write these exact facts and limitations in concise English or natural Roman Hinglish. Do not create performance or sourcing claims. References are only the allowed frozen fact IDs.”

**Explicit C migration:** current C accepts `same_family|new_family|unclear`, supplied production concept IDs and a primary Meta discovery phrase. Add a C-only versioned seed/renewal input mode for stable material IDs or coherent concept descriptions, nearby families and source-purpose context.
