Output keeps family admission (`same_family|new_family|unclear|no_useful_addition`) separate from proposed action purpose (`discovery|validation|revisit`); a useful direct list need not be forced into a fabricated Meta phrase. Validate empty/no-addition as a complete reusable result, not a schema failure that triggers repair or another proposal loop. Keep legacy C jobs under their original schema/version; never reinterpret them. Update prompts, validation, caller and cache keys together, not just the trigger in `run_expansion`.

Structured templates/versions and code validation, not these informal examples, drive execution. Reuse existing contracts where inputs truly match; changing one role does not invalidate A/B/E/F/G globally.

### 20.17 How discoveries expand the ecosystem

An unfamiliar in-scope coherent product from A can enter a bounded C proposal before recommendation fit/fullmarket/supplier assessment. P2's automatic admission checks canonical active identity, in-scope coherent description and effective brand; it does not require any fit score. Named C-v1 inputs, five-item batches and exact completed-result reuse remain unchanged. P1a's separate seed/renewal contract is still planned. Worth exploring is not worth recommending.

Layer 1 code selects compact accepted descriptions with new mechanism/use cases or actual coverage gaps, excluding duplicates/out-of-scope/mainstream exclusions and exhausted proposal keys. Layer 2 C proposes meaningful related questions and reasons, up to5. Layer 3 code validates categories/refs, compares catalogue overlap, saves observed/plausible entries and prioritises eligible requests within remaining run scope.

A new travel packing mechanism may suggest related compact packing tools and one targeted Meta question. It does not trigger ten automatic recursive searches. Feed back actual low-usefulness/repeats into later finite planning; no C -> search -> C unbounded loop.

### 20.18 Cost and identity acceptance

Cheap exact lookup/retrieval precedes B; D is selective, not all-pairs picture comparison. Track how many records skipped/reused, B shortlist sizes, widened/unclear/D cases, calls/time/usage and sampled identity corrections. Local indexed lookups do not require rereading every historic report; external repeat collection can still cost money.

Preserve original-B comparison scope in the saved-photo fallback, including original candidates omitted by narrower current retrieval. Current photographs carry their actual ownership/time/hash; do not invent historical photo/association hashes. Missing photographs or an explicitly incomplete comparison stay Unclear rather than prove difference.

Current real D cases show the helper works in controlled scope, not accepted automatic general visual association. A missing-photo Different response was rejected and changed no association. Preserve that result rather than saying all matching is proven.

No general per-product AI dollar estimate is defensible from the subscription. Measured job workload plus actual route capacity guides batching and scale. Do not guarantee perfectly deterministic semantic recognition: deterministic rules are reproducible, while B/D judgments remain recorded best-effort meaning assessments.

## 21. Action-by-action implementation contract

### 21.1 Execution decisions: code, AI or hybrid

| Action | Route | Layer 1 | Layer 2 | Layer 3 / downstream |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Pick next research question | Deterministic + occasional C | Current scope/history/capability/headroom. | Rank ready gaps/untouched/productive/revisit with age fairness. | Persist next bounded action; actual feedback before next choice. |
| Fetch/parse | Deterministic | Supported public/provider request and finite controls. | Capture actual scope/bytes/status/limits. | Schema/row validation; retain noise/failures and original refs. |
| Recognise functional product | Hybrid | Exact IDs/content reuse and useful local shortlist. | B, optionally D for useful unresolved visuals. | Validate refs/reason; stable concept or saved ambiguity. |
| Attach evidence | Hybrid | Exact capture/query/current-description binding. | E scope judgment where meaning is uncertain. | Same applicable scope used by every downstream consumer. |
| Describe product/service and judge brand | Hybrid | Original listing/ad context and accepted description; exact-source reuse. | A judges product/service and generic/small versus established versus unclear. Targeted brand correction addresses this upstream binding, not E. | Code excludes only established brand-specific products; unknown name/unclear remains eligible for further work. Preserve original judgment and reviewed overlay. |
| Assess practical fit | Hybrid | Facts/ten original active IDs/current policy. | E practical judgments, especially time-saving mechanism and Indian buyer positioning. | Code-derived score/Good-Decent tier, supported negative/unknown separation and named corrections. |
| Calculate market result | Deterministic | Relevant accepted fresh observations. | Real counts/period means/threshold OR branches. | Family state/metrics/label; no model arithmetic totals. |
| Interpret supplier terms | Hybrid | Actual relevant product/company pages and raw terms. | F ambiguous phrasing/relationship/conflicts. | Typed fields/ranking/qualified quotes; no invented dispatch or fees. |
| Normalise an encountered price | Deterministic; F/A only ambiguity | Raw quote/currency/unit/pack/MOQ/source. | Decimal/range/known quantity parsing with exact refs. | Quote or supported comparable amount; unknown conditions visible. |
| Select/update/allocate | Hybrid where change meaning needed | Eligibility/history/queued holds. | E only a material-change question if needed. | Code N/floor(N/5), category balance, reservations and frozen issue. |
| Write/translate | Hybrid | Frozen canonical figures/rows/limitations. | G concise language/phrases. | Code exact numeric panels, refs/language/content validation and render. |
| Publish/recover/meter | Deterministic | Exact approval/manifest/server state/origin account. | Safe conditional writes/reservations/reconciliation. | Immutable receipt/history; human only at actual authority boundary. |

No LLM decides budget, switches account, calls a provider, approves release or enforces history from memory.

### 21.2 Exact code seams and transition design

Keep existing function paths. New names in this table are proposed interfaces, not capabilities already present.

| Existing seam | Planned change | Affected consumers / migration |
|---|---|---|
| `research_planner.plan_searches` / `research_batch.execute_plan` | Typed family/list/concept actions, action-by-action feedback and finite `advance_work`. | Collection/controller, replay, task/feedback history, run status; old plans read by schema adapter. |
| `research_operations` question views/imports | Add scoped product-attempt/outcome and applicability helper shared by summary/selection. | Trends, marketplace, missing-source tasks, source refs and report fingerprints. Backfill only proven attempts. |
| `research_ai` / `research_flow` | E relevance-only/checklist-only modes and named accepted-assessment policy review; additive identity component digests. | Current assessments/cache/reasons; preserve old accepted/rejected results and scoped versions. |
| `catalogue_expansion.run_expansion` / proposed branch helper | Admit coherent ideas before recommendation fit; versioned C seed mode accepts inspected material without fake concept IDs. | Proposal keys/nearby-family meaning review/catalogue options/matrix; no automatic paid work. |
| `research_sources.amazon_scope` / `research_batch._verify_preparation` / paid import | Separate proven direct-list scope/mapper/import/dispatch inside current continuous authority. | Normal observations/AI/feedback, no fabricated paid attempt; pinned paid mapper remains compatible. |
| `accounts` / `benchmark` / `storage` | Registry/context/router + staged lock contract and account-specific prior-review/refresh checks. | Every POST/GET/recovery, money snapshots/reservations and current-account migration. |
| Supplier discovery/review | Source-specific pilots/adapters, actual query events and scoped company-term cache. | F jobs, offer facts, supplier status/ranking and report rows. |
| `visual_identity` / public fetch transport | Bounded real-image acquisition into fixed media root, provenance and scope. | D attachment helper, `report_selection._facts`, native local render; image URL strings alone are not fetched photos. |
| `report_selection._facts` / `live_outputs` | Versioned structured numbers/photos/checklist/complete supplier view. | G bindings/freeze, PDFs/workbook/portal summaries, new revision manifests; exact-input writing reuse only. |
| `report_workflow` / allocator | Distinct issue reservation/language ordinals and useful content review. | Bilingual first-two queue, cancellation/revision and capacity display. |
| `portal_storage` / release helpers | Prove existing fixed-owner private transport and reconcile server receipts. | Novelty/local published history, conditional index/revision/availability; no new public bucket or portal features. |

### 21.3 Record-level changes, correction and replay

Add versioned fields to existing stores with explicit validation and compatibility tests. Every source-scoped judgment includes actual input dependencies, not just a global sequence number. Keep small used-record/revision lists; resolve only affected consumers after correction.

A job saves response before applying it. Application commits a stable result/event key once; crash before/after application reuses the saved result and rebuilds the index. Valid independent observations can be saved even if a dependent decision became stale. Old results never overwrite a newer binding merely because an unrelated process completed late.

**Canonical commit versus optional-index outcome — implemented P2, consumed by P5:** `ResearchMemory.commit` preserves its True/False once-only meaning. A confirmed event followed by cache-write failure returns committed with `last_commit_diagnostic`, rather than reporting a failed assessment or restarting an external execution. Only optional-index errors are absorbed; chain/previous-hash errors remain real. A lost atomic-write acknowledgment is reconciled against the exact intended event before success is reported. Restart reconstructs current state from events, never an old index. P5 still needs to expose the diagnostic through its connected controller/status when writable; do not recursively append an event merely to record its cache failure.

Checklist correction accepts upward/downward changes for named inconsistency or current-policy meaning, with original assessment/description/evidence/rubric/reason. `repair_checklist_item` currently repairs rejected contract outputs, not accepted scoring judgments; implement a distinct accepted-review path. Save/reuse the accepted **or rejected** review for unchanged dependencies. Do not retry until a desired score.

Future unsupported Does-not-meet/not_stated rows become Unclear with a retained response/diagnostic, not Meets. Existing accepted rows change only through named recorded review. Permit observed and sensible explained inferred judgments; no new exact-demographic/ship-cost/performance-proof gate for every benefit.

### 21.4 Finite post-fill work and persistence

Persist run limits once. After issue selection fills, remaining allowance can finish saved interpretation, genuinely missing independent evidence and supplier work for later issues. Existing32 legacy fit-passing market-gap concepts are a starting finite queue, not an exclusive cap: newly five-of-ten fit-passing concepts join after policy review. Do not rerun all156 or recollect adequate sources. Weak/lower-fit cases remain set aside unless new evidence or an explicit contradiction warrants review.

**Growth acceptance:** the current derived index is about 10.5 MB against a16MiB write bound. Beyond that bound optional index refresh can be omitted although authoritative events remain. Before relying on long-running views/retrieval, test realistic 30/50/80-report workloads and label index sequence/hash/complete-or-omitted status. Every reader verifies index currency against authoritative events; an old optional index is not current evidence. Prefer compact identity/history projections and checkpoint-plus-tail/incremental updates within the existing storage boundary if measured replay becomes costly. Add bounded sharding only when needed, preserving the authoritative history; do not delete records or install another database by default. Measure snapshot/commit time, memory and top 10/20 lookup size independently of AI calls.

The new driver is one small operating-system schedule over existing commands; choose the existing Windows/native or WSL environment that actually runs the browser/model reliably during implementation. Use one canonical research writer initially. A scheduled start obtains run ownership, advances finite work, emits completed/pending/blocked counts and exits. No continuously polling LLM/service or charging each client click.

**Scheduled-period transition — P5:** add a versioned period record within existing `batch_runs`, with `research_period_id`, standing schedule/policy hash, fixed envelope limits/expiry, consumed/reserved work refs and predecessor refs. The reviewed boundary is **Monday00:00 Asia/Kolkata inclusive through next Monday00:00 exclusive**; persist those UTC instants. Derive its stable ID from client plus that calendar-week start only—not invocation time, settings/policy hash, operator focus mode, provider billing period or issue ID. Dispatch records reference it; readers distinguish period versus legacy batch records by declared kind/schema. Persist one envelope before dispatch; every owned start in that period looks it up rather than granting another. A settings change may require affected-step validation but cannot create another allowance. The fixed expiry and per-action deadlines remain on restart; interruption does not erase consumed work. On a new due week, close/preserve the previous run and create at most one new scheduled envelope within actual funding. Default missed-week handling creates only the current period, not multiple accumulated catch-up allowances.

| Transition | Exact continuation rule |
|---|---|
| Duplicate start or restart before period expiry | Same envelope, original remaining counters and exact pending executions. No larger limit from invocation arguments. |
| Same-period allowance/expiry exhausted | Save unfinished/unselected references and exit. A restart makes no new external call merely because the process is new. |
| Genuinely new due period | Admit still-relevant unfinished questions, unselected actions and saved interpretation by reference; recheck current dependencies, due triggers and fairness. New work consumes the new envelope. Completed work reuses its result; uncertain old execution is recovered, never replaced. |
| Missed start or computer off across periods | Start only the current period with explicit carried backlog/shortfall; uploaded releases remain online. No automatic pile-up of old allowances or false completed cadence. |
| Explicit cancel or operator stop | Preserve its recorded scope. Cancelled tasks are not carried automatically; a service-wide stop prevents new scheduled envelopes until explicitly resumed. Normal exhaustion/interruption is not that stop. |

Current `execute_plan` freezes `selected_actions`; current `prepare_continuation` covers only blocked/deferred unreserved preparations, and `run_flow` limits are per invocation. Preserve their legacy replay. New `advance_work` must enumerate unselected and interpretation backlog explicitly, freeze period limits and charge counters against durable action/model/read intents **before** external execution, then reconcile receipts without counting one execution twice. Do not create a different job/acquisition ID to escape an unresolved original. A permitted confirmed-ended retry keeps its logical task and explicit original/retry execution linkage; a new week does not renew per-question repair limits or reinterpret unchanged accepted/rejected output. Age-based admission includes ready carryover and fresh work without rigid quotas; waiting old tasks do not consume every slot. A renewed research envelope is work capacity, never replenished trial funding or a provider credit grant.

Use kernel/file-lock ownership, not “PID is old, delete lock.” Initially keep interpretation/application serialized while the single writer runs; short money commits remain available. Later concurrency is only added for independently scoped useful tasks after proving commit/recovery, not as a default requirement.

### 21.5 Concurrency and interruptions

Lock order for charged work is exact-attempt guard -> short financial lock -> release financial lock/network -> short financial commit -> release guard. Never wait for the attempt guard while holding the money lock. Financial reservation lock is short; exact-attempt launch guard spans uncertain launch; research single-writer guard covers decision application; package revision freeze and R2 ETag cover different objects. Document their order and avoid acquiring the financial lock recursively while already held. Current long collection lock can cause five-second snapshot timeouts; phased controller change must remove that bottleneck without breaking its required contract.

A completed model response is persisted before owned-job cleanup. Deadline terminates only the owned process group and saves exit status/usage uncertainty. A retry is allowed only when that attempt demonstrably ended, no completed response exists and a finite retry allowance remains. Missing termination receipt is recovery, not permission to start another potentially running job.

Network/acquisition workers write isolated capture directories; canonical writer validates returned refs/dependencies and appends once. Unrelated work changes do not invalidate all results. Stale dependencies preserve artifacts and requeue only affected decisions. Frozen issue bytes are never mutated by later research.

### 21.6 Numerical evidence and typed report facts

**Implemented current facts:** `report_source_facts` adds `source_route_contract="saved-source-routes-v1"` and exactly14 ordered `source_route_checks` to current `opportunity-priority-v2` products. Each row carries route/display label/role, current description hash, truthful outcome and plain display state, actual-attempt Boolean, explanation, measurements, task/receipt/evidence references, original observation date/public URL and a separate last-attempt receipt. Work completion is not a demand vote:14 routes still represent at most seven consumer-market sites; suppliers never add a market vote.

Fifteen supported metric names cover relevant ad/advertiser/active/unknown counts, relevant listing examples and list-position bounds, displayed-price counts, location-specific availability/unknown counts, chart intervals/nonzero intervals and previous/recent four-period interest bounds. Code reads the actual relevant saved sample and names its units; these are not sales, market size or distinct-product counts. Unknown is null, not zero. The same quick-commerce listing checked in different actual locations remains separate; repeated snapshots at one location reuse the latest applicable one. Original location/pack/quote/date context stays with the evidence. An independently normalised Trends index is not absolute demand or directly comparable to another query's index.

Each metric uses exactly `name`, `value`, `evidence_ids`; references must be nonempty and belong to that row's verified frozen references. Integers remain integers; computed fractional figures are losslessly represented as normalized ordinary decimal strings, for example `"20.25"`, never rounded to satisfy website hashing. Values are null or bounded nonnegative integers/decimal strings; ranges have exactly two ordered non-null endpoints. Strings have at most32 characters and no exponent/leading zero/trailing fractional zero; bounds are0..1,000,000,000. The website's integer-only canonical JSON rule is unchanged. Each product's route block is capped at64KiB, inside the existing4MiB facts limit. A synthetic ten-product/140-row packet used160,585 bytes; this is a size check, not real market evidence.

`route_dependencies` binds only that concept's actual description/task/receipt/observation dependencies, including applicable shared receipts and pauses. An unrelated target's progress does not force a revision. Report-time coverage excludes future receipts/reviews and process-local staged progress; a dispatch already started at the cutoff can truthfully remain pending. Same-meaning fresh observations reuse accepted current semantic review; changed meaning remains pending and retired review versions do not establish current relevance. Historical attempted work can be shown separately from whether old evidence is fresh enough for a current recommendation.

PDFs and Excel's Evidence sheet render these same rows/figures directly, without enlarging G's prompt or asking it to invent numbers. Exact current source and supplier hyperlink destinations must match frozen URLs, not merely be safe public links. New marked packets pin `deterministic-workbook-v2` and `functional-report-stages-v2`; historical unmarked rendering/version behavior stays intact. Future extra derived figures such as ad-age ranges or chart delta/ratio require actual calculator inputs and explicit tests; absent measurements remain absent. A zero baseline never supplies an invented ratio, and `<1`/uncertain chart values retain justified interval bounds.

New frozen current-platform/source-route facts also pin `supplier_terms_display_version="supplier-terms-v2"`. The existing supplier section and workbook notes use one shared deterministic projection of the seventeen saved purchase, dispatch, minimum-order, subscription, fee, India-coverage, packaging, branding and online-selling fields. Observed amounts/compound minima stay verbatim; optional/conflicting subscriptions do not become “not stated”; company-wide terms do not confirm this particular product's supply. Unsupported fields are not invented or padded across every lead. An absent marker retains the original two-field projection byte-for-byte, including partial-stage replay; a newly rebuilt dataset cannot silently upgrade an old frozen packet. Only a proper new revision changes the display. The website validates the optional marker's exact current-contract scope. This uses saved facts, not an additional model or supplier request.

All ten active checklist rows retain original IDs, full internal reason/ref/basis and a short client phrase derived from those same fields. Legacy thirteen-row records remain separately labelled under their original contract. Preserve multiple blockers/current applicability. Code derives score and market label once; PDFs/workbook consume it unchanged. **Implemented P2:** `writing_facts` retains up to the first six supported benefits for the short why-product text. Separately, `report_narration.make_narrative_input` supplies all ten active original criterion IDs/states/bases/full reasons/refs through the complete `criteria` array and its bound criterion facts. `research-g-v3` returns `checklist_phrases` with exactly those ten unique IDs per required language. G shortens/translates wording only; code retains state/score and checks coverage/references/qualifications. The six-benefit prose limit does not hide any checklist row. Legacy writing keeps its original declared contract. No mechanical paragraph cutting or ten separate calls. Accepted old narration is reused only when its own exact writing dependencies remain identical; new fit meaning is not a presentation-only change.

### 21.7 Supplier discovery, capability and subscriptions

Workflow: (1) choose 1–2 useful grounded product/sourcing queries, (2) capture bounded public search results, (3) fetch relevant Indian product/company pages and readily accessible pricing/FAQ/reseller/dropshipping terms, (4) F interprets only supplied sources, (5) code ranks/stores full lead facts and search outcomes. A sparse result, blocked search, challenge/layout failure or unavailable nominated pages can justify one bounded demonstrated alternative route. Bind it to the original problem and same run allowance, not every portal on every product. Replay of a saved failed search preserves its truth; it is not a successful retry or permission to silently replace the request.

Aim 1–3 useful leads, up to5 when easily available. New supplier search plans freeze a validated limit and schema version in the saved request. Current `_derive()` reads global `MAX_LEADS`, so simply changing 3 to5 would alter old replay: legacy plans must retain their original three-lead cap. New facts take the complete accepted `all_leads` set with an independent presentation preference, not only the capped `leads` view. Update F source/result bindings, selection facts and delivery checks together; test old three-lead replay and fourth/fifth accepted lead reaching new workbook rows without changing prior packets. Zero usable leads after a real bounded supplier search is acceptable and visible, including when actually attempted routes/pages failed. A prepared search or manual URL alone is not actual research. Preserve completed-empty, blocked/failed and interpretation-pending distinctions internally; never relabel a failed search as successful or imply no supplier exists. Known-page capture, discovery and interpretation remain distinct.

**Agreed best-effort selection contract:** derive whether supplier effort was actually attempted from bound search/page-dispatch receipts for the current product/description, real dates and the declared finite search/fallback pass. Reuse applicable saved completed effort; no extra search is needed merely to create this limitation. After that pass, preserve useful accepted leads or use the fixed client sentence **“Supplier search did not produce any usable results.”** when none is usable. Failed routes or pending follow-up do not block an otherwise eligible product after actual effort; keep that follow-up in the internal queue. Prepared-only/prelaunch-invalid/unrelated requests do not satisfy the actual-effort requirement. No verified stock, confirmed dropshipping supplier or fee certificate is required.

**Implemented P2:** the small shared `supplier_effort` projection reads existing source checks/discovery/page/job receipts. It returns actual-attempt refs, last attempt time, internal outcome, `effort_satisfied` and useful-lead count; model text cannot declare this boolean. **A URL/date or unavailable result alone is not dispatch proof:** `public_pages.fetch_public_page` can return `PUBLIC_FETCH_WORKERS_BUSY` before execution. Current versioned reader receipts preserve `execution_stage=not_dispatched|attempt_started|response_received`, `attempt_started_at` and an owned execution/receipt ref through discovery/page failure wrappers. Execution is marked after valid preparation and resource/capability admission, before DNS/network/navigation work. A genuine bounded DNS/network/browser attempt may fail and still count; invalid URL, busy worker or missing executable before an attempt does not. A thrown wrapper exception preserves stage evidence if known, otherwise records unknown dispatch. Legacy receipts stay unchanged: real effort derives from available HTTP/navigation/execution proof, not requested URL or `checked_at` alone; unresolved legacy evidence queues only that effort question.

Project verified effort into a typed versioned **existing `source_checks` record**, not selection-time folder scanning. Bind concept/description/sourcing scope, declared pass ID, actual receipt IDs/hashes/stages/dates, internal outcome, accepted lead refs and code-derived effort flag. Persist after verifying receipts; exact replay adds no event, and an interrupted projection is resumed from saved receipts without re-search. One bounded pass with real effort can close with no usable lead even while useful follow-up remains queued. Verify relevance for each target; a shared query/receipt can support several products only at its actual scope and physical cost is counted once. Do not let a receipt for a different product or an unexecuted sibling satisfy the flag.

**Implemented P2 consumer path:** `report_selection._suppliers` and the `supplier_search_required` gate in `build_report_pool` consume the code-derived `effort_satisfied` value, including genuine failed attempts and known-page effort with no F result. `_facts`, `_source_revisions`, `selection_scope`, reservation previous-dependency checks, report-workflow/package, `live_outputs`, `report_narration` and local/website-code checks bind that same projected record/hash. The usable/complete/zero no-lead rule is retained only as the applicable legacy compatibility path, not the current actual-attempt requirement. Internal status may retain Attempted versus Complete; client display uses the fixed clear sentence, not raw diagnostic enums. New facts bind actual receipt refs/outcome rather than force Failed to usable. Legacy packets remain readable and unchanged. P1b/P6 use the same receipt contract in new readers; P3 improves the panel; P5/P6 improve routes/follow-ups. Tests cover completed empty, real blocked attempts versus prelaunch busy with the same unavailable state, unreadable nominated pages, interpretation pending after real effort, useful surviving lead, prepared-only/wrong-product/stale receipt, known-page effort without F, interrupted projection, changed reservation dependency and exact zero-call replay. Both English and natural Roman-Hinglish preserve this meaning; the current phrase is “Supplier search se koi kaam ka result nahi mila.” G cannot turn this limitation into confirmed absence of supply.

Prioritise direct manufacturers/wholesalers/importers and public IndiaMART/TradeIndia/Maps/Google routes after their pilots. Maps headless first, headful if legitimate access needs it; no challenge bypass. Business presence alone does not prove exact product or dropshipping. No contact/quote requests/purchases.

Record company identity/location/type; actual product/terms URLs/date/refs; exact/close/family match; product MOQ/stock prepayment; single-piece purchase; direct customer dispatch; bulk-stock-then-individual-dispatch; bulk-only/unknown; COD; integration; neutral packaging; private label. “MOQ 1” proves only buying quantity; “e-commerce support” proves none of the individual services.

Subscription state: stated no mandatory subscription / mandatory recurring subscription / genuinely optional plan / Not stated. Preserve monthly/annual frequency/fee; distinguish setup/deposit/per-order costs and buyer versus seller platform membership. Free signup or no pricing page is not proof of free fulfilment.

Rank relevant fulfilment fit before stated no-subscription, then clear terms/freshness/stable lead ID; irrelevant “free” never outranks a fitting lead. Required-fee leads can remain lower-priority alternatives, unknowns remain useful when relevant. Share company-wide terms for up to30 days only when same company/workflow and unchanged evidence support it; product offer/price/MOQ/stock/private-label conditions do not inherit without stated scope.

F-v3 already preserves contradictory construction statements; keep conflicts rather than choose material/finish silently. Retain all accepted leads in workbook; PDF shows useful complete ranked set within its purposeful 2–3 pages, with explicit workbook continuation if necessary, not a silent two-lead slice.

### 21.8 Price and photograph acquisition

Prices naturally encountered belong to ordinary research. Store raw quote/from/range, supported Decimal amount/currency, retail/wholesale/unknown kind, unit/pack/quantity/MOQ, shipping/tax/eligibility, original date and source. Only derive per-unit value when quantity/basis is explicitly comparable; preserve the raw quote and derivation. Do not turn retail minus supplier price into margin or label unknown comparable amount as “no price found.”

Current price-display-v2 and exact legacy formatter selection remain. Explicit numericMOQ takes precedence; otherwise show one unambiguous observed compound minimum term without inventing company-wide quantity. Conflicting/missing/inferred fields stay unknown. Add regression tests for ranges, GST/from/pack prices, decimals, unknown unit and compound sets/rolls.

Photos: select actual saved source/product media for market-ready report products, not generate them. The installed image-byte public reader checks source/redirect safety, capped content, accepted MIME and decoding; it is separate from the HTML reader. Current limits are5MiB/25 million decoded pixels; corrupt/oversized/private/credential URLs are rejected without blocking unrelated work.

Save original-byte hash, source URL/item/acquisition/date, target meaning/scope and reviewed primary image. Fetch date is not source observation date. Store under `media/live/product-images/`; copy only checked immutable local bytes into the report/job context. Crop/resize only for faithful display with original preserved. No wrong carousel image, unlabelled family exemplar or ambiguous identity photo becomes exact product proof. Missing image is visible but not an exclusion.

Actual photos/metrics added to frozen facts create a new factual revision. Review meaning/provenance and actual rendering, not decoding only. D optional ambiguity review remains separate from report photograph selection.

Current marked facts require `image_path` to be null unless `current_public_image` supplies verified provenance at or before the report cutoff. That provenance binds the saved photo receipt, concept owner, actual observation in both `source_revisions.observations` and `source_refs`, original public URL/date, byte hash, checked local path and exact-product/relevant-family relationship. Python verifies the original bytes; the website verifies the frozen structural binding and rejects wrong-table references, another product or malformed scalar IDs/hashes. No future photo can enter an earlier frozen packet. Historical unmarked packets keep their own behavior.

### 21.9 Report layout and professional content

This section retains the approved visual reference for a separate redesign. The current nonvisual programme implements the functional fields, genuine photos, usable text/links and cross-format correctness below without requiring new typography, page styling or UI/UX approval. Existing content must remain legible and unclipped, but visual redesign is not a finish gate.

Use approved Signal Ledger colours/logo/type mood, not manufactured numbers/images or the preview's fixed 20-page map. The live report has its own useful page count.

**Two purposeful product pages by default:**

| Page | Content | Compact evidence examples |
|---|---|---|
| Product decision page | Product heading/New-update/category, one genuine hero photo when available, use-case/value hook, visible retail/supplier quotes with basis, core-source numbers/status/date and brief practical reason to investigate. | “6 ads · 4 advertisers · oldest 21d”; “India 12m · recent 18.5 / prior 13 · +5.5”; “Bestsellers #12 in captured list”; unavailable explicitly, not 0. |
| Fit and sourcing page | All ten active criteria with original IDs, Good/Decent fit and Meets/Unclear/etc and short grounded phrases, supplier leads/capabilities/MOQ/subscription/quotes, meaningful limitations and grouped clickable sources. | “Easy to ship — fold-flat”; “MOQ 100 pieces — bulk purchase”; “Customer dispatch — Not stated”; no full-paragraph checklist essay. |
| Optional third page | Genuine extra supplier/evidence detail, clear conditions or additional useful comparisons. | Not filler, decorative blank space, repeated methodology or source-only overflow. |

Cream/background must cover the **physical page edge to edge**, while text/image panels keep roughly 16–18mm useful insets on every page. This is not achieved by removing text margins or setting a single flowing first-fragment padding. Pilot explicit page wrappers for purposeful pages with separate ground and inner-content boxes; allow genuine long material its next purposeful page, avoiding fixed-height clipping. Chromium actual geometry/visual tests decide the viable paged approach.

Readable body approximately 10.5–11pt, restrained hierarchy and comfortable leading; no shrinking facts to cram dense columns. Photo size adapts to available page space and crop; when absent use a concise note/full-width content, not a large empty hero. Use compact phrases/numerical panels, short explanatory text only where helpful. Status is text plus colour, never colour alone. All links are actual descriptive clickable sources, grouped to reduce repeated URLs while preserving complete references in workbook.

Workbook/render contracts track frozen facts and manifests. **Implemented P2:** `delivery_package._workbook()` requires exactly four named sheets for current fit10, including Checklist with its bound IDs/rows; genuine legacy packets require their original three named sheets. P3 versions the additional metric/supplier columns and render contract when those facts are connected. Unknown/mixed contracts reject; do not accept either layout indiscriminately or invalidate earlier presentation-source packets.

The current workbook retains Shortlist/Evidence/Supplier leads and **includes Checklist** with all ten active rows/original IDs/reasons/basis/refs and code-derived fit count/tier. New marked packets enrich Evidence with all14 actual route outcomes and supported metrics, and show every accepted supplier in both the workbook and PDFs with arrangement/fee/quote details. Exact row values and actual hyperlink destinations are checked against frozen facts. Stable IDs, frozen/filter headers, useful widths/row heights, real dates/Decimals and formula escaping remain. Add no extra internal money sheets to client files.

Portal stays concise latest/archive/download summary. Two–three pages means **PDF pages per product**, not a new multi-page website feature. No internal job/account diagnostics in client output.

### 21.10 Writing, bilingual trial and independent quality review

G generates only required language slots from exact frozen facts; code builds price, numeric, checklist and supplier panels. Phrase concision does not erase unknowns/conditions. All number/date/reference/name/decision meanings agree across en, hi-Latn and workbook. Roman Hinglish is conversational Latin-script wording, not Devanagari or a mechanical word-for-word translation.

The first complete eligible/approved issue can proceed independently; it does not wait for enough inventory or photographs for the second. First-two ordinals derive distinct published issue IDs plus projected ordered upcoming queue, not revision count/files. Eleven ready products do not produce two distinct ten-item issues; reserve separately. Fill due 10-minimums before extras. Cancellation/reorder can change which issues need bilingual output; recheck affected projected ordinals without new research.

Before issue 3 choice, retain its facts and independently generated workbook as language_pending. The implemented `prepare_review_package()` calls the checked-core/shared-workbook stage before this return, without G or a renderer; the original issue/revision and chosen-language rule remain unchanged. No complete delivery manifest is created from that partial output. Do not block the first two, default English, buy indefinite bilingual service or ask the client to decide before seeing their two reports.

Inspect actual workbook cells and rendered functional text/images/links for both languages: correct product photo, all figures/criteria/suppliers, selectable content and no hidden/cropped conditions. Code validation catches structural drift; actual review catches misleading meaning. Full physical-inset/page-rhythm/design acceptance belongs to the deferred visual redesign, not the current nonvisual finish.

An old internal packet acceptance is not client marketing-quality approval. The owner has rejected current report quality; a new pilot must address it rather than cite old geometry checks as proof.

### 21.11 Costs, roadblocks and smooth continuation

Each source stage uses finite per-pass action limits; shared funds include charged tests/retries/reads. Automatic corrections do not recollect by default. Exact query reuse and saved-model-job repair remain. A charged repeat still costs externally and local deduplication is not a refund; a permitted direct local repeat may have no incremental collection fee. Free collection does not imply free additional AI interpretation. Local runtime is not the owner's cash constraint; section26 separates those decisions.

A route outage pauses that route/question; model quota pauses relevant model work; owner login pauses transport; missing client email pauses client activation; language choice pauses issue 3 language. Other independent saved research/planning/rendering continues.

The action matrix, failure classifications, retry limits, paired-failure tests and next-run triggers are in section 21.17. The fallback is an implemented permitted route, reusable valid work, or a different ready task—not a promise that an unavailable fact can always be obtained. A successful disconnected test is not automatically an application fallback.

Do not build an unlimited recovery/guardrail maze. Material permission/authority/charge uncertainty has a real boundary; minor cents, optional image/fee/lead unknowns and subjective benefits do not. Report exact reason and next action rather than repeatedly asking about the same unchanged condition.

Mistaken exclusion is reviewed at the child/description boundary before B/E, including children with no concept. No name blacklist, global A rerun or rewritten publication history. |

| Multi-account money | Existing migration, keyrefreshnoreset, oldperiodhold, asynchronousrenewals, funded limit, unknownread, aggregatecommitments, accountscopedpriorreview, exact original GET/POST, no autochargeupgrade. |
| Locks/crashes | Guard-before-money ordering, short commit, uncertainPOSTlostresponse, original-account credential recovery, midcapture/resultcommit, model timeout/noexitreceipt, singlewriter/deadline/overlap, saved response before application, no blind retry. |

| Reports | Same facts across en/Hinglish/Excel, all ten rows, actual metrics/price basis/full suppliers, no hidden/cropped facts and working text/images/links/downloads. Full ground/insets/design review are deferred visual acceptance. |

| Operations | Scheduledboundedresume, exhaustedlimitsnotnewallowance, grouped failures/reopentriggers, zero-fee effortcounts, actual 12–14 calendar forecast, backup/restore and notificationreceipt limits. |
| Continuation | Every action has a classified failure, affected dependency, permitted fallback or saved waiting trigger. Healthy sibling/independent lane executes after failure; paired outages, no-progress loops, canonical-write loss and exhausted allowance follow section 21.17F. |

Specific transition fixtures also cover seed-material C with no production concept ID, reusable no-addition output, synonym versus new mechanism/history preservation, direct captures without fictitious paid charges, positive broader-list scope versus two unknown None scopes, empty `no_known_match` and later recheck, original-B photo scope, planner cooldown/diagnostic-root migration, old three-lead supplier replay, fourth/fifth leads, old three-sheet packet validation, ten active original phrase IDs including unknown/negative rows plus preserved legacy thirteen-row replay, `<1` Trends bounds, capture-time ad age, exact 09:00 freshness and language-pending workbook-only generation.

Tests must also assert noninterference: standalone experiments do not alter production research/history/financial/catalogue/report data; scoped fixes leave unrelated accepted roles/products unchanged. Do not mark future tests passed or substitute prior fixture totals for these new cases.

### 21.13 Integration, corrections and continuity

#### A. Existing transitions

Update schema validators, all consumers and fixtures together. Typed request scopes need new real list/concept alternatives, not fabricated family IDs. Account registry needs origin-aware controller/previous-review/reservation logic. Photo transport needs an actual image reader. Structured report facts need new revisions and correct exact-input G reuse. Shared helpers are small direct functions, not a generic framework.

#### B. Durable results and corrections

Persist intent→response→validated result→applied event in existing stores, with stable logical keys and immutable execution artifacts. Unknown usage can coexist with accepted content. Local derived state rebuilds from events. Corrections add superseding current judgments; original sources/jobs/frozen reports remain intact.

No manual manufactured model answers, response edits, date refreshes, historical score inflation or blanket recollection. Recompute dependent present views after a named correction; review affected held/current packages normally.

#### C. Backlog without starvation

The current issue selection boundary and finite run boundary are separate. Save what was examined, why unselected work remains and its next trigger. Continue useful work within the same allowance after a report fills. Do not endless-loop lower-fit ideas or delay already-complete independent issues for every unfinished record.

#### D. Reservation and language continuity

Reserve per stable client/issue. Draft replacement excludes only its own superseded draft holds and replaces them atomically after validation. Approved uploaded revisions retain holds until confirmed remote replacement/withdrawal. Published history remains even if withdrawn. Projected bilingual ordinal changes are rechecked before approval; no additional discovery just to choose a language.

#### E. Private upload and reconciliation

Stage exact immutable objects, read bytes back, verify manifest/facts/download hashes, then conditionally update the active release index with exact approval/server time. Current R2 conditional code exists; test it. A lost reply is reconciled by issue/revision/hash/ETag rather than sending a different revision blindly.

A private-file transport test may use fixed approved illustrative artifacts under internal-only keys, leave active index unchanged and prove owner upload/readback without publishing a report. It is transport proof, not live content/design proof. Client activation waits for the deferred email. Upload staging, owner approval and scheduled availability are distinct.

Local start reconciles remote availability/history before final allocation. Offline saved analysis can continue; final dependent approval waits if remote history is unknown. For an uncertain activation, recognise its exact already-committed remote mutation **before** the saved expected-ETag conflict branch; section 21.17E specifies that transition. A diagnostic object-upload readback does not itself prove activation recovery. Late approval explicitly shows current release consequence. Old approved issues stay available when a new issue fails. No permanent browser-DNS change or certificate bypass is required.

#### F. Review/finish discipline

For each dependency-ready slice: inspect current seams, implement its connected changes, run focused tests, inspect actual output where required, fix known defects, rerun affected regression and update these distinct-purpose docs. Independent review checks consumer wiring and authority, not merely new-function code. Milestones are internal verification checkpoints; continue to the next ready item. Regroup only after the whole nonvisual closure review or when all remaining work requires a real external/owner-only change. Do not stop merely because a smaller block is verified.

Measured details live in the benchmark record. The preserved legacy revision5 has 11 products,143 thirteen-point checklist rows,20 quote rows,14 supplier leads,0 attached product photos and two19-page language PDFs; it is not a current-policy or owner design acceptance result. Completed local P2 supplies ten-original-ID assessments, Good/Decent allocation, source-bound brand corrections, genuine supplier-effort joining and current dataset/G/workbook/manifests through local and website-validator code. Verified internal revision6 has 11 products,110 checklist rows,two24-page PDFs and one four-sheet workbook; it remains unapproved with zero photos. Two no-lead selections retain explicitly applicable legacy F proof, not newly dispatched searches. Website-validator tests are not deployment proof. The private owner status page reports reachable storage/empty release index, not published reports.

At the preserved P2 checkpoint, source route coverage was partial and the direct comparator/QC/directory/Maps adapters, account pool and persistent continuation were not built. Subsequent8 October source/account/controller implementation and proof are recorded in sections22–23 and the documentation index; this earlier checkpoint is not current evidence that those functions are missing. Real all-product source interpretation, complete recurring service, remaining current replacement packets/client activation and enough distinct recurring inventory still require their own acceptance. Private transport/download proof for the preserved reference and two distinct ten-product packets is separately recorded in the benchmark; it is not whole-pool research completion.

### 21.16 Ten-criterion fit policy and coordinated migration

The **existing local schema3 fit policy** is implemented across interpretation, selection, allocation, local outputs and hosted-validator code, while genuine historical contracts remain preserved. Current extensions add practical all-product completion and the combined priorities in section20.9; their implementation/proof must be measured separately. The completed standalone Amazon experiment requires no product-fit assessment and does not prove these changes.

#### A. One deterministic policy and count

The small pure `fit_policy.py` evaluator is shared by service validation, research views, selection, follow-ups, allocation and outputs. Its interfaces are `policy_for(policy_id)`, `evaluate_fit(criteria, policy_id)` and the explicit legacy reader. Reviewed thresholds are fixed in code; incoming files cannot choose arbitrary thresholds. It is not another workflow engine.

| Policy field | New reviewed value |
|---|---|
| Policy ID | `fit-10-v1` |
| Original active IDs, in display order | `[1,2,5,6,7,8,9,11,12,13]` |
| Retired original IDs | `[3,4,10]`, outside score/client active rows |
| Denominator / inclusion / Good threshold | 10 / 5 / 6 |
| Allowed states | Meets; Does not meet; Unclear; Not assessed |
| Bands | Good fit for6–10; Decent fit for5; below threshold for0–4, with completeness reason |

The helper first verifies the declared policy and exactly one row per active original ID, legal states/bases and supported references/reasons. It then counts M=Meets, F=supported Does-not-meet and U=Unclear/Not-assessed; M+F+U=10. It returns policy ID, active IDs, total10, counts, `good|decent|below`, inclusion boolean and completeness reason. It does not accept model/client totals. If M<5 and M+U>=5, expose `fit_information_pending`; if M+U<5, expose `below_fit_threshold`. Neither means all unknown rows failed. Five points qualify even when the other five are unknown; no extra completeness/confidence quota.

Current service schema3 retains existing schedule/size/novelty/spending/language fields, sets `minimum_kpi_matches=5`, declares `fit_policy_id=fit-10-v1` and `good_fit_kpi_matches=6`, and validates their exact agreement with the reviewed helper. Legacy config/readers retain schema1/2 and their original seven-match interpretation. Configuration and consumer code are migrated together.

New frozen datasets/products/selections declare fit-policy ID, original criterion IDs, count, total and tier, all bound by existing fact/selection hashes. Use a new dataset/render/workbook/manifest contract rather than accepting unlabelled arrays of either length. The exact legacy contract remains readable: absent marker is legacy only at the old schema boundary, not a licence to submit new data without a policy.

#### B. Practical AI judgment, not invented measurements

E uses the **mode-specific fit** prompt/schema version `research-e-fit10-v1`, distinct from shared `research-abe-v1`. This is not a role-wide E replacement: E also performs `marketplace_relevance_only` with empty criterion rows. That unchanged relevance contract keeps its existing prompt/schema version and `REVIEW_VERSION`; A/B/C/D/F are not globally invalidated. New full-fit/partial-fit producers declare mode and policy, accepted product description/features, available scoped observations, Indian consumer context, requested original criterion IDs and supplied reference IDs. The full-fit response covers exactly all ten active IDs. A named partial-review mode covers exactly its explicitly requested IDs; code combines it with the frozen supported retained rows and validates the final ten-row assessment.

The small explicit E contract selector `e_contract(payload, declared_version=None)` is used by `prompt_version`, schema/instruction selection and `validate_results`. Its supported branches are historical full checklist/repair, unchanged marketplace relevance, and current full/partial fit; unknown or contradictory mode/version/policy is an affected-item diagnostic, not an automatic legacy fallback. Historical requests without the new marker are interpreted only under their saved version. Their exact old payload/request/hash is preserved; new fields are not inserted into an old request to make it match.

`Jobs.blocked_items/run` uses the selected contract consistently for individual bindings, job request/cache, results/completions and chunked/previous-job replay. Replaying a saved job uses its declared version, not today's global E version. `CodexRunner.__call__` supplies that same schema/instructions, and `_execution_binding` validates against the saved intent rather than reconstructing a historical request with a new prompt version. These are changes to the existing paths, not a second job engine. `market_validation._review_marketplace` reads/writes relevance review IDs and applicability using the relevance-specific version; a scoring-policy change alone cannot discard fresh existing relevance. A later actual relevance-instruction change would get its own reviewed version, not silently inherit fit10.

Each returned row has original ID, state, observed/inferred/not-stated/conflicting basis, a short reason and supplied references. Reasoned benefits and likely size/weight/handling may cite the product form/features from which the inference follows; measured performance, exact packed weight or an outside testimonial is not mandatory. A fairly reliable ballpark is acceptable, labelled Estimated/Inferred and never presented as a measured specification. Do not manufacture a precise gram value or pretend estimated low handling difficulty establishes shipping cost or RTO. Code rejects invented references/IDs/duplicates and normalises an unsupported not-stated negative to Unclear with its response/diagnostic retained. A malformed missing row may be filled as Unclear with a diagnostic, never invented Meets. It then evaluates the count once. E does not launch tools, search, add points, select products or set authoritative totals.

**ID7 time-saving prompt intent:** “Identify the ordinary task. Explain whether the described mechanism plausibly reduces steps, handling or repeated effort. Return Meets with a specific inferred mechanism, a supported Does-not-meet reason, or Unclear. Do not require measured timing and do not invent minutes/percentages.” Save the short mechanism in that same row; no extra per-criterion call. Example: a draining insert reduces transfers; a decorative vase does not obviously save time. A hypothetical ten-unit comparison is explanatory only if genuinely useful and labelled; it is not a numerical fact field or used by scoring/report calculations. Default output is a phrase, not a guessed stopwatch.

**ID13 audience prompt intent:** “Judge likely Indian-consumer use/buyer positioning moderately from this product and supplied context. Label women-centric, men-centric, unisex or unclear and give a short practical reason. Do not demand demographic shares, infer an individual's role, or mechanically label an entire category. Separate the end user from purchaser.” Save `audience_positioning` and reason alongside the fit row, not in canonical identity. A plausible women-centric judgment can meet; supported men-centric/unisex normally does not earn this particular point but cannot exclude the whole product. Unclear is Unclear. A plain suitcase can be unisex; a household kitchen aid may reasonably be women-centric; a baby is the user of a baby product even if its buyer positioning is women-centric. No hard female-percentage cutoff, sensitive individual profiling or new safety gate.

Other active benefits use the brief's practical interpretation. No measured shipping price, guaranteed confidence improvement or proof of future money-saving is demanded for a sensible labelled inference. A single product mechanism can reasonably support different benefits, but each gets its own meaningful reason; five copies of generic praise are not five sensible judgments.

The connected programme opts unfinished/new/changed fit inputs into `research-e-fit10-description-v3`. A current accepted unchanged v2 or v3 review is reused; changing the supported contract does not automatically reassess the whole pool. The default standalone preparation retains its declared v2 contract. V3 supplies `description_inference_context` containing the exact description hash and its genuinely populated `label/function/mechanism/features/audience` fields. Every returned criterion has `description_inference=null` for an observation-based or unknown judgment, or `{kind: product-description-inference-v1, description_hash, fields}` naming the fields the model actually used. Description-only positive and negative judgments require inferred basis, no fabricated observation references and an `Estimated:` reason. The evaluator receives the actual current description and the explicit contract; a copied hash or marker alone is insufficient. Product form may support qualitative weight/handling and plausible time-saving judgments without another search. Unsupported numerical measurements, sales, prices and demographic percentages are not created from the description.

Before a new description-contract question is created, the programme recovers an actually sent exact-current v2 original and any actually sent correction through their original immutable job ownership with no live runner. An empty prepared retry cannot erase an earlier sent request; unresolved dispatch remains that product's named pending work. An untouched preparation does not block, and a completed validated output rejection can enter v3 once. The 22 new recovery cases and 222 distinct neighboring cases preserve existing accepted work, batch ownership, finite attempts and healthy siblings. For a partial-v3 update of an older v2 parent, only the new base receives explicit `description_inference=null` for retained observation/Unknown rows; old answer bytes and attribution remain unchanged. In a marked-v3 report, an empty-reference writing benefit requires exact non-null accepted description attribution; removing or nulling that marker cannot create a benefit. Unmarked historical packets retain their recorded contract. The joined 220-case verification includes real Python-to-portal canonical hash validation, not live research or publication.

The assessment and frozen product facts carry `fit_description_contract=product-description-inference-v1`. This binding is validated through current-fit reading, selection, all ten report rows, supported writing facts, dataset/workbook/HTML, G input and the private website's canonical-description check. It gives no market-source vote and does not change family relevance. G receives the accepted benefit phrase and its inferred basis, not a made-up source reference. One v3-local output correction covers malformed selected fields, description hash or estimate label; an unchanged exhausted answer is replayed without further calls. A saved rejected v2 repair can additionally receive a separate hash-bound unknown attribution and one explicitly named v3 review; original response bytes remain untouched and automatic preparation reuses that same current full-review base. Current observed v2 facts and historical packets keep their original meaning.

#### C. Exact upstream/downstream consumer changes

| Seam | Required implementation and connection |
|---|---|
| `research_ai.CRITERIA`, E schema/instructions/validator/`prompt_version`, `Jobs` and `CodexRunner` | Use an explicit original-ID map, not enumerate over a shortened tuple. Mode-aware contract selection binds full-fit/partial-review, legacy checklist repair and unchanged relevance separately across runner/cache/replay. New output cannot force margin or fill IDs1–13. |
| `research_flow.CHECKLIST_SCOPE/CHECKLIST_RULES`, `assessment_item`, current-result lookup | Separate factual input hash from fit-policy/prompt/requested-ID binding. Current facts alone cannot make an old rubric current. Keep immutable historical rule definitions by contract; current-fit rules are separate. Cache/suppression keys include policy/mode; an old failed job does not block new-policy review, while unchanged new failure is reused. |
| `checklist_repair._original/_run_repair/REPAIR_RULES` and accepted-review path | Historical repair resolves original declared checklist scope/rules/validator from the saved request; its current global-rule equality checks and current E dispatch must change together. Keep the original13-ID repair question/version when repairing an old rejected response, including the runner/cache binding. A new-policy named projection/review is a separate appended result referencing original assessment/current facts/policy; never edits an old accepted answer or retries until five. |
| `research_flow.research_status/candidate_summary/write_summary` and `report_selection._assessment/build_report_pool` | Show active score/total/tier, current versus legacy rows and every separate blocker. Five-point ideas enter the fit-passing pool and useful missing-market queue. Unknown does not appear as criterion failure. |

Fit-policy change reuses valid saved market relevance/CSV instead of forcing collection or claiming strengthened demand. |

The up-to-six supported-benefit `writing_facts` list is for why-product prose; the separate complete criterion array feeds every checklist phrase. Retired points cannot leak through either field or positional arrays. |
| `report_narration` G phrase contract and `report_workflow` | Implemented `research-g-v3` supplies exactly ten active original IDs in each required language. Changes to fit policy/meaning are factual revisions, not presentation-only reuse. Individual old writing is reusable only when its actual inputs still match; no edited hash. Explicit declared-version G schema/instructions/validator dispatch covers Jobs, CodexRunner, `validate_results`/`validate_narration_result`, `_completed_items`, `_correct_roman_item` and `recovered_narration_problems`. Genuine G-v1/v2 schemas/contexts/intents and exact v1→v2/Roman-v2 recovery proof are preserved; merely allowing old version IDs while validating with new global SCHEMAS is insufficient. Ten phrases are required only for new G-v3; historical recovery is not current ten-phrase reuse and changed facts cannot recover/reuse an old answer. |
