Website release code recomputes count/tier; mixed/tampered contracts, Boolean/float IDs and unsafe actual workbook-link destinations fail. Blank references on unknown checklist rows round-trip as empty Excel cells without losing strict comparison of supported refs. Existing private access/hashes/conditional R2 writes stay intact. Deploying the changed website code remains a later release boundary. |
| `material_updates` and publication history | Compare genuinely comparable source/product/supplier changes; rubric, count, tier or removed rows alone are not useful updates and never create New identity. Preserve original published baselines. |
| `suppliers` / F / description and identity jobs | Do not change functional audience or product facts just to earn ID13. Unchanged supplier scope/quotes, A/B identity and source acquisitions remain reusable; no new sourcing request caused solely by scoring policy. |

#### D. Bounded legacy projection and judgment review

1. Freeze old policy/source/description/result references and enumerate currently applicable assessments. Old recorded147/61/86 and revision5's143 rows keep their legacy meanings. Retiring4/10 can remove awarded points; the old five-/six-score bins cannot simply be relabelled Decent/Good. Do not claim49 new ready products.
2. Read through the legacy adapter. Retain supported unaffected active judgments only when their facts/references are still applicable. Unsupported not-stated negatives become Unclear in the appended current-policy interpretation with an explicit diagnostic; no free point or alteration of the original record.
3. Complete a practical current ten-row re-review for all153 non-established working ideas. Use existing saved product context and applicable genuine judgments before collecting a missing material fact; do not make every subjective benefit await more platforms. The47 partial products contain315 unknown rows:94 time/audience placeholders plus221 older unknowns. Review all ten meanings, not only the94 placeholders. All six unusable current-assessment paths have failed reference-binding work and no completed repair; four lack an accepted assessment and two retain stale older assessments. Inspect the exact saved failures before repair; recover valid results or perform one current full assessment on their actual current inputs. Do not call them permanently unassessable or repeatedly invoke a partial helper that rejects the changed parent. Persist the current base, original job/failure/assessment refs, requested IDs, policy/mode/contract and evidence refs before dispatch. Batch compatible items under existing limits; practical re-review is not153 source recollections or every model role on every product.
4. Combine accepted reviewed rows only with that saved base, then validate exactly ten rows and code-derived fit. Before application, check the factual inputs and parent/retained binding under the canonical writer; use the existing previous-hash commit pattern from `checklist_repair`. Check the stable application key first on replay: a verified already-applied result is not applied again, even if a later assessment superseded it. A changed base retains the saved answer and records affected-item reconciliation; do not automatically rebase it onto different retained rows, overwrite a newer assessment or dispatch again. Append an accepted current assessment once, or save precise pending/rejected review and reopening trigger. Permit upward/downward/no-change results; no score-chasing. Old source relevance remains valid independently of an obsolete fit rubric.
5. Rebuild affected current views/pools/questions and feedback under the new policy. Keep old feedback as historical policy-bound evidence, not current “not promising” suppression. A useful four-point product with unknowns receives proportional practical review, not a permanent false rejection or unlimited research.
6. Revalidate affected draft reservations through the existing replacement mechanism. Preserve published/queued identity history and held products. Frozen old packets remain immutable; a new-policy issue receives a new ordinary factual revision. Do not silently approve old formats under a new meaning or remove live holds before confirmed replacement.
7. Measure new-policy completed screening, pending fit/market, eligible Good/Decent, supplier work, New inventory and selection reasons. Retain5% planning/3% stress as conditional old-cost sensitivities until measured new-policy yield justifies a forecast update. A relaxed rubric does not retroactively reduce US$0.50040006 or settle read holds.

#### E. Priority without new bottlenecks

Apply identity/category/brand, current evidence, held-history and release-freshness checks first. Use section20.9's four ordered combinations:6+ with two usable related sites;5 with two;6+ with one reasonably positive;5 with one reasonably positive. Two-site tiers do not retain the old mandatory positive-growth gate. One-site tiers need an actual supported favourable judgment, not missing data, a supplier result or bare QC stock/price. A supplier search is real work, not a minimum lead-count gate.

Allocation must maintain enough eligible New choices to meet each issue's floor and update cap. Among permissible choices, prefer the four-tier priority first, then meaningful support/current score, useful category balance/freshness and stable concept ID. Thus five Matches plus two usable sites precedes six Matches plus one reasonably positive site. Do not spend scarce New choices on extras before due issue minima or break the New floor for a stronger Updated product. Pool and allocation consume the same shared priority; category balance never forces weak selections.

Missing-evidence dispatch includes all four priorities and the initial all-product completion queue, with recover/saved-work/progress/age fairness. Ready independent work proceeds while another product's source is blocked. There is no Good-only report, demographic-proof gate or obligatory image. The all-route coverage audit records applicable attempts and truthful limitations; it is not a rule that every product must have every platform result before an issue can be assembled. Record exact deferred reasons and continue independent work.

#### F. Transition tests and stopping rules

Affected existing suites include service policy, research flow/operations/checklist repair, report selection/wiring, market validation/follow-ups, catalogue expansion, issue allocation, live outputs/narration/workflow/material updates, delivery packages and the hosted release-admin tests.

Add explicit fixtures for: original-ID coverage/no renumbering;4/5/6/10 boundaries; retired Meets ignored; unknown not negative; five points with five unknowns included; specific inferred time-saving; supported no-saving; women-/men-/unisex/unclear positioning without automatic product exclusion; baby-user versus buyer identity reuse; same factual hash/new policy; old rejection versus current review; unchanged new rejection reuse; independent marketplace relevance unchanged; five-point missing-market admission; Good priority surviving allocator/category ties; New-floor exception to priority; legacy13 versus current10 local/server packets; wrong total/tier/policy/duplicate IDs rejected; both language ten-phrase/workbook consistency; policy-only change not New/Updated; old packets/source dates unchanged.

Also test E full/partial fit versus empty-criteria relevance dispatch; unchanged relevance job/review IDs and reuse after fit migration; exact legacy-rule repair/replay/runner intent despite a new fit default; unsupported mode/version refusing only the affected item; and G declared-version schema/runner/intent dispatch preserving genuine old outputs, exact v1→v2/Roman-v2 recovery and completed-item reuse. New G-v3 requires all ten phrases; legacy schemas do not, and changed facts/context still cannot recover a failed job or reuse historical writing. Finish the migration when these consumers/tests and a real new-policy candidate/report sample agree. A failed source, optional-field unknown or absent Good quota is not a reason to halt unrelated work. Stop the affected transition only for a real binding/contract/history defect and retain exact evidence. No extra database, checker model, approval service or generic scoring framework is required.

Partial-review fixtures additionally pin retained rows/parent hash before dispatch, change another accepted row before completion, and interrupt before/after application. The stale result remains saved without overwriting the new base or starting another call; healthy siblings still commit. Exact replay before or after later supersession never reinstates the older assessment.

### 21.17 Action-level fallback and continuation contract

#### A. Required outcome, not an impossible success promise

**A failed attempt must not halt unrelated useful work.** At each step preserve what succeeded, isolate what failed, choose an eligible permitted alternative or a different ready task, and save the precise remaining work. This applies to source collection, interpretation, decisions, suppliers, report production and delivery. It does not mean a source/model will always answer, an off computer can run local research, or a corrupt money/history record can be ignored. Already approved uploaded issues remain independent of local research failures.

Use the existing direct dispatcher, jobs, questions, problem records and package-status files. No broker, permanent per-step watchdog, unlimited retry chain, second checker model or extra subscription. Each lane implements the contract during its owning block; P5 connects the shared dispatcher. Do not infer whole-program coverage from one component: paid dispatch/cause certainty retain their owning P4/P5 checks. P3 now preserves independently checked workbook/HTML/PDF stages across narration, language-choice and renderer failures; the exact implemented transition is below.

Every new action adapter must declare its stage, owned resources, execution deadline, required/optional dependencies, allowed alternative routes, retry class, durable result location and continuation trigger. Register no route merely because a trial worked: P1b/P6 must first implement and prove it. A fallback cannot silently broaden access, change countries/terms, bypass CAPTCHA/TLS/login, switch to a paid plan/model, launch Apify Trends without the agreed discussion, publish unapproved bytes or weaken fit/market/novelty/size requirements.

#### B. Exact dispatch and saved-result sequence

1. **Select:** read the current run scope, remaining allowance and dependency-ready work. Recovery is ready only when a saved completion/receipt, permissible recovery time, changed relevant evidence or real access/authority input exists. A waiting entry with unchanged dependencies is skipped, not continually tried first. Age fairness and required-issue minima remain part of selection.
2. **Prepare:** verify bindings, supported capability, ownership, hard payload/file limits and funded authority. Use valid same-question cached work before external requests. Split oversized inputs before intent/launch, never by slicing away meaning. No available route becomes `waiting` with its trigger, not a false failed product check.
3. **Execute:** record the exact intent/reservation first, then run only the owned bounded request/process. Retain its response/receipt/raw capture before interpretation. Deadlines clean up only owned local resources; a local timeout does not prove a remote operation ended.
4. **Classify/apply:** validate independent rows/items; commit usable siblings once. Separate transport success, parser success, semantic applicability, evidence outcome and package completeness. Keep confirmed execution, usage and evidence certainty separate. A bad item cannot discard valid siblings; unknown is never fabricated success or a negative demand finding.
5. **Fallback:** inspect execution certainty before retry. Reparse saved bytes or reuse valid saved results first; otherwise try only the declared bounded alternative when appropriate. If no useful permitted route exists, mark the affected action waiting/failed and choose another ready item/lane. Do not create another acquisition/job ID to conceal an unresolved equivalent operation.
6. **Advance/finish:** reconcile intent-bound consumption and save progress before selecting again. Completion of one issue freezes it but does not cancel useful remaining work. When deadline/allowance is exhausted or every remaining dependency is waiting, return completed/partial/waiting/failed counts, next scheduled trigger and last committed position. Same-period restart retains this allowance/expiry. A new due period receives only the explicit new envelope in section 21.4; old execution and liability IDs remain unchanged. Intentional operator stop/cancellation is saved and honoured, not swallowed as an error or cleared by a new period ID.

**Result fields:** action/logical-question/run ID; target concept/child/issue/source/route/account; current dependency fingerprint; stage; state (`completed|partial|waiting|failed`); observed reason/evidence/artifact refs; suspected/confirmed/unknown underlying cause; execution certainty (`not_launched|ended|may_be_running|saved_completion`); actual action/item/model/read/spend allowance consumed; fallback chosen/outcome; reopening trigger; independent eligible work. Store these versioned fields in the existing appropriate task/problem/batch/package records; stable event keys make replay idempotent. Batch summaries contain counts/refs, not growing copies of every source/trace. A `completed` empty search is possible, but unavailable/failed is not empty. An action outcome does not override a product evidence state.

`research_flow.problem` must describe the observed exception separately from its suspected root cause; do not set every underlying cause to confirmed or every retry count to zero. Keep detailed redacted diagnostics private and show a short useful grouped message. Unexpected adapter exceptions are isolated at the action boundary **only when** ownership, canonical state and execution uncertainty remain understood. Unknown write/commit outcomes are reconciliation, not an invented successful failure record. Never claim the failure was persisted when that write itself failed.

#### C. Bounded retry, cooldown and reopening

| Failure class | Permitted immediate action | Reopening rule |
|---|---|---|
| Confirmed prelaunch invalid/oversized input | Correct the known adapter/contract or split intact items; no external attempt was consumed. Preparation still consumes its declared finite action allowance. | Exact corrected input/contract; no unchanged invalid preparation loop. |
| Confirmed transient unbilled read failure | At most one same-route retry in this pass, then at most one implemented allowed alternative; obey request deadline and run limits. | Respect a supplied retry time; otherwise defer to the next finite scheduled pass, not a tight loop. |
| Provider billed GET/status/read | Existing provider-specific finite controls win; current `MetaTrial.read` bounds retryable reads to three within its deadline. Count all read holds/fees. | Original-account/run recovery; no free-read assumption or collector relaunch. |
| Source challenge, blocked access, unavailable browser or layout failure | No rapid same-route retries. Use retained bytes where possible or one demonstrated allowed alternate. | Actual route/reader repair or next scheduled diagnostic; one due diagnostic per shared source/purpose/transport boundary, not one per product. |
| Temporary model throttle versus quota exhaustion | Preserve the actual diagnostic distinction; generic rate-limit wording is not proof of exhausted subscription. Retain inputs/results and continue non-model work. | Supplied retry/reset time, reauthenticated capability or next bounded pass if unknown. No poll for a guessed reset. |
| Invalid model contract/phrase with a confirmed ended attempt | Reuse good siblings; allow at most one exact-item contract repair within the same run limit. | Corrected contract or real input change after that repair; no generation until a preferred score/brand verdict appears. |
| Factual/semantic disagreement or insufficient evidence | Save ambiguity; use one useful targeted evidence/review action if eligible. Not a schema-repair retry. | Material new evidence, named policy/meaning correction or useful newly available view. |
| Paid/model launch or exit uncertain; remote write reply lost | Recover original saved intent/identity/output first. Do not relaunch or re-chunk the same logical work; do not switch accounts to evade uncertainty. | Exact completion/termination/readback or properly bound recovery receipt. A new research/provider period does not clear uncertainty. Independent funded **different** work may proceed once its own dependencies are trusted. |
| Canonical state/permission/funds unavailable | Hold only dependent writes/spending/approval; use independently trusted saved artifacts or existing hosted reports where useful. | Actual trusted-state restore, access/authority change, or funded renewal. No corruption bypass or automatic purchase. |

These are planned defaults for new continuation, not retroactive permission to change historical retry caps. Each physical attempt consumes its source/model/read limits; fallback selection does not reset them. Section20.6's paid reuse/repeat-heavy cooldown is not a blanket outage delay or ban on similar free sources; section26 gives free work complete request-scope reuse and fair revisits. Use existing saved owner/problem messages instead of repeated prompts. A later actual diagnostic may prove route recovery; an old blocked receipt is neither permanent nor rewritten successful.

#### D. Per-action failure and fallback matrix

| Action / owner | Failures to cover | Fallback and independent continuation |
|---|---|---|
| Startup/config/run ownership — P2/P5 | Invalid policy, missing executable, changed settings, stale scope, overlapping start | Diagnose the exact capability. Use supported saved-input/offline work where dependencies are trusted; busy run does not acquire a second canonical writer. Bad source config need not invalidate other source config. Invalid shared policy blocks its consumers, not existing hosted reports. |
| Choose/catalogue/feedback — P1a/P5 | No eligible question, repeat-heavy area, synonym proposals, malformed C output, delayed feedback | Reuse accepted decisions; apply paid cooldown only at its charged scope. Useful free synonyms retain identity/history and may get a fair turn. Choose grounded ready work/revisit; no repeated C brainstorming until positive or default endless paging. |
| Paid preparation/launch — P4 | Bad key/permission/headroom/build/controls, intent write failure, lost POST reply | Proved prelaunch failure permits a different valid funded action/account. After possible launch recover the exact original account/run/receipt; liability remains. Direct/saved analysis or another independent funded request can continue; never launch an uncertain equivalent on account two. |
| Paid status/read/import — P4 | Still-running Actor, expired original key, charged read timeout, counter lag, partial receipt, delayed charges | Use verified replacement credential for the same account and bounded original-run reads; retain holds/bytes. Reconcile before recapture; append delayed charges. Other independently funded work is not globally blocked by this account's uncertainty. |
| Browser/HTTP capture — P1b/P6 | Startup/crash, owned cleanup uncertain, DNS/TLS/proxy trouble, network loss, timeout,429/403/challenge, chart/export failure | Keep exact stage and whether navigation happened. Saved usable capture → compatible permitted browser/reader → demonstrated equivalent route, within bounds. Otherwise defer that question and use another source/product/lane. Different marketplace evidence is not counterfeit Trends evidence. Shared transport failure suppresses per-item retry storms. |
| Parse/map/scope — P2/P1b | HTTP200 challenge, changed selectors, wrong list/country/query, empty/partial pages, bad encoding/duplicate/hash/ref conflicts | Reparse retained bytes after a specific reader fix; preserve usable independent rows and diagnostic refs. A failed parser is not zero demand. Wrong-scope records can remain useful for their actual subject, not be attached to the requested one. No blanket recollection. |
| A product/service/brand — P2/P5 | Oversize input, malformed child, model unavailable, mixed purchase units, uncertain/mistaken mainstream label | Reuse saved interpretation; split before launch; preserve good siblings. Unclear brand does not exclude. Review mistaken brand at its original child/description binding (section 21.18); E cannot restore an upstream-excluded child. Other products continue. |
| B/D identity — P2/P5 | Weak/missing shortlist, ambiguous meaning, invalid match ID, missing/wrong photo, new relevant indexed candidate | Use existing bounded10→20 candidate widening and useful real-photo path only when applicable. Unclear stays unresolved; do not force same/different or create a duplicate to fill a report. Reopen when relevant facts/views/candidates change, not every global event. |
| E fit/relevance — P2 | Retired/duplicate/wrong IDs, unknowns taken negative, stale/incorrect refs, failed sibling, incomplete response | Preserve supported rows/siblings under their exact contract; normalise absent basis to Unclear where allowed, review only named policy/meaning errors. Reuse unchanged valid relevance independently of fit. No score-chasing, invented refs or whole-pool rescoring merely to continue. |
| Market arithmetic/freshness — P2/P5 | Missing OR fields, insufficient points, unrelated chart/list, sparse data, expired scope, divide-by-zero/comparability error | Calculate only supported branches; missing family unavailable/insufficient. Complete an independent missing core family or advance another product; refresh affected expiry only. No extra proof gate, substitute family count, policy-only demand improvement or fabricated zero. |
| Supplier discovery — P2/P6/P5 | Blocked/layout/challenge search, zero results, irrelevant leads, all nominated pages unavailable | Distinguish completed empty search from failed search, inaccessible pages and interpretation pending internally. Use one demonstrated permitted alternate plus surviving useful pages/leads. After actual bounded effort with no usable lead, include an otherwise eligible product with the fixed simple limitation below; failed attempts do not become successful searches or proof supply is absent. No mandatory successful lead. |
| F supplier meaning — P3/P6 | Unrelated/mixed purchase units, unknown membership/dispatch, conflicting terms, one failed lead, stale company scope | Preserve usable lead siblings and honest Not-stated/Conflicting fields; fetch another relevant accessible terms page only when useful. No monthly-fee absence claim, MOQ-to-dispatch inference or exact supplier price from a related accessory. |
| Prices/photos — P3 | Missing amount/currency/pack, misleading range/from/tax, wrong image/carousel, expired URL, corrupt/oversized/MIME/redirect failure | Keep raw qualified quote or Unavailable, not zero or margin. Use a provenance-checked saved same-product image/alternative real view; otherwise visible no-photo treatment. No generated/family image passed off as the product, blind unit division or fresh model call for every missing optional field. |
| Selection/allocation/history — P2/P7 | Fewer than10, unavailable Good candidates, New-floor/hold conflict, ambiguous identity, unknown remote publication state | Advance eligible Decent work; preserve supported unselected inventory and exact shortfall. Other complete issues continue. Unknown remote history permits saved analysis but holds dependent final allocation/approval. No weak padding, forced updates or released-history reset. |
| G writing/translation — P2/P3 | Bad slot/reference, empty title, script error, model unavailable, one failed language/criterion phrase | Retain accepted exact-fact siblings; one eligible exact-item contract repair. Produce a facts-only **internal incomplete** workbook when useful, not invented prose or a complete bilingual manifest. First-two issue completion still requires both editions. |
| Workbook/HTML/PDF/inspection — P3 | Missing renderer/font, timeout/crash, overflow/cropped conditions, bad links, invalid/tampered saved PDF, one edition fails | Persist per-artifact/language stage and verified siblings; resume exact missing stage. Re-render unchanged reviewed facts without G when valid; changed template/bytes get declared new render attempt/revision. No manual PDF editing, silent overwrite or public-quality approval from structure alone. |
| Package/approval — P3/P7 | Incomplete manifest, changed facts/expiry/ordinal, missing third-language choice, owner unavailable | Preserve correct internal artifacts, hold only affected approval/package. Third facts/workbook and other complete approved issues proceed. No default language choice, fake complete hash or implicit owner approval. |
| Upload/activate/reconcile — P7 | DNS/owner access failure, partial upload, lost reply, ETag conflict, stale activation state, download mismatch | Read exact immutable object/index and verify issue/revision/hash/ETag before another write. Already committed exact state is success; unresolved state stays pending. Keep previous valid releases; no alternate public bucket, TLS bypass, new revision to hide uncertainty or blind activation. |
| Event/result commit/index — P2/P4/P5 | Busy lock, stale dependencies, interrupted atomic write, full/read-only disk, corrupt authority, optional index failure | Reread conflicting dependency once under proper lock; apply saved valid result once when possible. Confirmed event plus optional-index failure is committed/index-degraded, not lost application. Rebuild disposable index from trusted events. Actual canonical failure preserves last committed position/isolated captures and holds dependent external intents/spending. No deletes, forged event or false “problem saved” claim. |
| Schedule/cancel/alerts/backup — P5/P8 | Missed start/computer off, repeated start, exhausted old run/new due week, cancelled run, failed warning delivery, unavailable backup destination | Resume same-period remaining work or admit useful backlog into the one new due-period envelope in21.4. Uploaded reports still release online. Honour stop/cancel scope and ownership; do not accumulate missed allowances. Durable grouped status handles notification failure, not another service. Keep originals if backup fails; do not claim backup/restore or billing protection proved. |

**Agreed supplier continuation — implemented P2:** actually attempted supplier research with no usable lead does not hold an otherwise eligible product out of the issue. Client wording is **“Supplier search did not produce any usable results.”** Keep blocked/error/empty/pending detail and next trigger in internal records, not jargon in the client panel. This does not claim that supply is unavailable, zero suppliers exist or any supplier is confirmed. Prepared links, a busy reader and prelaunch failure are not actual effort; a launched owned navigation/read failure is. Selection, frozen facts, both-language rendering and local/hosted validators use the same receipt contract from section 21.7.

#### E. Partial internal files and exact downstream recovery

Extend P3's workbook-only path (already needed for third-language waiting) to narration/model/render failures. Freeze checked **facts**; this is not owner publication approval. Create only the independently validated internal workbook/HTML/finished editions actually supported. Status records include dataset/fact binding, required languages, each stage's input/artifact hash, completed/pending/failed result and next trigger. An incomplete output is visibly incomplete and cannot produce a complete delivery manifest or be activated.

**Exact partial-file transition — P3, implemented:** keep immutable `package-input.json` as issue/input authority. `live_outputs.write_issue_workbook(issue,dataset,destination)` saves checked narrative-independent core facts and one workbook under `data/outputs/<issue>/revision-<n>/internal-stages/`. `write_issue_edition(issue,dataset,destination,language)` independently saves `media/live/reports/<issue>/revision-<n>/internal-stages/report-<language>.html`; valid English does not require the workbook to have saved or Hinglish to be complete. Core excludes `narratives` and `selected_language`; its hash matches G's frozen fact hash. Each completed language binds that core, exact own writing/slots, actual English-source hash for Hinglish, price-display-v2 and pinned render/style/logo inputs. An unrelated pending language cannot change its binding.

Each small stage keeps its exact complete `.payload` bytes durably **before** the original intent, then saves the checked artifact alias and receipt. Restart verifies hashes/byte bounds/paths and finishes only an interrupted save from the same payload; an already completed artifact that later disappeared or changed is an integrity problem, not silently repaired. Paths and symlinks are checked. New workbook stages pin `deterministic-workbook-v1`, including ZIP/property timestamps, so a pre-intent interruption can compare the expected bytes rather than bless a foreign file. A short local stage lock covers these local writes only; there is no model/network call. `write_issue_package` promotes exact checked workbook/own HTML bytes only after all required writing exists, then freezes final narratives/facts. Final draft manifests retain additive core/writing/render/receipt bindings, and the delivery validator checks them without requiring working-stage payloads to remain forever. Native PDF intent/inspection/promotion stays in the existing workflow; there is no falsely complete partial delivery manifest.

Historical whole-dataset manifests remain immutable. An older partially completed packet's original nondeterministic workbook may be adopted only after same core/selection, original manifest paths/hashes/current price contract and semantic workbook validation all pass; record `legacy-verified-workbook-v1` and its exact original manifest origin. A workbook alone is not provenance. Original final facts/HTML/workbook/PDF bytes are preserved. If an old immutable final dataset truly lacked required later prose, adding it requires a new revision; matching core facts are not permission to rewrite its historical full-dataset hash.

`report_workflow.prepare_review_package` records useful workbook progress before language/G completion. Render/inspection exceptions save the failed language/stage; another fully written edition renders independently within the same allowance. On restart verify stage hashes, reuse exact accepted G/edition bytes and continue only missing work. After all required language slots pass, assemble the final dataset once, create the final facts/workbook/edition manifests and validate the complete delivery manifest. Reuse staged workbook/PDF bytes only when their final dependency contract matches; the final bundle records both shared dataset hash and edition-specific fact/writing/render bindings. Never overwrite a frozen final dataset with later narratives or drop a language to make its hash fit. A byte/dependency conflict requires diagnosis and a declared new attempt/revision, not an untrusted overwrite; no extra G call for unchanged valid writing.

`generate_report_narratives` isolates owned local-file failures at saved-item reads, bounded G chunks and exact-item corrections. Completed editions and accepted saved items survive a failed sibling. A failed problem-record save is returned separately in `problem_save_failures` and retained in the workflow's writing status; the program does not falsely claim that record saved. Raw OS diagnostics become `REPORT_NARRATION_LOCAL_FILE_FAILED`, not exposed paths or credentials. Pure invalid frozen facts and authoritative store/intent/result conflicts still raise rather than starting a replacement job. An unreadable correction context launches no new request and preserves previously validated supplied editions. A replay reuses exact accepted writing and completed English PDF bytes, completes only missing Hinglish and creates the bilingual delivery manifest only after both pass. Fifty-four focused narration checks and53 neighbouring workflow checks passed without provider calls; these are separate overlapping test groups, not a whole-application completion percentage.

**Completed packet replay:** readiness still derives from the actual checked delivery manifest/files, never a previous status claim. After that check, the workflow may retain `job_ids`, presentation `source_job_ids` and stage diagnostics only from an exactly matching completed issue/revision/input/package/manifest status with valid attribution shapes. Workbook receipt and language/PDF/HTML hashes, byte lengths, owned paths and required languages must match the actual manifest. Missing, stale or malformed optional status donates no metadata; protected path/integrity failures remain visible. Reopening the same completed packet therefore keeps its original AI attribution without another AI call or PDF render. Fifty-seven focused workflow checks passed, including six original job links retained on exact replay; this is local recovery proof, not new report publication.

**Exact private-write transition — P7:** `portal_delivery_check` demonstrates immutable object-upload readback, not activation-index recovery. Save upload/activation intent with approval, client/issue/revision/package/selection hashes, schedule, object hashes and original expected ETag/prior state. Recovery first reads objects/index and checks whether the exact approved mutation is already committed, **before** `activate_delivery_package` rejects its original ETag. If that exact active complete revision/schedule is present and referenced bytes verify, return its original server receipt and reconcile local history without another conditional write. Preserve activation time/availability intervals; do not use today's readback date as first publication or recheck archive freshness as a new activation.

If not committed, distinguish exact unchanged pre-write state from an unrelated/conflicting/superseded/withdrawn state. Only the still-valid missing uploads/conditional action may continue against verified state with current approval/ordinal/source-validity checks. Do not silently replace the saved ETag with today's value, overwrite a competing revision, regenerate the report or reactivate a superseded issue. A historical committed-but-superseded revision contributes its real availability history but is not recovered as currently active. Lost reply plus expired owner access defers readback; offline work and prior server releases continue. Management-plugin access is not a named-owner credential, and public access is never its fallback.

If authoritative storage cannot save intent, new dependent model/provider calls do not start. If a response exists but application commit fails, retain its isolated output and original execution identity; trusted restoration applies it once. With no writable storage, no route/model/access and no eligible cached work, the only honest continuation is a clean waiting result/next start, not an infinite loop or imagined alternate source. Do not promise unattended local operation while the machine is off or full paid fallback permission under “keep going.”

#### F. Failure combinations, tests and finish evidence

Test failure families at each adapter boundary plus concrete shared-dependency combinations; no claim to enumerate every conceivable future circumstance. Every new action must reuse this contract and add its relevant tests.

| Fixture | Required evidence |
|---|---|
| One A/E/F result invalid; another valid | Good sibling saved/applied, failed item has exact reason, next useful item executes. No all-batch rerun. |
| Good-fit source blocked; eligible Decent item ready | Decent work advances; fit, market, history and New-floor rules remain unchanged. |
| Browser capture succeeds; model throttled/quota unavailable | Original capture/date retained; pending interpretation distinct from zero yield; deterministic/saved work advances. |
| Model completion saved; commit interrupted | Exact result applied once after trusted recovery; zero second model call or altered intent. |
| Paid launch reply lost; original key or provider period expires | Liability/guard retained, same-account credential recovery, separately funded new recovery read when required; no equivalent replacement POST or reset lifetime read limits. |
| Shared DNS/transport outage across sources/accounts | One grouped transport condition, bounded due probe, no per-item/account retry storm; local supported work advances. |
| Browser fails; owned cleanup or receipt saving also fails | Both observations retained where possible, running certainty not invented; no deletion/relabelled success or leaked unrelated-process cleanup. |
| B is unclear; D photos unavailable | Association deferred with original scope, no forced new concept/merge; other products execute. |
| Supplier search blocked; alternate inaccessible; F/model unavailable | Actual execution-stage proof/canonical effort projection and reopening triggers preserved. Real effort permits fixed-limitation inclusion; prelaunch-only busy does not. No false successful-search status or fabricated supplier row; independent work advances. |
| English complete; Hinglish G/render fails | Core facts/internal workbook/English stage preserved without prematurely freezing final narratives. Complete Hinglish after restart, assemble once, reuse exact English bytes with no second G call; bilingual packet remains incomplete until then. |
| PDF defect; photo absent; valid writing unchanged | Layout/photo treatment fixed from actual same facts, no G call/manual PDF patch; required inspection remains pending until passed. |
| Upload/activation reply lost; owner login expires; client email deferred | Preserve original intent/ETag. Recovered exact committed activation produces one history entry/no second CAS; genuine conflict/supersession is not bypassed. No public fallback; offline work/prior releases continue. |
| Busy/invalid canonical store; optional index unavailable | No lock steal or unrecorded spend. If the event committed but its index failed, return committed/degraded and rebuild after restart; exact replay applies once with no provider/model rerun. Actual event-write uncertainty is reconciled separately. |
| No eligible work; every route waiting; allowance ends | Stable waiting summary/next trigger; unchanged restart makes no external calls, no busy loop or renewed budget. |
| Computer restarts after missed schedule; duplicate start/cancel | Single ownership, exact persisted progress, cancel honoured and uploaded releases unaffected. |
| Original allowance exhausted; new week starts with unselected and interpreted backlog | One new explicitly due envelope, old counters/attempt IDs unchanged; useful carryover and fresh work advance. Duplicate start grants none, missed weeks do not accumulate allowances and service stop cannot be evaded. |
| New-period admission while old model/provider execution remains uncertain | Original output/exit/run recovery only; completed content applies once. Different ready work may use new capacity, never an equivalent replacement launch or renewed repair limit. |

Acceptance counts actual dispatches/retries/reads/model calls, saved good siblings, once-only application, dependency-specific waits and unchanged historical files. Fault fixtures do not need deliberate charges. Add bounded actual route/private-owner proof within current continuous authority; client publication still needs exact package approval. Independently review healthy-sibling progress and uncertainty accounting, not merely that exceptions were caught.

### 21.18 Moderate brand judgment: actual owner and correction design

**Current local implementation:** A's `PRODUCT.brand` returns `generic_or_small`, `established` or `unclear` alongside its product description. A's instructions explicitly say unknown small manufacturers are not established brands. P2's shared `current_brand`/`brand_binding` applies an exact compatible current overlay/operator/creation-origin correction before the original verdict, without rewriting the functional description. The upstream child gate, E admission, supplier work and final selection use that same helper; unclear is not automatic rejection. Code validates shape/legal values/reasons and records the judgment, not semantic certainty. There is no brand-name blacklist. A separately labels product/mixed/service/uninterpretable material; a supplier's service capabilities are F terms, not a branded physical-product decision.

`exclude_established_brand` is an existing **one-way source-backed operator correction** for an existing concept, with original-description hash, reviewer/date and source excerpt. It preserves prior jobs and frozen report/history, but does not restore an upstream-excluded child that never obtained a concept. Do not present it as a complete two-way automatic correction mechanism or ask E to recover such a child.

**Target decision:** supplied product context → AI mainstream-versus-small/generic/unclear judgment → code applies only the reviewed verdict. An established retailer selling a generic tool is not automatically the tool's brand. A manufacturer's name, unfamiliar label, private-label uncertainty or ambiguous excerpt is not enough for exclusion. Known mainstream brand-specific products such as Lakme/Hasbro/Meyer normally remain excluded. Retain the visible reason and supporting context; ordinary subjective judgments do not require certification, worldwide brand research or a second model for every item.

**Implemented targeted correction, not routine extra AI:** additive `brand_reviews` records use stable review keys in existing memory. `prepare_brand_review` freezes target kind (`child|concept`), actual target/source IDs, original accepted description/association hashes, verdict, rationale, evidence refs, reviewer, version/date and superseded review; `apply_brand_review` rechecks them under the canonical writer. `current_brand(state, target_kind, target_id, description)` reads a compatible concept overlay, applicable source-backed operator correction, valid creation-origin correction, then original A verdict. It does not replace functional description/identity; initial unchanged A jobs remain reusable. The table is additive in event replay; an old chain's absence means no new overlay, not lost original evidence. Automatic model-driven brand re-review below remains a separate P5 contract.

Update **all eligibility consumers** to that helper: `research_flow` incoming-child gate, assessment admission and candidate summary; `report_selection._identity` (the actual raw-brand gate), `_assessment` applicability and selection scope; `market_validation._review_marketplace`; `research_operations` question views; `checklist_repair` applicability; `supplier_capture_store` current-concept validation; `visual_followups` question/application gates; and `identity_corrections` survivor validation. Bind the current review ID/verdict into affected eligibility/selection/question fingerprints, not unchanged functional A/B or raw-source identities. Do not let a helper fix the report while upstream still filters on the old `description.brand` field. Existing legacy frozen datasets preserve their recorded original verdict, rather than applying today's overlay to old client files.

For automatic *named* brand re-review, use a separate explicit A mode/contract (`brand_review_only`, planned `research-a-brand-v1`) inside existing Jobs/runner dispatch, not a new model/provider. Input: target ID/current purchase-unit description, the bounded actual original/changed listing excerpts, previous verdict/reason and named concern. Output: same target ID, one of the three brand values, concise reason and only supplied source references. Prompt intent: “Is this particular product an established mainstream brand-specific product, a generic/small-manufacturer candidate, or unclear? Do not equate retailer/manufacturer names with a mainstream product brand. Unknown is not rejection. Do not demand relabelling permission or invent brand history.” Code validates exact target/ref/version and applies once; no arithmetic, identity rewrite or product selection is model-owned. Keep initial A triage schema/prompt and saved intent/cache compatible; mode-specific instructions/schema/version must flow through Jobs, validator, runner, saved completion and replay, as for E. No new call on every product and no retry until it says generic.

An explicit source-reviewed correction uses the overlay without a model call. For a child excluded before concept creation, the implemented named brand-only supersession requires unchanged original child/association/source binding, existing association `status=excluded`, `reason=established_brand`, no concept and an accepted generic/unclear review. It appends that review and replaces only this association with `brand_review_pending`, original semantic/child IDs and review/previous-association refs. The under-writer previous-hash check prevents stale overwrite. Pending-child enumeration includes this exact compatible state as well as missing associations; the ordinary effective-brand/category gate and B identity processing still run. Success replaces the pending association once using a stable review-bound event. Exact review replay does not reopen a resolved child or charge again.

**Carry correction through identity resolution:** current flow copies the original A product into a newly created concept. Keep that original description unchanged, but in the same resolve transaction store `origin_child_id` and the exact accepted `origin_brand_review_id`/source-description binding on the new concept and resolved association. For that new concept, `current_brand` first uses a compatible concept-target correction/existing operator review, then this verified creation-origin review, then the original A verdict. Verify the linked resolved association, child/review/description hashes and review applicability; a broken link records only the affected binding problem. An explicit later concept correction outranks the inherited creation review. Do not automatically transfer a child verdict to an already-existing concept from a B Same result or override that concept's own review; retain the child's admission provenance and handle any canonical brand conflict through its normal named review. This prevents corrected children being immediately excluded again during E, supplier work or selection, without rewriting A/B jobs or changing functional identity.

Do not reopen all excluded/deferred children. Out-of-scope, merged, retired, unresolved identity/conflict and protected-history blockers remain independent. This path does not invent a concept or copy another product's market/fit/supplier evidence. For an existing concept, correct its current verdict and invalidate only affected selection/dependency views; changing the brand cannot clear another exclusion reason. Published/held history is never deleted; protected allocations require their existing revision/reconciliation route. A mismatched/stale review remains unapplied with its exact reason, leaving other work running. Score changes do not themselves trigger brand re-review.

P2 preserves/exposes moderate A behaviour and fixes shared brand-consumer/explicit correction seams; P5 may dispatch the bounded automatic review mode after its named contract is implemented. Tests cover mainstream product, unknown manufacturer, established seller/generic product, unclear brand, product versus service, rejected child with no concept, correction→new concept→E/supplier/selection, exact replay after resolution, changed origin hash, B Same without overwriting another concept review, stale target, initial-A cache and unrelated/published-history noninterference. This is not a hard brand dictionary or routine human approval requirement.

### 21.19 Continuous execution and review order

The prior local P2 checkpoint remains measured evidence:147 projections,10 named reviews and an11-product internal packet. It is not all-product completion. Current execution continues through the entire nonvisual tracker below, using small connected changes and actual checks without milestone handbacks.

1. Continue the connected practical fit/source priorities and product-family query contracts; finish all153 applicable current assessments and reconcile exact saved failures before any permitted new attempt.
2. Complete applicable per-product work and interpretation through the existing14 adapters/common import/source controller. Reuse valid acquisitions across relevant targets, preserve actual not-tried/blocked/empty states and continue healthy routes independently.
3. Use the existing funded routing, durable advancement and native schedule; finish source-grounded catalogue admission/renewal and actual original/follow-on research, with no cloned156-product scope or fictional future yield.
4. Complete current functional report source numbers, photos, full suppliers and language stages; generate valid replacement/remaining packets and verify their private staging/downloads using the existing presentation. Two previously verified ten-product packets remain immutable evidence, not automatically current-ready files.
5. Review the whole connected flow, repair concrete defects, safely load verified changes and prove scheduled restart/recovery without duplicate work. Update measured coverage/cost/yield and local daily-backup acceptance. Request only actual missing owner/client inputs at their owning boundary; publication is not implicit approval.

Do not call a future adapter, a diagnostic queue or a documentation change an implemented capability. Keep progress visible with actual counts and exact limitations. If no dependency-ready work remains, give the specific external blockers and saved continuation triggers; otherwise continue.

## 22. Source connections and practical assessment implementation

### 22.1 Shared product-family query profile

Use the versioned profile in `product_queries.py` at the existing concept/description boundary, not another catalogue or autonomous agent. Input: accepted label, function, mechanism, features, category and nearby known family aliases. Output: core product type; meaningful narrower variant; useful broader family; alternate spellings/language where supported; excluded seller/colour/pack/marketing words; reason and supplied references. Code uses existing aliases/object anchors first; a compact named C `product_query_profile` interpretation handles genuinely ambiguous meanings and is reused for unchanged inputs. Code validates categories/refs and source-independent scope. It must not mechanically strip meaningful words such as child/adult, heated, folding, dimensions or voltage.

Example: “expandable door hanging pull-up bar” becomes core “pull-up bar”, variant “adjustable doorway pull-up bar” and broader family “home pull-up equipment”. Trends normally receives the common type/variant rather than a seller title. Supplier searches add India manufacturer/wholesaler/single-piece terms to the type. Amazon/Flipkart use the type/variant and review the actual returns. A freestanding power tower is related context, not automatically the same doorway product.

The current validation builder selects a primary source-appropriate phrase and considers at most three reviewed alternatives, advancing only after actual sparse results or accepted target-specific no-match/insufficient interpretation. That is its implemented behavior, not the desired lifetime exploration limit. Section26 adds separate renewable free exploration: useful alternate wording or independent sources can be considered even after a successful read. Keep the current finite validation sequence and its history compatible. Failed/challenged access and uncertain launches remain on their own recovery path; keywords are not an access workaround. Exact duplicate terms are skipped, broader context is labelled, and different location/options keep their real scope. A meaningful known subtype must survive catalogue fallback; a broader family label must not erase it.

Persist the profile hash, exact term, term position/count, real source/purpose/location/options and previous task/receipt refs explaining an alternative. Planning is free of hidden model/provider calls. Actual source request—not caller product ID—is the acquisition reuse key within the finite run. Each target separately receives exact/relevant-family/unrelated/unclear scope review. Share an acquisition where it genuinely covers multiple products; do not share another product's score or positive verdict. This avoids blind156×source requests while maintaining full per-product audit.

### 22.2 Common adapter and receipt contract

Direct ordinary functions now connect the existing source/capture modules through `src/product_radar/source_coverage.py`. The controller uses existing `query_tasks`, `source_checks` and `batch_runs`; it introduces no separate queue framework or paid-Actor fiction. Each prepared request declares actual list/family scope, country/location and public query/URLs. Physical acquisition identity uses the real request and finite run, while every target keeps its own current description hash and interpretation. Preparation creates no attempted-search claim.

| Actual interface | Action and downstream owner |
|---|---|
| `product_source_request_builder` | Read accepted C/catalogue/short-type profiles and actual current-run target outcomes; advance up to three genuinely different phrases only after saved sparse/unrelated results. Share Amazon department requests, freeze real supplier URLs and require real QC location. Missing input is a precise preparation gap, not an attempt. |
| `prepare_source_tasks` / `prepare_all_source_tasks` | Freeze finite public questions and join targets; record all current products against 14 agreed routes, including explicit applicability and unresolved inputs. No collection occurs here. |
| `installed_source_handlers` / `route_registry` | Compose actual marketplace, supplier, Meta and Trends handlers; accept the supplied funded QC client and direct-first QC factory when installed. Missing functions remain Not connected. Constructing the registry reads no secret, balance, browser or provider. |
| `execute_source_run` | Dispatch each due question once per pass. Only a proved request/navigation starts an acquisition attempt. Save/import the actual outcome before moving on; isolate route failures and keep healthy siblings working. |
| `continue_source_run` | Re-prepare newly discovered company-page dependencies and advance presently actionable work without a filled-report stop. A fixed point returns exact waiting/reconciliation/interpretation work to the next service stage; it is not completion or a programme handback. |
| `bind_source_relevance` | Attach already validated target-specific E/F/source-scope interpretation to actual saved references. Do not recollect, copy another target's judgment or fabricate an extra market site. |
| `retry_source_prelaunch_task` / `resume_source_route` | Reopen only a saved, supported local failure after an actual repair, or clear an explicit route wait after actual recovery. Retain failed receipts and original jobs; never reset an actual-request budget or assume an uncertain launch did not occur. |
| `source_coverage` / `source_run_progress` | Read genuine per-product outcomes and cheap canonical task counts. A 156 × 14 grid has 2,184 questions, not 2,184 completed searches. A caller may supply its verified snapshot for progress instead of another expensive evidence join. |
| `scripts/run_source_coverage.py` | Default read-only view. `--prepare --execute --continue --run-id <existing finite programme>` advances the authorised source stage and prints each result. Optional funded QC fallback uses existing private configuration/credits only; it cannot purchase or upgrade. |

The receipt envelope has route, acquisition ID, original observation date, immutable reference, capture/observation/evidence IDs, exact reason and separately scoped target results. `dispatch_started=true` means actual execution; `false` means proved no request; `null` means uncertain native execution. An uncertain receipt cannot become a completed check or a blind replacement request. Its later verified stage creates a new immutable controller receipt for the same acquisition, preserving the first uncertainty. `tries` counts only confirmed acquisition attempts; local pre-dispatch failures have separate finite counters and new acquisition generations, so an actual local-lock repair does not consume or collide with a provider request.

A local intent-save admission conflict cannot start a source request: keep that task prepared, record the precise local pending reason and let another healthy task run. Retain a returned public reply in a hash-addressed local stage before the canonical commit. If outcome/error-note saves are temporarily busy, the existing intent and original source receipt remain available; saved-only reconciliation applies that same reply without a second request. Canonical import/reference checks still apply, and the staged file is not a completed-product claim. A pending pass-summary keeps its receipt-backed route pauses even when new dependencies are prepared; a later applied pass or explicit actual recovery supersedes that derived stage. Data-integrity problems still stop the affected commit instead of silently accepting corrupt research.

All five supplier handlers expose the existing controller's separate `reconcile` function. This path verifies the exact original acquisition/query, current target-description binding, original parser contract, receipt dates and file hashes before projecting supplier effort; it cannot call HTTP, Chrome or a company-page fetcher. A completed saved capture or valid partial progress can settle the same acquisition, including a real blocked or unreadable result. It cannot adopt a later parser-version file, manufacture a missing page or turn a challenge into an empty supplier search. Company-page replay verifies the original frozen destinations/pass and all claimed bundles before writes. If a page was downloaded but the later local effort record failed, retain its actual receipt in the pass and require local reconciliation; do not download it again. Exact replay is idempotent. Missing, undated, corrupt or foreign proof remains named pending work while healthy tasks continue. The focused169-case local verification includes27 saved-only recovery cases; actual current Google-task reconciliation is a separate live check.

Save intent before navigation/read; record whether execution began, navigation/response/export stage, requested/final URL, original observation time, HTTP/challenge state, location, bounded original bytes/CSV/screenshot where useful, completeness/omitted counts and content hash. Return completed/partial/failed/waiting with exact reason, not a general exception that ends the programme. A challenge with HTTP200 is failed access. A legitimate empty page requires recognised search/list structure plus its empty-result marker; no cards on unrecognised markup is parser uncertainty. Reparse saved bytes after extractor repair before recollecting.

Parsed rows carry original source IDs, titles, canonical URLs, explicit returned/list positions, quote/pack conditions and real image URLs, with raw row refs. Import direct acquisition records without paid Actor/charge fiction. Under the canonical writer, validate immutable capture/hash/source scope, apply target relevance, commit once, then update question/outcome feedback. Existing paid-only query/feedback/diagnostic readers now skip the explicit all-source task/check kind rather than incorrectly indexing paid-Actor fields. Complete remaining service/model/report consumers through their actual seams, not by pretending another route is Meta.

Local controller proof: 79 focused cases passed. They cover shared family acquisition, the 156 × 14 denominator, no phantom source-wide checks, partial/empty outcomes, imported evidence binding, timeout/healthy siblings, finite retries, persisted route pauses, same-acquisition recovery, saved-only funded-Q import, company-page continuation, fixed points, snapshot reuse, three-phrase outcome-driven refinement, per-product malformed-information isolation, local intent/outcome/progress-save contention and exact acquisition reuse into separately bound follow-on targets. Supplied verified snapshots cause no progress-store reread; a 300-row skipped/prepared-task fixture makes at most three snapshots and no external request. Explicit per-target supersession corrects the unsupported Home and Decor list scope without rewriting its original attempt or receipt. Target-specific staged receipt pointers cannot substitute another product's Trends/supplier import. These tests do not establish live all-product source completion, a recurring schedule or client publication. Actual 8 October native marketplace receipts separately show a 30-row kitchen Bestsellers page, a scope-recognised empty kitchen Movers page and two 30-row partially complete generic searches; source relevance remains a separate step.

Saved-reply reconciliation and known dispatch recovery are selected before due route probes, fresh source reads and retries. A source-local pause suppresses new navigation, not verified saved-only reconciliation or a locally staged reply. Exact local Trends receipt/hash/time-validation failures remain failed proof checks with the original intent and reconciliation requirement, not a claim that Google blocked a valid read. A verified Google 429 receipt is an actual blocked access result; neither case means an empty market. Five added controller checks cover this ordering and classification without an external request.

Verified Google Trends refusals and the four supplier-directory routes can carry an explicit route-wide access failure only when the exact original requested/final host, navigation/response stage and overall blocked outcome support it. A proved429 permits one delayed probe;403/401 or a recognised challenge does not cause automatic repeat requests. Saved reconciliation and healthy sources remain usable while the affected route waits. Successful permitted fallback, unrecognised markup, local proof/clock/hash failure, uncertain dispatch or a foreign receiver cannot donate route-wide blocking proof. A company-page refusal is scoped to that company, not every supplier website. The original receipt bytes/dates are preserved; the route-scope classification is a separately verified projection, and already applied historical question-local receipts are not silently rewritten. The329 focused access/controller/supplier/Trends checks include23 new access cases; the23-case file also passes independently. These are local checks, not proof that a blocked provider has recovered.

Trends wrappers and children retain timezone-aware microsecond timestamps for new acquisitions. For an older native receipt whose wrapper saved only whole seconds, replay permits its independently hash-verified observation only within the recorded final one-second completion interval. It cannot extend other dates, replace the original clock, claim a chart from a failed read or rerun the request. Actual page/CSV observation time and pre-observation failure completion time remain distinct. The 209 focused Trends/controller/neighbor checks prove local precision, blocked-result bridge and original-receipt recovery; live chart collection remains a separate measured outcome.

### 22.3 Route-specific implementation contracts

Every row needs capture/parse/replay fixtures, a real bounded ordinary-access check from the intended operating environment, relevant target review and a connected dispatch test. Safe no-result or blocked proof counts as an observed outcome, not successful extraction. Source-local access failures never become invented per-product attempts.

| Route and owning seam | Capture/parsing/scope | Failure, fallback and acceptance |
|---|---|---|

### 22.4 Full practical assessment and coverage reconciliation

Reconcile all156 product IDs against current descriptions, jobs, questions, route captures and actual attempts. Three established exclusions keep their reasons; all153 other products get a completed practical current ten-row assessment or an exact genuine unresolved outcome after attempted review. Finish time/audience placeholders and older unknowns from ordinary described features wherever reasonable. The process must not substitute “not assessed” for “cannot reasonably judge after this actual attempt.” If a material feature still cannot be inferred, save the specific question, checked sources, failure/no-result distinction and next action.

Fix reference-contract errors at their owning prompt/context/validator binding, then recover the exact saved output where valid or run a bounded current full assessment. Do not demand extra searches for an output-schema problem. All six failing paths have actual prior jobs: inspect those failures rather than describing four as though assessment was never attempted. Reuse unchanged valid A/B/source/F work. Preserve original answers and historical packets; a current correction creates a versioned superseding judgment, never an edited old response.

Produce a per-product/per-route coverage view with applicability, exact question/term/profile, acquisition/attempt, completed/no-match/partial/blocked/not-tried state, interpretation, practical source support, dates and next trigger. Counters include all156 product rows,153 assessment targets, actual route questions, physical acquisitions and distinct relevant bindings. Shared acquisition counts once financially and separately by target coverage. Before claiming completion, reconcile the counts from canonical events rather than copying source-wide capability messages onto all products.
