## 24. N03 current source-support and N25 record-recovery contracts

### N24: unattended private staging without publication rights

The programme must save completed internal drafts to the existing private bucket without depending on an owner's expiring browser/CLI login. Reuse `stage_private_draft` and the existing immutable object transport; a validated first-two-issue bilingual packet has five files and a chosen-language packet has four. Derive the exact required file set from that validated packet; do not reject a correct four-file packet or add a second report store or grant the research process release rights. A separate Cloudflare Access self-hosted application protects only `dropshipper.neuraflow.co.in/_automation/drafts`. Its single Service Auth policy names one service token, not every valid token or an email domain. The existing owner application and Google sign-in remain unchanged.

The direct Worker verifies the automation application's trusted `ctx.access.aud` and the authenticated service-token identity before storage access. If the provider's identity lookup is unavailable or returns no identity, verify its signed Access assertion against the configured team's public keys, exact issuer and machine audience, valid times, application type, empty service subject and exact signed Client ID. Audience may be one exact string or a bounded array containing that exact value, as permitted by RFC7519§4.1.3; do not reject a valid single-audience token or accept a different audience. An explicit wrong, malformed or non-service identity cannot use that fallback. A header decoded without signature verification is never identity. Fetch public signing keys from only the configured HTTPS Cloudflare Access team, with a bounded request/body, a small warm cache and bounded rotation refresh; no request field selects another key server. Fixed rejection labels may identify which check failed but never expose claim values. The machine route supports only release-index GET and the five possible fixed issue/revision object GET/immutable PUT names for the configured client. POST, DELETE, publication approval, release-index mutation, arbitrary bucket keys and owner/client pages are refused. Store the service credentials only in ignored `config/local/`; never print them or put them in reports, run receipts, ordinary command arguments or model inputs.

The separate programme `private_staging` stage takes only a locally validated complete, unapproved packet. It saves the package hash and exact verified file outcomes; the manifest is uploaded last. Exact successful packets are reused without uploading again. A possibly completed PUT is reconciled by exact GET, not blindly retried. Missing credentials, failed files or an inaccessible portal defer only staging; local spreadsheets/PDFs and independent research remain usable. Staging cannot change approval, publication ordinal or client availability. Owner download-page verification stays a separate named-owner acceptance check.

Acceptance covers missing/wrong service identity and audience, forged headers, forbidden methods/keys/index writes, immutable conflicts, exact response hashes, credential reflection/redirection/deadline handling, successful replay without another write, a changed revision reopening its own staging, a staging outage with another healthy report proceeding, and live machine upload/readback with an unchanged release index. This is functional private delivery, not client release or visual redesign.

### N03: matching and support are one bounded interpretation

Source-E v3 is explicitly `research-e-source-support-v3`, selected by `relevance_style="short-references-support-v3"`. It returns relevance for every supplied alias and one `source_support` row per actual supplied platform, with `reasonably_positive`, `neutral`, `weak` or `unclear`, observed/inferred/not-stated basis, same-platform relevant references and a concrete reason. It receives original captured source facts and dates, not just titles. There is no desired checklist score, second checker model or all-history model input.

`source_interpretation.review_source_results` saves the original input, original evidence hashes and versioned opinion in the existing `market_reviews` table. `market_validation.current_source_support` checks the current product description, exact original source bytes' record hashes, latest opinion and seven-day freshness before `discovery.compute_market_signals` adds its meaning to current site prioritisation. Neutral/weak/unclear still permit two usable related sites; the opinion does not change original numerical market figures. A missing number is not zero. An all-zero or missing Trends chart cannot be projected as accepted positive demand. Report facts retain interpretation basis and original references; inferred support is labelled estimated, and its revision binds the selected packet.

The current policy is `opportunity-priority-v2`, with seven possible distinct sites: Meta, Amazon, Flipkart, Google Trends, Blinkit, Zepto and Instamart. All Amazon routes count as one site; suppliers are not consumer-market sources. Priorities are six-or-more fit matches plus two-or-more usable related sites, five plus two-or-more, six-or-more plus one reasonably positive site, and five plus one reasonably positive site, in that order. Two-site ranking has no positive-growth prerequisite. Mere inventory, a price or a photograph is not automatically positive support for the one-site branch.

A fit-passing current-ten product with one usable unsupported site may receive a named saved-evidence interpretation without a new collection. Its complete current source scope is hash-bound; the actual supplied bounded slice and omitted count are explicit. An accepted neutral, weak or unclear opinion completes that scope and is not retried toward a desired positive. Changed meaningful input can create a new scope. One finite shape/reference repair uses the same facts. Failures defer only dependent work. Historical source-E v2, legacy requests, old measured group rules, frozen report packets and publication history retain their declared versions and are never rewritten.

The supplier helper's optional `concept_ids` confines a programme's finite F slice to its frozen product list. Exact captured page/quotation rules and actual dispatch/fulfilment unknowns remain unchanged. Current reuse and route binding verify the immutable F job's model/prompt version, exact input hash and original page dates; a same-input older F-v2 result is not silently reused as current F-v3 meaning. Its original record/answer remains intact while one current interpretation uses the same saved pages; replay then makes no new model call. The read-only saved-capture loader validates failed bundles without asking F to interpret them. A usable reviewed sibling can confirm a lead even if another page failed; without a usable lead, failed/unclear pages settle the company pass as insufficient, not an endless interpretation wait. All reviewed unrelated destinations settle the bounded search as completed without a matching result. A search with omitted or unreadable destinations is insufficient, never proof that supply is absent. Its exact frozen company dependency, original search references, current description and original dates remain bound; unattempted or other-run company work cannot close it. A corrupt capture is not accepted as either a usable lead or a verified failed-page result.

Practical fit binds the canonical product description and original associated descriptive observations, not every later price, rank, campaign activity, source opinion or photograph. Same-meaning fresh observations retain their original checklist references. The programme reuses an unchanged accepted practical-v2 or description-v3 fit with no new model job. Changed mechanism, changed descriptive evidence or a new accepted identity association requires the scoped fit review; this rule is not weakened merely to improve yield. Saved market/supplier interpretation alone does not reassess all ten points.

### N25: exact local record recovery and lightweight saved-record audit

`operational_backup` snapshots one verified immutable research-event prefix and the exact financial journal under its short lock. Their checkpoint dates are separate, not a fictitious cross-store transaction. It copies only canonical events, the journal and explicitly selected original source/model/report/image files into one named immutable backup. File hashes, original event-chain hashes, state hashes and record counts are checked before a ready manifest is written. Directory ancestry is checked once per owned directory set; final files remain descriptor-checked. Restore requires an empty isolated destination and verifies canonical replay before producing a proof. Existing working data is not replaced. A failed folder is kept without a ready manifest; independent work continues and recovery uses a new named backup, not deletion or overwrite.

`operational_audit` reads saved accounting, model receipts and explicitly supplied direct-capture receipts. It separates measured charges, unsettled holds, recorded tokens, unknown token/time rows, actual dispatch, pre-dispatch failure and uncertain dispatch. Reused receipts are not new calls. Duration sums are recorded computer effort, not elapsed programme time when work overlaps. Collection totals are not invented as one report's exclusive cost, and AI/hosting/other provider invoices are not guessed.

No additional database, provider poll, browser, model call or monitoring service is introduced. The owner-selected destination is this project's `data/backups`, configured in `config/operations/backup.json`. This is a second local copy, not protection against whole-drive loss. The local restore does not prove a permanent independent destination, full automatic original-path relocation, all unselected artifacts, hosted recovery or unattended backup recurrence. These limitations do not block research or demand a cloud-storage choice. The [operator guide](offline_runner_guide.md#saved-source-interpretation-and-record-recovery) owns commands; the [benchmark](benchmark_record.md#10-current-source-support-and-record-recovery-proof) owns measured acceptance. Read-only verification of a saved checkpoint is a separate operation from a full new copy/restore/audit rehearsal; their timings must not be compared as the same workload.

#### Automatic essential selection and daily second copy

Extend the existing backup helper, not the client-report storage path or a second task. `select_essential_backup_files(research_state, financial_state, *, root, pending_controls)` receives the same verified state that establishes the event prefix. It returns deduplicated typed file references, expected hashes where available, unresolved references and a selection fingerprint. Do not select from a later snapshot, traverse arbitrary paths supplied by source text or copy entire private folders.

| Essential group | Exact selection and boundary |
|---|---|
| Captured source work | Follow canonical captures, their owned query/receipt/artifact references, task acquisition/receipt references and saved returned-receipt pointers. Preserve original dates, failures and recovery files. |
| Model work | Follow canonical job IDs to original and actual named retry intents, execution/result/completion/failure receipts and accepted repair references. Keep unfinished sent ownership; no login/profile files. |
| Suppliers, Trends and catalogue | Follow original supplier pass/receipt bundles, original Trends scopes/CSV, prepared branch manifests/materials and accepted addition sources. Unresolved reference is a named backup limitation, not a new provider request. |
| Photos and reports | Follow source-bound image paths/hashes and exact current or retained frozen report revisions, delivery manifests, writing inputs and staging/release proofs. Include immutable original report bytes rather than regenerating them. |
| Controls and application | Include valid programme controls, explicit application code/dependencies/report assets/documentation and narrowly validated public settings. Default credentials, cookies, browser profiles, `.env`, installed dependency folders and unrelated files remain excluded. |
| Paid recovery identity | Export only a schema-validated nonsecret original account/generation projection when needed. Never copy the private credential registry or the whole `config/local` directory. |

`create_essential_operational_backup` establishes the verified record prefix and financial checkpoint once, selects from those same inputs and invokes the existing exact-byte copy/verification mechanism. Preserve historical v1 manifests/readers; code/public-settings additions use a narrowly typed versioned extension, not removal of the privacy exclusions. Selection must distinguish absent optional files, unresolved required references and unsafe paths; none permits a claim of complete operational recovery.

`run_due_operational_backup` uses one hash-bound India-day control across programmes at `data/operations/backup_controls`. Missing or disabled configuration returns before any snapshot or copying. Each due copy is `data/backups/daily-YYYY-MM-DD`; one full attempt per day includes96 fifteen-minute ticks. A failed or interrupted day's intent and the last healthy backup are preserved, rather than copying again on every tick. An interrupted control save may recover the exact original ready-manifest receipt only after its version, project root and hash binding are checked; that cheap recovery does not reread the whole copied payload or restamp the original verification. A missing ready manifest remains a named incomplete attempt until a later day. Missed days create no invented historical snapshots. The same optional callback runs at saved cycle/final boundaries and the execute-only saved-retry-wait path, under the existing programme leader. Owner pause and the default read-only status command start no housekeeping. Diagnostics stay outside research progress signatures; backup failures cannot reset funds or stop independent research.

The chosen implementation is local only: no cloud transport, new bucket, sign-in key, second scheduled task or paid plan. Do not broaden the draft-only key or use client-download storage for backups. Preserve the existing512MiB logical-backup,64MiB/file and100,000-file bounds; measure selected size and report capacity problems without deleting a healthy backup. Whole-drive-independent backup can be considered separately if the owner later requests it. One daily exact-copy verification is not a daily restore rehearsal.

Acceptance covers every essential reference type, exact checkpoint selection, private/unrelated/recursive-backup exclusion, deduplication, missing/disabled configuration with zero actions,96 same-day checks across programmes with one copy, continuous-cycle and retry-wait hooks, unchanged research signatures/funds/history, interrupted ready-receipt reuse, corrupt/incomplete/foreign readiness rejection and historical v1 compatibility. Original events, source captures and report bytes are preserved, with missing or changed attachments recorded rather than invented. Record-copy verification is separate from application resumption on another computer. Existing readers expect original paths: preserve those records and prove an empty original-layout restore or explicit relocated-reader compatibility separately. Credentials require reconnection and are never included in this backup.

## 25. N22 connected programme and native schedule

`src/product_radar/programme.py` joins the existing functions and stores; it does not introduce a broker, second research database or model. `scripts/run_programme.py` is read-only by default. Preparing a programme freezes the actual current product IDs, source-run identity, India research week, client and three release dates without collecting anything (quick-commerce reference locations come from `config/research/quickcommerce_locations.json` since Plan R10, not from the frozen programme). For the present whole-pool work retain **`scope156-nonvisual-20261008`** as the source run; do not replace it to make stalled acquisitions look new.

| Existing connected step | Exact execution and continuation |
|---|---|
| Profiles | Verify current C profile bindings once per saved state; recover or prepare only missing current products through `ensure_query_profiles`. A malformed product gets its own repair reason; healthy products continue. |
| Practical fit | Build one evidence index. Before ordinary reuse of an unchanged accepted v2/v3 assessment, honour any explicitly prepared, unapplied exact-current partial v2/v3 review in this frozen product list. It must bind the same current parent, original input, brand and named original criterion IDs; stale/changed-parent bases cannot silently rebase. Execute only those named questions through `run_fit_reviews`, preserving the other answers, relevance and compliance fields. Original sent/pending jobs retain exact ownership and finite correction rules; accepted negative/Unknown answers are final for that unchanged scope, not retried toward a preferred score. Then reuse remaining unchanged accepted v2/v3 judgments. Before upgrading a missing current answer, replay any actually sent exact-current v2 original/correction using its original job ownership with no live runner; uncertain dispatch remains named pending work. An abandoned preparation does not block, and a genuinely completed rejection may enter v3 once. Select only the next finite missing group through `prepare_practical_fit_reviews(..., contract_version=DESCRIPTION_CONTRACT)`/`run_fit_reviews`. Named and missing reviews share the existing fit limit. Description-grounded estimates bind the actual chosen fields; they are not outside-source confirmations. A brand exclusion is separate from a failed benefit judgment. |
| Source acquisition | Call `continue_source_run` with the original run, frozen products, current family builder and the installed route handlers (section 30). Reconcile saved replies before new reads; repeat preparation after discovered company-page dependencies. The default12-handler-callback allowance is shared across the original and all follow-on pools for one uninterrupted acquisition-stage pass, not12 per pool,12 products or a report-size stop. Saved reconciliation consumes a callback slot but is not a new source request. |
| Target matching/support | Use `review_source_results` on actual saved source questions. Bind a waiting target to its exact supplied input; skip unchanged not-yet-due failures but permit changed meaningful evidence. Completed unrelated results can permit the next reviewed phrase; pending interpretation cannot. |
| Supplier interpretation | Call `review_supplier_results` with the frozen product list and exact deferred capture IDs. Preserve original page captures and F jobs; unrelated historical supplier inputs cannot consume this programme's slice. Supplier failure/absence remains a report limitation, not a required successful lead. |
| Original photos | Read actual relevant image candidates from the shared report facts, prefer exact-product relationships and try up to three original candidates. Preserve actual image receipts; a failed download is not a missing platform check and does not invent an illustration. Existing current images are reused. |
| Internal reports | Build each missing ready issue while other research continues. Allocate distinct dated issues using real history/reservations; reserve through the existing hold gate before writing. Recover the original frozen input after interruption and reuse exact completed files. Read this issue's selected inputs/history/holds/language ordinal from one coherent stage snapshot; a genuine selected change can append an immutable replacement while unrelated work remains productive. Unrelated discoveries do not rerender it, and global research quiet is never required. Keep old bytes intact. Protected uploaded/published/cancelled issues are not replaced, and an unexplained reservation change retains its named wait. No approval, publication, outreach or paid upgrade occurs here. |
| Private draft staging | Only complete current unapproved packets enter `private_staging_callback` with the scoped machine-only service. Verify the original local files, the exact manifest-derived full remote set and unchanged release index: five files for the first-two bilingual issues, four for a valid later chosen-language issue. The upstream packet validator owns the language contract; the programme does not invent another file-count gate. Save metadata/hashes, never credentials or raw file bodies, in the programme. Missing credentials, unreadable local packets and upload/readback failures defer only their packet; other research and valid packets continue. Unchanged verified packets skip upload; changed package/revision reopens its own binding. No owner login, approval, index write or client publication is performed. |

`advance_programme` repeats dependency-ready rounds, rather than finishing after one milestone. Meaningful progress is a change in saved product inputs, accepted judgments, source requests/outcomes, observations, supplier interpretations, image evidence, owned jobs, actual catalogue additions, a current programme's prepared/visited seed-branch inputs, or draft bindings. An exact local family merge or a recorded failed seed input can therefore yield the next healthy unvisited branch without requiring a new model job. Clock/diagnostic writes alone do not keep it running. Its optional execution slice preserves all remaining work; a fixed point means work is genuinely waiting, not that the project is complete. Per-target/lane deferrals carry exact input binding, reason, original date and next retry. The technical retry interval is configurable; it is not a product inclusion condition. A global model-account wait suppresses new model launches while saved interpretation and independent public-source work continue.

One kernel-backed `programme_writer` guard owns the outer execution; no financial lock is held while browsing or analysing. The same `ResearchMemory` instance and verified warm cache are reused. A finite source pass refreshes its verified state after an actual returned/failed action, not for every skipped/paused/queued row; supplied verified progress/coverage snapshots are reused without another store replay. Commit/receipt validators still own their current predecessor/hash verification. Each phase checkpoint is staged locally before its short existing `batch_runs` commit. A temporary busy canonical save keeps a hash-bound predecessor/checkpoint for exact recovery. That checkpoint does not certify lower-layer research, source launches or costs: their original receipts/intents still own proof. Conflicting or corrupt authoritative state is not silently overwritten. Observer/output errors do not own the research state.

AI executable selection is shared by the research runner and connection checker through `resolve_codex_executable`. This project's public configuration pins the verified existing Codex0.160.1 absolute path, so a scheduled Windows/WSL start cannot accidentally choose an older incompatible PATH entry. Explicit invalid paths do not silently fall back; older configurations without a pin remain compatible. No installation, account/model/isolation change or new saved-job identity follows from this selection. Preserve original failures, reconcile exact execution/owned-process state and deliberately prepare only an eligible failed request; automatic research keeps the original programme and source IDs. Ninety focused offline runner/recovery checks and native help checks confirm selection/options, not complete unattended research.

The small owner switch is `programme_service_state`/`set_programme_service_state`, stored as a hash-checked local control file. `--pause` stops the next bounded stage and is also checked immediately before model launch; it never kills a dispatched request. Its successful in-flight results remain. `--resume` permits the same saved programme to continue and grants no fresh source/model/spending allowance. A paused automatic tick creates no new research week. This is owner control, not a supplier/source failure or automatic cancellation policy.

The Windows task is generated by `programme_schedule.native_task_xml` and `scripts/setup_programme_task.ps1`. It uses the existing WSL distribution, repository and virtual-environment Python, the logged-in owner's least-privilege identity, a15-minute continuation check and login/missed-start recovery. `IgnoreNew` plus the programme guard prevents overlapping starts. It neither wakes the computer nor deliberately terminates a running request. Definition generation does not install or run it; an existing different task is never overwritten. Namespace-aware comparison checks the owner/action, enabled triggers, repetition and meaningful settings, not merely the command. Compare equivalent owner names/SIDs after Windows resolves them, and compare trigger meaning rather than XML field order; wrong/duplicate fields, changed owner/command/interval or engine settings remain invalid. Native registration is create-only, never create-or-update. The one `NeuraFlow-Dropshipper-Research` task is installed/enabled; repeat-install left it unchanged and a timer check finished with result0 without a second observed writer. Whole-pool scheduled recovery remains under acceptance. Research still needs the computer on and the actual model/browser session. Uploaded approved release dates are independent server metadata: Tuesday, Thursday and Saturday at09:00 India time.

`catalogue_discovery.create_catalogue_discovery_step` is the default execution callback. It runs renewable heading waves that read only the free Meta handler (Amazon and Flipkart are the crowding check, not discovery), then wider discovery and the product-map lane; the dropshipping platforms run as their own lane (section 30). It saves original scoped receipts and uses the existing A/B identity admission. Actual acquired cards, descriptions, known identities and genuinely new identities remain distinct counts. Constructor/preparation makes no external request. It does not launch a paid Actor or silently expand the original156 pool. Its discovery choices and actions per round are configurable work slices, not catalogue size or a product inclusion rule.

The Amazon-page seed lane (repository inventory, staged seed manifest and `catalogue-discovery-seed-cycle-v1` branch turns) is retired (Plan R10 W10, D4); its 224 headings stay in `config/research/catalogue.json`. The product map now grows through `product_map.map_step`: local staging of platform, Trends, advertiser and supplier-listing material (`map_materials.py`), one seed C call a round for the emptiest sub-area with waiting material, and at most one `catalogue_expansion.run_expansion` renewal call a round, all inside the round's ordinary AI allowance. See the [catalogue document](../../catalogue_discovery_and_coverage.md).

Every newly admitted identity receives an explicit immutable follow-on pool/source-run ID inside the same programme. Profiles, practical fit, all applicable14 routes, target matching, supplier interpretation and photos continue for that pool. Original programme/source IDs and original156 membership never change. Exact same-family requests may replay acquisitions from explicitly related source runs or the same discovery cycle; saved-only import re-verifies original files and binds the new target. It cannot copy another product's relevance verdict, fabricate a new request, reuse unrelated request scopes or call the live collector if a saved-only reconciler is missing. One shared report pool can include the original and these explicit new products; progress shows both denominators separately.

Source scheduling reserves half the pass's allowance for the original pool and shares the remainder with follow-on pools in a persisted finite rotation. Unused original quota passes to the children; children with no ready work do not consume callback slots or hide the next ready child. Each child is considered at most once per uninterrupted pass, and unused child quota can return to an original pool that filled its first quota. A one-slot allowance alternates original and follow-on work. Additive admission ordinals and a monotonic sweep counter retain restart-safe rotation; new children join a later cohort rather than perpetually pushing earlier children back. Cursor/sweep changes are bookkeeping, not useful research progress. A missing local callback return reserves that callback's whole allotted quota instead of claiming zero dispatch. Counts separately report callbacks, newly recorded owned acquisition attempts and callbacks without a fresh dispatch; an original receipt's `dispatch_started=true` does not make saved reconciliation or shared reuse a new request.

This callback slice is a time/fairness control, not another spending allowance. A restart preserves completed tasks, exact acquisition IDs, original receipts and actual cost history, then starts a new finite callback slice. The same unfinished labelled programme round can therefore contain more than12 total callbacks across interruption and restart; both slices remain actual recorded work, without duplicating a completed request or resetting funds. Do not present current-pass counters as a durable whole-round or monthly request cap. Existing financial limits remain authoritative. Callback deadlines bound acquisition work, with separately bounded owned cleanup; mounted-drive verification/commits and later review/file work are not included in a claimed12 ×45-second total wall-clock guarantee.

The source controller's outer alarm is a dedicated `SourceActionDeadline` cancellation signal, not an ordinary provider `TimeoutError`. Broad transport/provider exception handling must not swallow it and start another fallback after the callback's time slice expires. Installed native/HTTP/browser adapters clean up only their owned work, retain original request/dispatch/cleanup evidence and rethrow the signal to the controller. The controller records `SOURCE_ACTION_TIMEOUT`, preserves the original acquisition and requests saved-result reconciliation; healthy later callbacks continue. Unknown dispatch stays unknown, never the wrapper's default false. Marked cancellation receipts and original native proof are replayed locally without a browser/provider call. Ordinary inner provider timeouts may still use a permitted bounded fallback within the remaining callback time. Cancellation during local saving/import is not permission to erase successful original captures, overwrite partial canonical history or submit a replacement request. This repair's local tests and same-ID live acceptance are distinct evidence.

A successfully completed original native transport followed by an interrupted local extraction/import retains its original query, date, page bytes and receipts. Where the adapter explicitly supports it, saved-only recovery adds a separately named reparse/import capture; it never overwrites the original capture or navigates again. Failed/unknown native execution remains failed/unknown without a fabricated successful cleanup proof. Facebook cancellation similarly uses a separate cancelled-snapshot file, retaining an existing completed snapshot/result/receipt.

For an old terminal supplier task whose actual original dispatch was omitted from its saved attribution, permit one bounded saved-only reconciliation of that same acquisition/generation. The task's `supplier-original-dispatch-recovery-v1` marker must bind its exact original acquisition ID and unchanged request hash. The programme compares it against the pre-callback owner before excluding that original attribution repair from the fresh-request count. A later acquisition, changed request, malformed marker or another task never gets that exemption. Keep the original failure and uncertain owned-process exit evidence; an unsuccessful recovery settles that attempt without entering an automatic repeat loop. Restoring dispatch attribution is one recovery and zero new requests, not a new spending allowance or evidence that the source was usable.

Owner summaries use one verified research snapshot and one short locked financial read. Account status loads the private registry from the project/config root, not the financial-journal directory; both use the same supplied saved financial view. A configured original account is separate from optional multi-account-pool activation or current provider balance. A busy/unreadable optional input is explicitly unknown and cannot hide the other product/source/report counts. Actual source-attempt counts require dispatch proof; prepared intents and local before-request failures can have workflow states without being external attempts. Do not call the difference between Not tried and actual-attempt counts completed or not-needed work.

The connected owner-cost path uses `programme_cost_snapshot.build_owner_cost_snapshot` with that same verified record/research/financial view, the selected public baseline at `config/research/collection_cost_baseline.json` and existing service policy. It opens no receipt or credential and makes no provider/model call. Shared project collection fees include failed physical attempts and keep unresolved read holds separate. The explicitly labelled shared average divides known project spending by actually generated distinct logical issue packets, including a valid retained original, never by language editions, revisions, downloads or proposed allocations. This is a changing shared-research average, not an exclusive per-issue invoice or a predictor of future fresh-discovery cost. The exclusive/all-in bill remains unknown where no bill supports it. Supporting paid capture lineage is a separate narrower subtotal and cannot omit failed-work expense from the shared total.

The public `config/research/collection_cost_baseline.json` pins the named historical156-candidate known-fee reference with its fingerprint. It includes original Meta/Amazon/Trends collection work and failed attempts, but not unresolved fees; it is not a current direct-source/fit10 yield measurement. The existing `yield_forecast` uses explicit5%/3% planning scenarios and the real Tuesday/Thursday/Saturday India-calendar count, not an assumed twelve every month or a promise of funded supply. Invalid/missing baseline or policy data leaves only that optional section unknown. Account warnings distinguish one configured account from three approved nominal accounts; no configuration grants funds. Proposed issue membership is explicitly labelled a current research proposal, separately from actual saved packet membership and current/retained draft status.

Optional recorded model effort is reduced incrementally from its own original intent/execution/result/completion/failure receipts through `operational_effort_cache`. Saved cycle/final checkpoints check a persisted300-second due gate and probe at most25 jobs per due refresh, including bounded initial/retry metadata; unchanged receipt bodies are not reread. A fair cursor reaches earlier/new jobs without rescanning all historical bodies. Hash-bound reduced rows retain original request ownership; receipt metadata detects completion even when the canonical job record does not change. Bad bindings cannot donate tokens or duration. Missing/unprocessed records remain explicit partial coverage, not zero usage; duration sums are recorded computer effort, not elapsed programme time or cash invoices. Default read-only status reads the reduced cache only. A failed cache save is labelled observed-not-saved, not a saved measurement. Owner pause skips refresh; optional measurement failures cannot stop healthy research or alter financial guards. Original receipt bytes remain untouched.

Live execution refreshes time at each stage/scope entry, at an actual failure and at final observation. Source captures completed after a round began are therefore visible to later matching, supplier and report steps in that same round. Failures receive their full retry delay from the failure time, not an earlier round-start time. Explicit historical/test cutoffs remain fixed. Live supplier interpretation selects captures at its starting cutoff, then refreshes only its final saved-result binding after F finishes; it does not backdate F, fetch a page again or add another model call. The company-page and original-search tasks can settle from that same newly accepted answer. An invalid final clock leaves the original answer recoverable without creating a completion claim. These local mechanisms still need verification in the existing live programme.

Weekly selection preserves old backlog and its original funding/recovery records. Prepared, progressing or running work and saved in-flight source intents resume before a different week. Once old work has a genuine saved fixed point, only the latest due Monday programme may be created; its initial product pool is empty, so fresh discovery does not clone the old156 checks. Ready old/new programmes rotate by last serviced time while retaining their retry dates. Missed weeks are not multiplied, finished manually named current-week work does not create a duplicate identity, and no new allowance or provider funds arise from a new week. Owner pause prevents week creation as well as launches. Current-pool completion remains separate from owner approval, language, missing photos, actual discovery completion and publication; each retains named unfinished work.

Later weeks freeze separate `reporting_inventory_concept_ids` for eligible unused products from verified earlier programme scopes. These IDs join reporting only, not new profile/fit/source/supplier/photo scopes. Previously published, held elsewhere, unavailable or untrusted inventory is excluded; selection checks current eligibility/history/holds again, while a hold belonging to this same issue remains resumable. This does not enlarge the original156 pool or copy older opinions into new products. A research-finished programme with private delivery waiting remains eligible for fair retries, without preventing the next genuinely due research week.

The actual Amazon India Home and Decor mapping is `kitchen/1380374031`, not guessed `home`. Future list questions use that verified node. A settled or untried frozen `home` question may be explicitly superseded per target by the new exact-node question; original request/receipt/attempt/cost history stays intact. Unsupported list emptiness cannot establish weak demand or completed current coverage. An uncertain original acquisition is never superseded away from its reconciliation.

### Operator commands and acceptance boundary

Commands below are implementation interfaces, not evidence that the live continuation or native task has run. Start live continuation only after earlier independent writers finish.

The current existing-run repair resumes with the `--execute` command only. The `--prepare` line documents initial creation; do not repeat it, reset the run or replace its source ID during recovery.

```bash
.venv/bin/python scripts/run_programme.py --programme-id scope156-nonvisual-20261008 --source-run-id scope156-nonvisual-20261008 --prepare
.venv/bin/python scripts/run_programme.py --programme-id scope156-nonvisual-20261008
.venv/bin/python scripts/run_programme.py --programme-id scope156-nonvisual-20261008 --execute --live-model --funded-quickcommerce-fallback
.venv/bin/python scripts/run_programme.py --pause
.venv/bin/python scripts/run_programme.py --resume
.venv/bin/python scripts/run_programme.py --tick --execute --live-model --funded-quickcommerce-fallback
```

Model flags refer only to the approved signed-in account/public inputs; funded QC means existing configured credits, not a purchase. Config/authentication/catalogue problems are named per lane and leave healthy lanes running. Read-only progress reports actual original/follow-on product, checklist, profile, source, attempt, photo-record and draft counts, model/setup waits and next triggers, not a fictional whole-project percentage. Terminal observers print short counts/states and stage/model-call starts/returns, never full profile prompts, source bodies, receipts or credentials; complete results remain in their original records. Observer errors never stop work. Raw OS errors use a local-file-failure label; other unexpected programming/contract exceptions use a distinct stage-failure label with their type and affected lane, not a misleading file diagnosis. Local tests do not prove actual whole-pool completion. Remaining N22 acceptance is a connected full-pool pass, full scheduled recovery, actual post-fill/fresh-discovery/source continuation and recurring financial/release-history rehearsal. Task activation and machine staging have separate actual receipts; no client publication or claimed future yield follows from local test success.

Owned Python files also compile. This includes fixed-point weekly fairness, retained old retry states, no cloned156-product scope, no duplicate week, saved in-flight protection, global owner pause, actual installed marketplace discovery/import/admission fixtures, productive local-seed/failure continuation, exact manifest injection, explicit follow-on bindings, cached receipt replay without copied verdicts, native XML comparison, compact observer output, unused reporting-only inventory and manifest-derived four/five-file private staging with strict identity/type/hash checks. Actual Windows task installation, preserved repeat-install and a completed timer check have their separate [activation receipt](../../../../archive/2026-10-13-before-fresh-start/data/operations/programme-task-activation-20261008.json); whole-pool scheduled recovery and the current live result remain outstanding. No provider/model request or native-task activation was made by the local regression itself.

Narrow suites overlap this total and are not added again. This verifies the frozen code offline, not a new native reload, actual automatic backup, whole-pool completion or client publication; [benchmark section8](benchmark_record.md#8-preserved-p2-test-proof-and-current-remaining-work) retains that distinction.

## 26. Narrow-type recommendations and sustainable research for 2,000 reports

### 26.1 Outcome, precedence and honest scale

The recommendation is a **narrow functional kind of product**, independent of one manufacturer, with actual products, sellers and supplier offers underneath it. For example, recommend an “Adjustable doorway pull-up bar,” define its mounting/mechanism, and show actual matching offers. “Fitness equipment” is too broad; a seller's full promotional title is too specific. Do not combine features from separate listings to invent a narrow type no observed product actually implements. Narrowing must help the client's decision, not manufacture novelty by splitting colours, sellers or trivial variants.

This section is the proposed implementation delta from the 9 October inspection. It refines N04/N19–N25 and existing P-blocks; it is not a second application, scope denominator or runtime acceptance claim. It supersedes any older rule that universally suppresses similar free searches, treats another source only as a fallback, caps all future discovery at one four-action cycle, or treats 500 report versions as sufficient long-term storage. Section26.14 fixes the exact lifecycle, turn accounting, dependency and activation contracts for this design; older section25 describes the currently implemented restartable slices, not those planned durable counters. Historical jobs, captures, policies and frozen packets keep their original contracts. The active ten criteria, four priorities, minimum ten, ≥80% New, supplier limitation, languages, cadence, privacy and actual authority boundaries remain unchanged.

| Planning relationship | Arithmetic and practical meaning |
|---|---|
| Longevity target | 2,000 **logical issues**, not 2,000 PDFs, translations, revisions or downloads. This is a design target, not a promise that today's catalogue contains enough suitable unseen types. |
| Normal issue size | 2,000 × 10–15 = 20,000–30,000 recommendation appearances. Optional twenty-entry issues increase the requirement proportionally. |
| Minimum New if every allowed useful update exists | 80% of those appearances = 16,000–24,000 distinct types first shown to this client. Without useful updates, 20,000–30,000 distinct New types are needed. |
| Time horizon | At the owner's 12–15 issues/month planning range, 2,000 / 15 to 2,000 / 12 = approximately133–167 months, or11.1–13.9 years. Actual releases still follow the India calendar, usually12–14/month; fifteen is a scenario, not a schedule change. |
| Lower-yield workload example | Ten all-New entries at5% useful New yield need200 distinct screened candidates per issue; over2,000 issues,400,000 candidates. At3%, rounding per issue gives334 × 2,000 =668,000. Raw cards/searches are not those candidates. |
| Fifteen-entry sensitivity | With no updates,5% gives300 candidates/issue and600,000 over2,000 issues;3% gives500/issue and1,000,000. Carryover reduces additional discovery only while that real eligible unreserved inventory remains. |

Similar free searches help explore the market; they cannot guarantee the market contains16,000 useful distinct types within these four categories. Measure real replenishment and warn of sustained inventory pressure while continuing useful work. Do not silently change novelty, split trivial variants, promise an exact reserve or add a guarantee gate. Retain extensive upfront source-grounded breadth, but renew it throughout operation rather than claim that a fixed six-month list is a lifetime inventory.

### 26.2 Verified starting point and required corrections

| Inspected owner | Current behavior | Required change and reason |
|---|---|---|
| `research_ai.INSTRUCTIONS['B']`, `research_flow.run_flow` | The existing `concept_id` already means recommendation-level functional type. Many source observations can belong to it. Its initial description/title comes from the first accepted child. | Strengthen that existing identity with a manufacturer-independent definition/title and explicit examples. Do not assume all156 concepts are seller SKUs or create a competing type registry. |
| `catalogue`, `catalogue_expansion`, `product_queries` | Branch/family headings, accepted aliases and per-concept core/subtype/source terms exist. Family parents are branches, not arbitrary other families. Family fallback can replace a known subtype with a broader label. | Keep research headings separate from recommendation identity; preserve meaningful subtype anchors. Use existing nested branches for organisation, not an unimplemented family-parent schema. |
| `catalogue_discovery` | One immutable cycle per programme; default four **total selected actions**, with at most two external callbacks per round. A primary success, empty result or pending interpretation can suppress sibling sources. Recent family+wording suppression omits source identity. | Add renewable numbered waves and independent source obligations; distinguish per-round work from lifetime research. Recent capture matching must use complete actual request scope, not only wording/department. |
| `research_planner`, `research_operations.plan_research` | Paid Meta planner has family-level repeat cooldown and a bounded paid alternative. It is not the driver for every free route. | Keep funded launch controls; scope paid repeat suppression to its charged route/request class. Join its questions to the same subject view without pretending710 catalogue choices execute all sources. |
| `source_coverage`, source adapters | Fourteen-route questions, exact shared acquisitions and target-specific review exist **after real concepts exist**. Supplier/Trends projections require real concepts. | Support type/direction-first original capture, then project it to actual concepts when bindings exist. No invented products, dummy IDs or empty-ID workaround for concept-bound adapters. |
| `Jobs`, `research_memory`, `material_updates` | Exact saved role answers, small matching shortlists and immutable events exist. New changed creatives can still create new AI work. Automatic useful-update admission currently covers only newly substantiated single-piece dispatch. | Introduce cost-aware new-job admission and stable interpretation inputs; add bounded judgment for other genuinely useful comparable updates. Do not rerun every role for every free card. |
| `release_index`, `portal_storage`, portal validators | One release index has500-version/512KiB limits; revisions consume entries. Private draft browsing is a bounded three-list-page/eight-version recent view. | Add authenticated paginated archive reads and bounded versioned indexes without deleting history or merely raising caps. Keep exact approval/withdrawal/download semantics. |
| `research_memory`, `storage`, `operational_backup` | Snapshots scan event metadata/deep-copy state; disposable index limit16MiB; financial journal8MiB; one backup512MiB/100,000files. | Plan verified segmented/checkpoint reads and incremental verified backup sets before these limits become operational failures. Reuse stores and one writer; do not introduce a paid database or delete evidence. |

### 26.3 One identity, explicit meaning and actual examples

Use the existing event store and one writer. Store additive typed records in existing tables; add central kind-aware validators/readers and explicitly test legacy records. Proposed helper/contract names below are implementation targets, not currently available commands.

| Record / existing owner | Required fields and deterministic behavior |
|---|---|
| `functional-definition-v1` in `source_checks` | `concept_id`, revision, `meaning_hash`, exact record hash, separate presentation hash, manufacturer-independent `report_title`, primary use, mechanism, mounting/use context, meaningful age/compatibility constraints, included/excluded distinctions, observed/inferred basis, source refs and original review/job ID. Existing concept ID remains the recommendation identity. Raw descriptions/titles stay unchanged; nonessential missing specs stay unknown. |
| `concept-example-v1` in `source_checks` | Existing concept ID + original child/semantic/observation IDs; meaning hash, accepted membership/reason, actual seller/title/URL/pack/price/image references and dates. Derive exact existing resolved membership locally first; only ambiguous functional membership needs B. Different offers remain; repeated placements are not additional types. |
| `catalogue-concept-scope-v1` in `source_checks` | Stable catalogue family/heading ref + actual concept/meaning hash; relationship `same_scope`, `narrower`, `broader_context`, `unrelated`, or `unclear`; references/reason/method. A family label/alias/query alone cannot establish equivalence. Category overlap does not create duplicate concepts. |
| `research-question-v2` in `query_tasks` | `subject.kind` is `concept` or `catalogue_direction`. A concept subject binds its definition hash. A direction binds an existing accepted family ID and frozen question scope/qualifiers, not a fake recommendation ID. Include route, discovery/validation/sourcing purpose, geography/location, source options, wording candidates, freshness/revisit reason, dependencies, channel/cost classification and interpretation state. |
| `scoped-evidence-v1` in `source_checks` | Original capture/observation/ref hashes + subject/definition hash + separately reviewed relationship and support. Scope is `narrow_type`, `broader_context`, `example_offer`, `supplier_company` or `supplier_offer`; preserve source date, location and usable fields/limitations. One physical capture may have several accepted bindings, not several charges. |
| Future-history compatibility view | Resolve reviewed same-type aliases against existing concept IDs for future novelty/holds. Never rewrite old IDs, raw events, published packets or availability dates. An alias/split cannot automatically make an already-seen type New. |

`effective_functional_definition(state, concept_id)` uses a current accepted definition or an explicitly labelled legacy fallback; it cannot silently promote a broad family label. `definition_hash` on a subject denotes `meaning_hash`: use/mechanism/context/meaningful constraints and included/excluded boundaries only. Presentation/title, provenance/extra citations and selected example prices have separate exact hashes. Fit/membership/source applicability bind meaning **and the actual facts they used**; frozen packets also bind the exact selected record/presentation. Meaning equality is necessary, not sufficient, for answer reuse: an observed weight/material/compatibility fact that contradicts an earlier practical estimate reopens the affected judgment even when the type is unchanged. The selective invalidation table in26.14D owns that decision. Verify the original description/assessment actually supports the accepted meaning before reusing it—narrowing a definition is not automatically compatible.

`build_example_memberships(...)` derives exact existing bindings, checks functional contradictions and queues unresolved judgments. `prepare_research_questions(...)` accepts either subject kind and performs no source/model call. **Keep `associations` reserved for its existing child→concept records**; use already-kind-dispatched `source_checks` for these overlays, as with query profiles. This avoids injecting definitions into readers that require `semantic_id`/resolved child status. Add central `iter_functional_definitions`, `iter_example_memberships`, `iter_scoped_evidence` and positive `is_paid_query_task` selectors. Paid dispatch must not assume every task other than `all_source_task_v1` is paid.

Before any production writer emits a new kind, run a mixed legacy/current-state fixture through research-flow evidence indexes and summary, catalogue expansion, query profiles, brand/visual review, identity correction/merge protection, report selection/source facts, costs, programme progress, research operations, paid dispatch, coverage and backup selection. Explicitly extend merge/alias/dependency readers to project overlay records through accepted canonical IDs without mutating originals. A typed record's mere `concept_id` does not make it a source child, attempt or market vote. Do not half-adopt another overlay location during implementation.

For new definitions, use existing A/B descriptions and exact accepted bindings before calling AI. Where judgment is needed, extend B in a new versioned mode: give one observed description, at most10 candidate definitions plus actual distinguishing facts/refs; permit one local widened retrieval to20 if useful. Ask: “Do these describe the same useful functional kind, a meaningfully different mechanism/use, or is it unclear? Ignore seller, colour and ordinary pack differences. Explain only with supplied facts.” Code validates IDs/refs/contradictions, accepts same/different/unclear, and commits through existing association logic. Do not send all history, ask for a New quota or invent missing features. A separate definition mode may propose a short title and boundary from accepted examples; it cannot change identity or scores by itself.

Uncommitted genuine duplicate concepts may use the existing reviewed merge. `identity_corrections` deliberately protects held/uploaded/published concepts; those need the compatibility view, not a forced merge or overwritten history. If meaning remains uncertain, park only that association and keep independent capture/assessment moving. A split requires supported meaningful distinctions and explicit history inheritance; neither new child is automatically unseen just because it received another ID.

### 26.4 What to research at each source

Consider every agreed route for each research subject; **considered is not attempted or successful**. Select source-appropriate scope and actual applicability. Research headings can produce narrow types; accepted types can reuse earlier heading captures after scoped review. Specific offers answer offer-specific questions. This is one connected process, not a discovery universe and an unrelated validation universe.

| Route | Type/direction-level work | Concrete example or offer work; limits |
|---|---|---|
| Meta ads | Short narrow type/mechanism and grounded synonyms discover offers, advertisers and related functions. Independent free exploration can continue after a usable answer. | Bind actual ad/advertiser IDs and dates to accepted types; inspect actual destination where useful. Count repeated placements once. No sales claim from ad presence. |
| Amazon Bestsellers | Inspected India category/child-node lists discover or contextualise types. One list can serve many subjects. | Rank belongs to a matched listing in that actual list/date, not every variant or seller. Reuse saved lists before another visit. |
| Amazon Movers | Its own inspected India list/child scope. | Movement/rank belongs to the actual returned listing. Recognised empty is complete collection, not poor demand or a false successful extraction. |
| Amazon search/detail | Search narrow type, subtype and useful aliases. | Detail only useful missing distinguishing facts, actual prices/packs/photos. Search order is not bestseller rank. |
| Flipkart search/detail | Type/subtype/alias searches with actual location where exposed. | Review concrete matches and their own prices/packs/photos; do not borrow Amazon meanings. |
| Google Trends | Common narrow type where there is usable volume; broader family or useful local wording when narrower data is sparse. Preserve India/period/property. | Scope chart applicability explicitly. A “pull-up bars” chart is broader context unless it supports the narrow subtype; seller titles are normally inappropriate. Chart/export/import are separate outcomes. |
| Blinkit | Relevant type/subtype at actual reference location. | Match real listing/pack/availability/price. Location-specific context, not national sales. |
| Zepto | Same research purpose with its own request/location/parser. | Its own stock/listings, not substituted Blinkit results. |
| Instamart | Same research purpose with its own request/location/parser. | Its own stock/listings, not invented demand from presence. |
| Google suppliers | Type/mechanism + India manufacturer/wholesaler intent; discover actual businesses. | Read actual linked product/terms pages; excerpts are leads, not confirmed fulfilment. |
| Maps suppliers | Type/category + manufacturer/wholesaler/location; discover businesses/sites. | Verify product relationship and actual company pages; a map listing proves neither exact supply nor India-wide dispatch. |
| IndiaMART | Type/subtype/alias directory discovery. | Match complete purchase unit and actual quoted conditions. A contact/login wall is failed access, not no supplier. |
| TradeIndia | Type/subtype/alias directory discovery. | Read useful actual product/company terms; no borrowing sibling product MOQ/prices. |
| Company/product/terms pages | Only grounded discovered public URLs; company-level research can serve relevant types. | Preserve product-offer MOQ/price/stock separately from company/workflow fees, dispatch, packaging and subscription. No outreach or quote-request submission. |

Photo downloading is a supporting pipeline, not a fifteenth market-vote source. These14 routes represent seven market sites plus supplier routes: three Amazon methods are one site; suppliers add no demand votes. Exact narrow-type or reviewed relevant-family evidence can support the agreed priorities with its limitations, but very broad context must not be promoted into usable narrow-type confirmation merely to reach two sites.

Implement `build_subject_context(...)` for original public capture independently of a found listing. Meta/marketplace already support direction capture; refactor supplier/Trends/QC adapters into capture → parse → subject review → optional actual-concept projection. Preserve original concept-bound readers. No all-route preparation with invented empty product descriptions. Company-page tasks wait for actual discovered URLs; irrelevant QC can be Not applicable with a reason, but difficult access or no results cannot. All route rows expose preparation/attempt/result/interpretation/dependency separately.

### 26.5 Free search, AI work and client novelty are separate decisions

| Decision | First layer: local rules | Second layer: useful continuation | Third layer: boundary and saved result |
|---|---|---|---|
| May another direct local search run? | Verify permitted configured direct handler; read complete request identity/freshness/access state; reuse exact unchanged fresh capture. | Try unvisited source, grounded alternate wording, inspected child list, changed useful filter or due refresh. Similarity/repeated known results lower priority if unproductive; they do not automatically forbid free work. | Save immutable intent/receipt and selection reason. Actual429/challenge respects existing route wait; no keyword rotation to evade it. Another healthy source continues. |
| May a paid provider request run? | Resolve exact original uncertain job first; check provider/account-period identity, known fee envelope and commitments. | Prefer adequate saved/direct results; admit a useful unanswered question under existing funded allowance. Paid repeat-heavy cooldown remains scoped to that charged request class. | Reserve before launch, preserve original account and unknown exposure, never substitute another key to repost the same uncertain task. No paid upgrade. |
| Does captured material need AI? | Match exact source IDs/semantic content and accepted role/input versions; reuse original answers and bindings. Changed price/date alone updates facts, not product identity. | Batch genuinely new descriptions or changed relevant meaning; deterministic retrieval selects a shortlist. Invoke the appropriate role when new interpretation is needed and no reusable answer/adequate deterministic extraction exists. Identity ambiguity uses B/D; new fit/source support/terms/writing retain E/F/G ownership. | Before a new job intent, apply programme/model admission; saved answers/reconciliation remain allowed when new launches wait. Record tokens/calls/unknown usage; no paid fallback or dollar bill invented for subscription usage. |
| Is the recommendation New? | Resolve canonical functional identity and reviewed aliases; consult all actual per-client publication history and queued holds. | Use unused current types, or compare supported meaningful changes to the client's latest genuine baseline. | Seller/colour/wording/photo/date changes alone are not New/Updated. Code enforces issue count/mix and reserves exclusive types before writing. |

`physical_request_fingerprint` includes route/method, canonical public destination, query/inspected node, purpose where it changes returned material, country/location, actual transmitted date/property/filter/sort, pagination/depth and capture options affecting requested bytes. Preparation time/desired freshness is not a transmitted date filter. A consumer-only reason, product ID or **parser version** does not multiply physical acquisitions. `parse_fingerprint` separately binds original capture hash + parser contract; interpretation binds those facts + meaning/role version. Parser-only repairs reparse original complete saved bytes with an additive local receipt and original observation date, not another navigation. New retrieval is considered only for actually missing coverage/bytes or a useful scope/refresh question. Normalize only documented equivalents; never strip all numbers, distinguishing words or arbitrary URL parameters. Equivalent retrieval can serve several separately interpreted purposes.

Define `collection_channel = direct_local | funded_provider | saved_replay | unknown` and separately `incremental_collection_fee = known_zero | reserved_paid | unknown`. A browser running on a paid hosted service is not `direct_local`. Unknown fees are not free. The approved local computer's running hours are not an owner-imposed cash/time cutoff. Provider pace, memory/disk capacity and finite fair turns still matter for reliability. Repeated free work is allowed; pointless exact unchanged reads are reused unless there is a useful documented refresh question.

Keep up to three reviewed phrases in the current **validation sequence**. Add useful further free alternatives to later exploration waves, with their original grounding and same identity; do not restart validation at phrase1 or count three as lifetime exhaustion. No AI call is needed for an existing accepted synonym. C proposes wording only for a concrete unresolved scope/related discovery, using saved public facts and allowed source purposes; code validates refs, bounds and novelty of the *question*, not product novelty. A negative/empty result is not an instruction to keep inventing terms until success.

### 26.6 Renewable finite waves, independent sources and fairness

Extend `catalogue_discovery` with a new `discovery-wave-v2` in existing `batch_runs`: programme ID, increasing wave sequence, previous-wave ref, frozen policy/catalogue hash, original questions/requests, per-question states, work counters and selection reasons. Existing v1 cycles remain replayable. `prepare_next_discovery_wave(...)` resumes trigger-ready original work, serves the frozen wave's currently dispatchable first-pass questions, and parks dependent waits with original IDs/reasons/next triggers. Complete **or explicitly parked** is settled for wave scheduling, not completed research. Later waves can contain independent due work without waiting for blocked sources/AI. Keep parked obligations in a bounded fair cursor, not growing active-wave scans. Check equivalent open/uncertain requests across **all waves/programmes** before launch. Paid/unknown-fee/side-effecting execution remains original-only; the strictly defined free-read recovery below is not a charged retry. Four is a configurable wave size, not lifetime cap. The v2 source work-turn has12 callback slots **shared by discovery and existing/follow-on source work**, with a base2 exploration slots and unused capacity donation; it is not12 plus an invisible discovery allowance. Section26.14C defines donation, durable consumption and actual-request counting.

For **known-zero-fee permitted read-only local retrieval only**, an uncertain old response need not permanently prohibit that search. First prove its owned process has exited; then attempt exact saved reply/bytes/receipt recovery. If no usable reply can be recovered, permit one explicitly linked bounded `free_read_recovery` under the current fair turn, with original attempt ID, exit proof, failed recovery reason and normal source pacing/access state. Its new capture has its own receipt/current observation date; the original stays Unknown and retains all original records. Mark the recovery generation so restarting/waving does not spawn another replacement. No concurrent retry while the old browser may run; no bypass of429/challenge or conversion of unknown fees to zero. A paid Actor POST, model execution, side effect or possibly charged retrieval cannot use this exception. This is a planned v2 path, not retroactive relabelling of uncertain v1 captures.

Replace fallback-only family actions with **independent research questions**. Within one question, headless/headful/saved/permitted provider transports may be alternatives. Amazon research is not merely a failed-Facebook transport. Facebook success/empty/pending can settle or wait its question without closing Amazon, Flipkart, Trends or supplier questions. Pending interpretation blocks dependent interpretation/eligibility, not every free source about that family. Unknown paid/possibly charged execution prevents an equivalent replacement and its dependencies; known-free read-only loss has the explicit bounded recovery above.

`select_due_research_question(...)` reads original recovery, unfinished fit/source/supplier/report work and due discovery/refresh. Use two persisted lanes: ready backlog and exploration; initially reserve one callback for each in a two-callback selection, donating unused capacity. With one available slot, alternate by last-served cursor. Rotate category→subject→source within each lane by saved age; inspecting an unchanged wait consumes no launch slot. Trigger-ready recovery takes its lane's turn, not every turn indefinitely. Model waits cannot stop capture and a large backlog cannot permanently suppress exploration. Existing stage-specific model/photo/report limits remain distinct from source callbacks.

Add durable **cooperative work turns** to the existing outer programme, not another scheduler. Each `work_turn_id` freezes per-pass limits/counters/cursors; a completed bounded round yields after saving its exact checkpoint, even if productive discovery remains. The existing task can immediately select another due programme or later turn while the computer keeps working. `due_programme` must admit/rotate the latest real due week after a safe turn boundary, not wait for every older programme to reach a global research fixed point. Preserve unfinished scopes and origin funds. Source/model work still physically running retains the writer; after uncertain execution, its exact request remains protected while independent ready work rotates. Missed weeks do not create catch-up grants. This is internal fairness, not a2/6-hour runtime stop or false research completion.

A success/empty on one route is not whole-subject completion. Calculate state from all actual obligations, named inapplicable routes and genuine waits. Uninterpreted cards stay pending even if capture is complete. Only actual productive changes or a newly served due question advance the loop; timestamps/diagnostics/new empty waves do not. No due safe work produces a clean saved wait, not a busy loop or invented fallback. Owner stop remains authoritative across wave creation and launches.

### 26.7 Continuous catalogue growth without inflated runway

1. Inspect saved actual marketplace child navigation, new list cards, related Trend queries, advertiser/product destinations and relevant supplier catalogues. Keep original refs, source/list identity and observed dates. Source pages may contain irrelevant services; A/relevance must filter them, not the parser fabricate a corrected source.
2. First use local exact source/semantic IDs and existing grounded aliases to reuse known work. Give genuinely new items normal A/B admission; attach new examples to known types or create supported functional types. Useful new related directions can enter C before being report-ready.
3. C sees at most the existing bounded branch/example/nearby-heading input, not every old report. Ask it to choose same direction/new functional direction/unclear/no useful addition; explain function/mechanism/use and suggest short source-appropriate wording. No target family count or guaranteed positive result. Version the legacy Meta-primary expansion rule so new proposals may start with another appropriate source; old C jobs retain their original validation.
4. Admit accepted aliases to the same heading, and genuine functional additions to `catalogue_additions`. Organise with existing branches and accepted relationships. A related idea without observed support remains a labelled plausible direction, not a product or a market confirmation. Title/alias equality across functions/categories is not sufficient global semantic identity.
5. Schedule new sources/wording/due refreshed lists through numbered waves. A tried query is not an exhausted type; a renamed query is not a fresh type. Seasonal refresh and genuine new launches remain useful when supported, not date-only novelty.
6. Keep a rolling measured inventory of usable unreserved New types, held issues, still-promising unfinished types and useful updates. Compare it with the next actual scheduled releases. Section26.14G specifies intake and repair ownership, including types that become ready after a week starts; reporting membership alone is not completion of research. Warn about a shortfall with useful actions: complete saved promising work, broaden real directions, try unvisited free sources, or discuss material funding/delivery mismatch. Do not build thousands of speculative jobs or demand a six-month certification before working.

### 26.8 AI admission, reuse and matching at scale

Extend existing `Jobs.run` with an optional owning-programme admission callback **after cache/exact saved-job recovery and before new execution intent**. It receives role/mode/version, exact input-item keys, prepared input size, model route and current programme envelope. It returns `reuse`, `resume_original`, `allow_new`, or `wait_new` with reason/next trigger. Reuse/resume must identify an exact existing binding; the callback cannot fabricate an answer or permit a replacement of uncertain execution. Apply admission at every actual execution leaf, including previous-job/batch-splitting recursion. The original Jobs lifecycle still owns whether execution occurred; constructing/planning a job is not usage. Reconcile a lost model reply from its original result/owned process before another call. The exact counter/job/intent save order and no-nested-lock implementation are specified in26.14B.

Use current finite item/action bounds as initial work admission **per durable work turn**, not as lifetime programme quotas. Store work_turn_id, configured role/item/new-call limits, reserved/actually-started job IDs and used/remaining counts before launch. A crash or retry resumes that same turn's counters; saved-answer application adds no new call. A genuinely next fair turn can continue unfinished AI work; creating a wave cannot reset the active turn or any metered/account-period cap. Turn renewal creates local work capacity, not subscription credits/provider money. Provider/subscription waits retain real reset evidence. Do not invent a hard dollar cap for the signed-in subscription, treat unknown tokens as zero, or halt approved analysis solely because no API invoice exists. A metered paid model needs actual rates/funds and is not an automatic fallback. Effort targets are visible tuning inputs, not per-product proof gates.

| Role | Exact supplied context and responsibility | Reuse / nonblocking outcome |
|---|---|---|
| A | Genuinely new source semantic material; describe actual product/function and moderate brand. | Exact known source/content reuses A. Repeated price/rank/date changes do not create A work. Unknown product content can wait while deterministic captures continue. |
| B / definition mode | New described item and local functional candidates; scope/title only from supported examples. | Reuse accepted same-type membership; no whole-history prompt. Unclear parks membership, not all source work. |
| C | Grounded related directions and useful wording/branch additions; bounded neighbours. | Existing aliases are local. Cache no-addition/unclear for unchanged input; no repeated idea requests merely to grow a count. |
| E | Practical ten-row fit when needed; separately scoped source match/support or comparable material-update judgment. | Do not repeat checklist because a price/photo changed. Do not demand documentary proof for reasonable labelled weight/benefit estimates. Scope/refs and numerical totals remain code-owned. |
| F | Actual relevant product/company page bundle and proposed workflow. | Reuse company-wide terms only at their real scope; product-offer terms retain actual offer bindings. No borrowing other products' prices/MOQs. |
| D | Two or a few genuine supplied images plus a specific unresolved identity question. | Optional only; same image is not identity proof, and failure does not impose a photo/vision gate. |
| G | Frozen verified report facts, ten short reasons, chosen language and approved supplier/source wording. | Exact inputs reuse writing. It cannot invent prices, counts, supplier promises, evidence or novelty. |

Derive stable role inputs from original semantic facts using source-specific documented normalisation. Keep original bytes and meaningful changed captions/features. New seller URLs or creatives may genuinely require A; do not call fuzzy similarity deterministic proof. A local rebuildable function/token/accepted-alias index narrows candidates; compare its top candidates against current full-scan retrieval fixtures before adoption. It is a retrieval aid, not an authority to merge. Recheck accepted New against relevant late arrivals/aliases/holds immediately before reservation.

### 26.9 Selection, source truth and functional report contract

Select the existing concept **once** and display its narrow title/definition with available distinct concrete examples/supplier offers underneath. No new requirement for two example listings, two suppliers, exact weights or positive growth is introduced. One useful example remains valid with honest limitations. Apply ten practical criteria and the four source priorities at that definition's scope. A small example does not prove all variants are small; state the intended form/size and qualify inference. An audience positioning judgment is not a type boundary by itself.

Attach every price/photo to its actual example and source date. An example retail range needs actual sample count, comparable pack/unit scope, dates and limitations; do not pool incomparable currencies/packs or present it as a wholesale quote. A photo can show a genuine example of the defined type; label representative broader-family imagery separately and do not substitute generated images. Multiple useful company/product offers are retained, while duplicate company placements collapse for display without dropping meaningful different terms. Supplier effort/no-usable-result handling remains the exact simple sentence in§1, not a false assertion that supply does not exist.

Version the type/example contract through **pool → allocation/hold → frozen input → factual dataset → G/writing → HTML/PDF/workbook → manifest/package → private draft/server validators → release/history**. Add meaning, exact selected definition/presentation and example-membership hashes to `_source_revisions`, `source_scope_hash`, selection fingerprint and `programme._current_report_selection`; separate semantic from exact presentation invalidation. Python allocation/holds **and** `portal/release-admin.mjs` novelty checks must use the same reviewed alias/history projection. Bind factual currentness to the **selected types'** relevant alias/exposure/hold hashes; the current publication-root generation is separate CAS/read authority, not a reason to rerender after an unrelated report changes. No server AI; conflicting alias/hold projection parks the affected selection/publication only. Section26.14F covers already queued aliases, where merely parking a future local mutation is insufficient. Title/citation-only correction does not recollect or rerun A/B/fit; meaningful mechanism changes review only affected unpublished bindings. Preserve legacy readers/immutable originals; markers cannot grant old packets new semantics.
