Replace `_reports`' global `research_advancing` replacement barrier with **issue-scoped readiness**: one verified selection/facts snapshot, its own source/meaning/example hashes, relevant history/alias/hold bindings and exclusive holds. Recheck these dependencies immediately before reservation/freezing; render that immutable revision while unrelated research continues. If selected facts change during production, retain the coherent frozen version and schedule one current replacement afterward, not a mixture of facts or endless restart. Coalesce relevant changes at a safe work-turn boundary; do not wait for discovery across the whole application to become inactive. Protected uploaded/published revisions still require their actual correction authority.

Use the explicit new packet and five-sheet example contract in26.14E, with legacy readers retained; do not silently change the current four-sheet fit10 workbook. Sections26.14H–J specify supplier scope/currentness, actual frozen image bytes/aggregate limits and item-scoped writing reuse. PDFs/portal summaries can contain an “Example products” section using current styling. This is functional content, not a UI/UX redesign. English/Hinglish facts, counts, links, source limitations and supplier conditions remain identical.

For material updates, retain the deterministic newly-substantiated single-piece-dispatch rule. Resolve latest genuine client publication → **immutable issue/revision/package/facts hash**, not the mutable current reservation; a newer unpublished reservation cannot erase the older genuine baseline. Version `material_snapshot` for current seven-site scoped facts, comparable offer conditions and practical differences; current v1 stores historical three-group facts only. Adapt legacy snapshots or reconstruct from exact original packet bytes when possible; incomparable scope remains unclear. Add a bounded E mode supplied only that baseline/current comparable facts, code-computed changes and actual old/new refs. Output useful_update/not_material/unclear with short consequence. Code validates hashes, comparability, refs and ≤20%cap. No baseline means no claimed update; new capture IDs/date/format/seller/colour or any changed number alone cannot earn Updated.

### 26.10 Long-lived local records, private reports and backups

These are real longevity gaps, not permission to replace the application. Implement additive versions in current stores, validate old readers first, and use safe local fixtures before activating migrations. Keep originals by default; deletion/retention is not approved here.

| Existing seam | Designed change, correctness and recovery |
|---|---|
| Research state/events | Introduce verified immutable checkpoint/segment manifests with sequence ranges/event counts, predecessor/head hashes, original segment-byte hashes, bounded table-state pages and evidence namespace; replay only verified tail. Partition exact event-ID/idempotency lookup so no checkpoint copies a lifetime-sized ID list. Generate checkpoints from canonical replay, not self-hash trust; persist verified anchor under the same writer and check immutable segments. The disposable index never becomes authority. Bounded table/key projections replace full-history copies where consumers need only current rows; retain full replay for migration/repair. Corrupt checkpoints use retained verified chain; unknown/truncated history cannot reset novelty/money. |
| Research retrieval/index | Rebuildable function/alias/source-key projections carry policy/sequence/head hash. Incrementally apply new committed events. A stale/corrupt index rebuilds or uses bounded trustworthy fallback; index failure after canonical commit does not cause another source/model request. Do not rewrite a >16MiB whole-state cache on every event or create a paid/vector database. |
| Financial journal | Add immutable bounded period/sequence segments and a verified current-state checkpoint. Preserve global/account/period totals, original attempt IDs, unsettled holds and origin-bound recovery even after renewal. One short transaction reserves against the complete verified view. A new segment creates space, not credits; old `events.jsonl` bytes stay unchanged and old commands remain readable through the shared loader. Test crash across segment/pointer update and reconciliation before any paid launch. |
| Release index and downloads | Add `release-index-v2` small root + bounded month/sequence pages in the same private R2/Worker. Pages bind hashes/client/generation/issue/revision/server-time activation/withdrawal; roll over before byte/entry limits. Write immutable pages before conditional root replacement; retain legacy v1 readers/migration. Exact issue lookup and authenticated cursors use bounded reads **per request/page**, not constant reads for all history. Client download authority always resolves the current trusted root/generation and relevant page/status. A stale active page cannot override later withdrawal or an untrusted/unreadable root. Isolate unrelated page failure only while current authority for the requested report is verified. Local research and owner-authenticated immutable draft reads remain separate; no public fallback. |
| Release/history coherence | Maintain complete per-client type exposure/hold/alias knowledge in bounded deterministic ID/hash shards/pages, not one growing all-history document. The small current root binds release and identity/history shard generations together; validate only selected types' relevant shards for activation. Conditional root replacement atomically references the intended consistent pages; lost reply reconciles that exact generation. Migrate availability/withdrawal/ordinals/approvals/bytes without resetting first-use. Withdrawals/revisions update affected pages/root, not permanent exposure. Draft-only machine rights cannot write aliases, release roots or history authority. |
| Owner draft access | Preserve honest recent view; add bounded cursor/issue lookup with authenticated per-page checks. Distinguish total known versions from versions inspected on this page. Older drafts must remain accessible without scanning/checking all objects every refresh. Draft-only machine key stays draft-only: no approval/index mutation/client list rights. |
| Automatic local backup | Extend daily hook to segment/checkpoint manifests + content-addressed unchanged attachments. One set spans bounded parts;512MiB/100,000file limits apply per part, not lifetime. Save original set ID, frozen selection/head, part cursor and chain refs; resume exact interrupted set, not start another full copy each tick. Missing/unverified canonical or required manifest parts means Incomplete. Verified canonical copy with explicitly unavailable historical attachments keeps distinct `saved_with_limitations`, not false full readiness or endless retries for unchanged missing files. Verify isolated restore by heads/finance/holds/reports/bytes. Exclude credentials/recursive backups. Preserve old readers; same-drive copy does not protect whole-drive loss. |

Use one writer and atomic/conditional pointer changes; never hold financial locks during network/model/archive uploads. Snapshot/index optimisation must not weaken exact original execution proof. Introduce no new cloud service, subscription, distributed queue, global recovery engine or whole-program kill monitor. Measure actual bytes/lookup counts/latency on local scale fixtures; do not invent maintenance-hour or guaranteed speed claims. Existing same-drive capacity can be reported; no automatic evidence deletion. Segmentation is for bounded correctness and access, not merely fewer seconds of local running.

### 26.11 Counts, provenance, yield and expense that the owner can follow

Use one owner funnel with explicit dates and examples: “We chose these research areas; these words are alternative ways to search them; these source questions were considered; this many actual pages were read; these rows became distinct functional types; these types finished review; these unused types can fill these issues.” No naked denominator, mixed scope or invented whole-project percentage. Distinguish stored plan, actual attempt, complete capture, reviewed match, fit completion, usable market support, supplier effort, available photo, eligible unreserved type, proposed allocation and generated/published logical issue.

**Retained lineage, not a current complete inventory:** the8 October18:42:51UTC view has233 research families and470 family/wording pairs. Its710 stored source-purpose instructions are245 pairs assigned once +210 assigned twice +15 assigned three times:245+420+45=710, and245+210+15=470. The instructions split229 Meta-discovery +227 marketplace-validation +231 Trends-validation +23 marketplace-discovery. There is no universal “expand every term to all14” algorithm or evidence that this assignment is optimal. Some families are headings rather than reportable narrow types. The later19:32 view is240/482/722; do not combine its totals with the earlier dated consumption.

The original156 concepts did **not** come from executing710 choices. The retained original paid ledger has44 attempts:30 catalogue-mapped focused Meta discovery +4 legacy broad Meta discovery +5 Meta validations +2 Amazon Bestsellers +2 Movers +1 failed Trends. Tracing each original concept to its creation child and earliest retained source observation links all156:101 first-origin concepts from focused Meta,34 from broad Meta,4 from validation captures and17 from Bestsellers;21 captures contributed origins. These are encountered functional ideas, not156 confirmed matches or eligible recommendations. Other attempts may have returned repeats/irrelevant/empty/failed material. The later32/229 mapped-discovery consumption is a different dated measurement, not a substitute for these44 original attempts.

Under the new design,14 route considerations for470 wording pairs would be6,580 **possible cells**, not6,580 required requests or new types. Group aliases by actual research subject and reusable physical question; select source-appropriate wording, list nodes and real applicability. A shared list can answer many cells; multiple locations/purposes can create more than one question on a route. A considered inapplicable route is not an attempt. The original153 non-established concepts ×14 =2,142 target-route audit rows;308 individually proved attempts at the dated checkpoint leaves1,834 without an individual proved attempt, **not1,834 unused catalogue searches or completed checks**. These views join through family/direction ↔ concept scope bindings and original acquisitions, not by multiplying unrelated totals.

Record yield in separated cohorts: raw cards → source-unique records → newly resolved functional concepts → completed practical assessments → eligible current types → never-shown unreserved New types → frozen entries/logical issues. Show known-existing and meaningful updates separately. Repeats need cheap recognition, not automatic exclusion of the free search. Completed no match and blocked access are both real effort but different outcomes; pending assessment is neither a rejection nor completed screening.

Financial reporting has four separate columns: **incremental provider fees**, **actual incremental AI invoice if any / recorded subscription effort**, **local runtime**, and **hosting/storage/other actual charges**. Shared acquisition expense appears once; failures/charged repeats stay in totals. A generated-issue shared average is not a future price. Forecast10/15 entries, all-New/maximum-useful-update cases and5%/3% useful-New yield with current eligible backlog shown separately. HistoricalUS$0.50040006/156 stays a labelled old paid baseline, not the assumed rate for new free collection. Use measured direct/funded cohorts and actual model rates only where genuine; unknown bills stay unknown, not zero. Cash warnings remain the existing simple owner/native mechanisms; no extra monitor service.

### 26.12 Implementation order, consumer closure and fallback

These are six dependency blocks of **one connected implementation**, not six mandatory sessions or routine handbacks. Relative size describes touched consumers, not measured hours or equal percentage weights. The owner has now approved implementation; follow the joined workstream without routine milestone stops. Section27 distinguishes current code/proof from the remaining work.

| Block / relative size | Implement, then verify at its actual seams | Dependency and continuation |
|---|---|---|
| S1 — functional meaning and request contracts; Medium | Add current-definition/example/scope records and kind-aware readers; preserve IDs, subtype queries and complete cost/request fingerprints. Create a read-only migration inventory of existing concepts/headings/aliases and exact affected consumer map. | First: later exploration/reports must agree on what one recommendation means. Reuse current exact membership; unknown bindings leave independent tasks ready. |
| S2 — renewable free exploration and generic capture; Large | Numbered waves, full physical request keys, independent route questions, inspected list/direction admission, capture-before-concept bridges, cooperative work turns and due-week rotation. Preserve v1 cycles/original156 scope. | S1. No broad removal of paid controls. Connect bridges through original receipt/replay/relevance. Activate broadened discovery with S3 new-job admission, not unlimited new A/B calls first. |
| S3 — economical interpretation and inventories; Medium | New-job admission/cache inputs, bounded matching/index, complete practical ten-row reviews, offer-bound supplier interpretation and rolling New/update intake with actual due-repair ownership (§26.14G–H). | S1–S2; existing valid saved interpretations continue throughout. AI limits wait new AI jobs, not free captures or exact saved-result application. |
| S4 — type/example outputs and meaningful updates; Large | Update allocation/holds/alias projections/definition dependencies, issue-scoped asset/fact freezing, item-owned G reuse, workbook/PDF/manifest/private validators/history and immutable published update baselines (§26.14I–J). Inspect actual coherent packets and aggregate file sizes. | Local construction follows S1/S3. Enable new private/server contracts only after S5's required release/history readers exist; section26.14F fixes the activation order. Supplier/image failures remain labelled nonblocking; missing facts cannot be invented. Keep current approved styles/immutable packets. |
| S5 — bounded long-lived storage and access; Large | Verified research/finance segment checkpoints, release/archive pagination, permanent novelty projection, multipart incremental backup/isolated restore. Shared loaders/validators migrate together. | S1 foundations; implement local migration/scale proof before activation. Existing functioning legacy paths remain until migration readback succeeds; no paid/new cloud infrastructure. |
| S6 — whole-programme acceptance and owner handoff; Medium | Focused regressions, interruption/paired failures,2,000-issue/calendar simulation, bounded real current-source checks, actual current private downloads and revised cohort-based monthly forecast. | S2–S5. Report implementation/local proof/actual route proof independently. Only real remaining owner/provider boundaries remain; no artificial stop after the first ten recommendations. |

| Failure specific to this design | Preserved work, permitted fallback and next trigger |
|---|---|
| Scope/definition unclear or conflicting | Save exact question/refs; use supported legacy view for unaffected work, not invented narrow meaning. New source examples can reopen that binding. |
| One source blocked or its model interpretation pending | Save exact stage/capture; respect actual route wait; other independent source questions and deterministic processing receive turns. Access errors do not become no results. |
| Free query repeat-heavy or response lost | Keep repeats/earlier answers and fair alternatives. For lost known-free read-only response, prove old-process exit and exhaust original saved recovery before one linked recovery read under26.6; old uncertainty remains. No paid escalation, uncertain POST replacement or permanent family ban. |
| Provider funds unavailable | Keep original liabilities/uncertain launches; use permitted direct/saved routes or another independently funded new question. No account switch for the same unknown launch. |
| New AI work waiting | Preserve raw captures and job ownership; exact reuse, extraction, free research and already-completed report stages continue. Resume on real model/work-envelope trigger, not every tick unchanged. |
| Capture saved, membership/commit interrupted | Reconcile exact original capture/result, then bind it once. Never navigate again merely to recover a local projection. |
| Segmented research/finance history cannot be verified | Use retained verified original chain/checkpoint where possible. Pause dependent writes/spending/novelty claims; known valid owner files and hosted reports with independently current verified release authority can remain usable. No reset to empty history or stale withdrawal bypass. |
| Archive root/page mutation reply lost | Read exact intended root/page hash/generation and reconcile before another conditional write. Unrelated local research continues. Never expose private files publicly as a fallback. |
| Backup part missing or disk space exhausted | Keep earlier complete backups and exact pending-part cursor; mark new set incomplete, surface actual capacity need and continue work that can safely persist. No silent deletion or false Ready label. |

### 26.13 Acceptance and adversarial review matrix

These are required implementation tests, not tests executed by this planning pass. Use local synthetic namespaces for scale and failure injection; genuine provider/model checks are separately bounded and charged/reported under existing authority.

| Area | Required cases and invariant |
|---|---|
| Functional identity | One type/three sellers/five colours gives one recommendation and multiple examples. Meaningful folding/heated/mounting/age distinctions can remain different. Same image/text is not automatic equivalence; synonyms with no overlapping words are not automatically New. Broad heading cannot erase known subtype. No combination of unrelated example features creates a fictitious type. |
| Current/legacy migration | Reuse suitable original concept IDs; original156 scope/source ID, jobs, raw events, finance and immutable packets remain intact. Held/published merges use compatibility views; splits/aliases/title changes cannot reset New. New typed records do not confuse legacy generic readers. |
| Independent free research | Facebook success, empty, failed and pending each leave useful independent Amazon/Flipkart/Trends questions ready. A paid Meta cooldown does not suppress free siblings. A fifth action can enter a new wave after four settled choices; an empty queue never spins new waves. |
| Physical-request reuse | Same full route/request shares across targets; different city/period/node/depth/filter does not falsely reuse. Ten repeated cards are not ten signals. Uncertain paid/unknown-fee launch is never reposted after new wave/week/key. Lost known-free read-only reply permits one linked reread only after proved process exit/saved recovery; active/unconfirmed old browser or429 wait prohibits it. |
| AI economy | Ten thousand unchanged copies reuse accepted analysis with zero additional role calls. Price/date-only changes refresh facts, not A/B/fit; changed commercial terms may need F. Title/citation-only changes do not change meaning. Meaningful mechanism changes review affected bindings. Crash after ninth of ten configured turn slots permits only one more in that same turn; a later fair turn can continue without new paid funds. Exact cache/replay bypass new-job wait safely. |
| Fairness/continuation | Continuous ready backlog and exploration each receive donated/alternating bounded turns; a parked wave does not freeze future waves. Continuous successful discovery across week boundary admits next due week while old work remains resumable. Changed selected report facts produce a coherent replacement despite unrelated discovery. Exact uncertainty remains exclusive across waves/weeks. Pause is respected; diagnostics do not create progress. |
| Evidence/criteria | Shared broad Trends chart remains broader context; target bindings cannot copy another type's verdict. Seven-site rules and all four priorities work; QC presence is neutral; supplier routes add no market vote. Practical estimates and unknown≠failure survive all consumers. |
| Offer truth/output | Neighbour supplier price/MOQ, another pack's quote and representative photograph cannot become exact offer facts. Retain all useful leads, company/product term scopes and simple no-result limitation. PDFs/workbook/languages/private validators agree on every selected type/example/number/condition. |
| Updates/history | EightNew+two supportedUpdated passes ten; seven+three fails. Date/seller/colour/capture-ID-only change cannot earn Updated. Comparable seven-site/offer changes may. Publishedr1 remains comparison baseline after unpublishedr2 reservation. Python/Worker aliases agree; missing/corrupt history never makes old types New. Per-client exposure/holds stay separate. |
|2,000 logical issues | Simulate2,000 issue identities with revisions/editions,20,000 minimum appearances,≥16,000 distinct New types and valid limited updates. Synthetic contract/capacity proof, not real inventory. Verify oldest authenticated retrieval, stable ordinals, exclusive holds and bounded reads per page/request with cursor traversal. Stale active page after root withdrawal never authorises client download. |
| Beyond current caps | Exercise >500 release versions/>512KiB aggregate metadata, >16MiB research projections, >8MiB financial history and >512MiB backup through bounded segments/parts. Roll over checkpoint event-ID lookup/history shards too. Test corrupt/missing parts, interrupted rollover/root races and exact restore. Missing canonical backup part is Incomplete; known missing historical image remains explicit limited copy. No deletion/money reset. |
| Calendar/recurrence | Simulate12–15 years of real Tue/Thu/Sat India dates, month/year/leap boundaries, provider renewals, missed/reboot periods, seasonal revisits and deferred language/client approval. No accumulating missed-week funds, repeated grants or publication without authority. |
| Failure at each boundary | Interrupt before/after intent, navigation, saved reply, parse/import, A/B/E/F result, wave advance, allocation, each artifact/upload, archive/checkpoint/backup pointer. Reconcile original once; pair model outage+source success, write contention+returned capture and archive conflict+healthy local research. |
| Generic bridge/parser migration | One actual type-only subject per adapter class collects without fabricated concept; original bytes replay and later project to a real newly admitted concept with zero recollection. Parser-only upgrade reparses saved bytes with zero navigation/provider charge; unchanged semantics reuse AI. Mixed paid/controller/v2 tasks and legacy/overlay records pass read-only summaries, report/brand/merge/profile/cost/backup paths before activation. |
| Exact new lifecycle | Two subjects/weeks sharing one physical request produce one original acquisition and independent relevance bindings. Joining after capture does not navigate; a stale target cannot erase another target's result. Interrupt each model admission/event/intent/result boundary, including recursive old batches: reserved counters survive, no nested lock, no unpaid or uncertain duplicate launch. Several carried never-started jobs still consume current-turn execution-service slots and cannot bypass its10-call size; original admission/accounting is not duplicated. |
| Actual tick and shared waits | One automatic tick serves repeated finite turns and another due programme, rather than returning after its first selected programme. One crashed turn resumes remaining counters; a genuine closed turn can renew work capacity. A Google or Amazon access failure suppresses only requests sharing its actual host/session/access context across discovery and validation; unrelated origins and saved parsing still run. |
| Selective facts and activation | New contradictory observed weight reopens the affected inferred criterion without rerunning unrelated A/B. Exact09:00 scheduled freshness uses full timestamps; old published files remain downloadable after evidence ages. New facts/manifest/workbook contracts are accepted only by matching readers; rollback cannot silently run a legacy writer against unsupported authoritative records. |
| Rolling inventory and due repair | A reporting-only older type receives its exact needed repair; rediscovery does not suppress that obligation. A finished origin's due support refresh executes once outside the old final-task guard. Types becoming ready after week preparation enter upcoming unprotected issues. Eight unused New plus two accepted genuine updates can fill ten across weeks; date-only updates cannot. No full historical-pool clone, refreshed observation fiction or duplicate hold. |
| Supplier offer/currentness handoff | One company/two offers/different MOQs keeps each price, unit and restriction together. Same-name unrelated businesses do not merge; a proved directory/direct-site match may group without losing offers. Unchanged recaptured company terms reuse F but bind actual new capture dates; an old page interpreted later does not renew age. Changed exceptions/fees invalidate only dependent fields; stale useful leads remain dated, not product failures. |
| Photo acquisition and immutable assets | Two separately reviewed subjects share one physical image GET. Crash after byte save recovers without GET; saved404 parks rather than spinning; a genuine transient recovery remains possible. Remove shared media after report asset freeze and restart between languages: both use verified owned copies. Missing/corrupt owned assets recover exact bytes or lead to one unprotected new revision, never altered frozen content or blocked healthy issues. |
| Aggregate report size | Twenty individually permitted near5MiB images cannot produce an oversized embedded packet. Verify bounded display transforms, EXIF/orientation/transparency handling, actual encoded byte budget, deterministic replay and one failed derivative. Actual PDF/workbook/HTML bounds are checked before promotion/staging; no product rejection, source mutation or silent required-fact truncation. |
| Writing reuse is narrower than packet identity | A photo/path/byte-only revision makes zero new G calls when its actual writing inputs are unchanged. One changed protected supplier limitation reopens only that item and dependent translation; changed English reopens corresponding Hinglish. Original job/row provenance survives reuse. Unknown legacy projection cannot bless arbitrary prose; a changed whole-packet hash alone cannot invalidate every writing item. |
| Owner math and scope | Assert470→710 assignment sums,44 original-attempt taxonomy,153×14 audit denominator, considered/attempted/interpreted separation, shared charge once,10/15 entries and3%/5% forecasts with rounding/backlog boundaries. Never mix generated issues with proposals/revisions or historical paid yield with new free yield. |

Completion review checks actual consumers and failure cases, not only record schemas. Keep implementation, local deterministic proof, real source/model proof and owner/client delivery distinct. The independent planning review and its resolved findings are retained in the existing review document; no new plan, provider, monitor or guarantee is introduced.

### 26.14 Exact lifecycle, dependencies and activation contracts

These 9 October tightening passes make the preceding design executable at its actual seams, including saved-work re-entry and report/supplier/photo handoffs. They specify the implementation contract; section27 identifies which interfaces now have code/local proof. They are not installed commands or measured performance promises merely because they appear here. Use direct helpers in the existing modules, current event tables and existing native task. No additional scheduler, queue service, decision engine or monitoring process is required.

#### A. One research question is not one paid request or one verdict

| Object / deterministic key | Implementation and ownership |
|---|---|
| Logical question | `research-question-v2`: key from contract version, subject kind/ID/meaning-or-frozen-direction-scope, route, purpose and substantive question options. Programme/week is ownership/provenance, not a reason to duplicate the same unchanged physical read. Store the frozen binding separately from mutable progress. Candidate wording IDs bind exact grounded wording/options; later alternatives link to their parent question rather than overwrite a dispatched request. |
| Physical acquisition owner | `claim_source_acquisition(...)` in the small `source_acquisitions` helper is called by `source_coverage` and discovery adapters to resolve the full physical fingerprint across controller tasks, discovery waves and retained legacy intents under the one writer. Reuse a fresh complete capture, join a still-owned original attempt, or reserve a genuinely due new acquisition generation. `acquisition_owner_ref` identifies the original task/attempt; other questions point to it. Keep this ownership in existing task records/projections, not a second financial ledger or speculative product. A projection must be verified against canonical records before launch. |
| Actual attempt | Original acquisition ID/generation binds channel, fee classification, exact request, origin account if charged, actual dispatch proof, owned-process evidence and immutable receipts. A refresh gets a new generation with its actual due reason/date, not a new type or new funds. Legacy uncertain execution is never adopted as a definitely unstarted v2 request. |
| Interpretation | Binding key includes subject meaning, original capture/parsed-fact hashes and role/contract. It refers to one acquisition but owns its own relevance and supported fields. A newly joined subject can reparse/review the saved capture without another acquisition or copying another subject's verdict. |

`prepare_research_questions(...)` only prepares records. `claim_source_acquisition(...)` checks owner stop, current source wait, original saved recovery, exact request compatibility/freshness, then fee/admission. It commits the original ownership/intent before the collector can run. Charged launch additionally uses the existing original-account financial reservation, never an inferred free fee or an account substitute. Recheck ownership after any local conflict; no networking occurs inside a financial/research commit lock. All source-controller/discovery/direct/manual-import entry points use the same ownership helper or remain explicitly non-launching.

Persist and expose separate dimensions: acquisition `prepared / reserved / dispatching / response_saved / parsed / completed_empty / failed_access / execution_unknown`; interpretation `not_started / pending / accepted / unrelated / unclear`; scheduling `ready / waiting / parked / settled / superseded`. These are v2 states with explicit adapters for old controller states, not renamed historical receipts. A complete empty recognised source response is collection completion, but not usable demand evidence. A parser failure is not empty; a blocked response is not absence. A parked obligation is settled for selecting a later wave only. Not applicable needs an actual source/type reason, not an access failure.

Save response bytes/receipt before parsing and save a parser receipt before any target application. Record requested/delivered coverage, intentional card/depth limit, byte truncation and parse defects separately. A successfully bounded30-card read is not a failed attempt just because the page has100 cards. Usable partial rows continue; only the unmet useful coverage question can justify reading more. If original bytes are missing/truncated, do not claim parser-only repair; classify missing coverage and consider a permitted new read under normal ownership/cost rules. Apply derived bindings in bounded commits (initially at most20 subjects per commit), recording original acquisition and next unapplied subject cursor. Verify each current subject independently; a changed/superseded subject cannot discard the capture or other subjects' accepted results. Replaying the same receipt/event binds once and costs no new request. One callback can contain several page/image requests: callback slots, proven physical requests, model calls and provider charges are separate counters.

`marketplace_browser.replay_marketplace` currently reparses using today's parser and compares against the old `parsed.json`; `research_memory.import_capture` accepts only the current deadline-reparse recovery variant. Extend both explicitly: verify historical artifacts with their original recorded parser contract (or its original hash-bound saved-output verification), then write a separate `parse-revision-v1` artifact/receipt keyed by original capture hash and parser version. New capture/import/observation keys additionally bind that parse receipt; original acquisition ID, observation date, dispatch and expense remain the same. Teach relevance/facts/dependency readers to select the accepted parse revision, not count both revisions as extra evidence or overwrite immutable capture fields. Reject unverified old mappings rather than silently relabel them with the new parser. Test a genuine changed parser output plus unchanged output, with zero navigation in both cases.

#### B. Model allowance, original job and intent have one recoverable save order

`Jobs.run` keeps exact item-binding reuse and its original immutable result files. `work_turns.admit_model_job(...)` sits at the execution leaf, not merely at the outer batch call or `_ProgrammeRunner.__call__`. Expose the context through the owning programme/Jobs memory binding so every A–G call site, including nested helpers, inherits it; keep turn IDs/counters **out of semantic input/job cache keys**. Reordered/chunked/previous-job recursion must carry the same admission context; resolving an older batch cannot launch its unstarted remaining items without admission. An original ten-item batch requested through one overlapping item reserves its complete original execution payload, not one item. A job metadata row without an intent is not proof of usage or an outage. An existing result/completion is recovered before new admission; an existing uncertain intent remains original-only.

1. Prepare deterministic job/attempt IDs and exact item bindings; read cache/results and original intent/process evidence. Validate payload/role/model/version before reserving. Reuse accepted saved answers immediately; leave genuinely pending original execution protected.
2. For genuinely new execution, use **one `ResearchMemory.commit`** with dependency validation to add/update the original job admission binding and the owning `work-turn-v1` counters in `batch_runs`. The admission token binds turn ID, job ID, attempt generation and item-binding hashes. Repeated admission of that token consumes no additional slot. Do not call `memory.commit/put` while already inside `memory.journal.locked`: the current journal explicitly rejects nested locks. Planning/progress snapshots cannot reset these canonical counters.
3. Write the original filesystem intent referencing that admission token, read back the exact binding, then enter the existing runner. No model/network call occurs under the short commit lock. Record reserved, actually started, returned and applied separately; uncertainty consumes its reserved slot, not a guessed zero. Recheck owner pause and route permission immediately before execution.
4. Save original response/usage before applying accepted rows. If the response is saved but application fails, reconcile/apply it once without another call. If the counter event exists but intent is absent, recover the same reservation/job; do not create a second origin admission. Proven-before-launch cancellation may close that original reservation without usage, retaining its reason; lack of an execution receipt alone cannot justify reclaiming it.

Distinguish original admission ownership from **the turn servicing an execution**. A closed turn can carry a proved never-started reservation forward without blocking other programmes. Before that job actually starts in a later turn, atomically reserve a current-turn execution-service slot (and its full relevant payload-item size), bound to the same original job/attempt and origin admission token. No second fee/usage/admission identity is created; an unused original slot is not a launch voucher outside later-turn fairness. Initial admission reserves origin and service bindings together. Saved reply application or inspecting an already-running original uses no new execution-service slot because neither launches. Missing/uncertain original execution cannot be classified never-started merely to enter this path. Multiple carried jobs cannot bypass the shared10-execution turn ceiling.

One narrow compatibility case prevents an old indivisible original batch becoming permanently stuck after a purpose-item tuning reduction. A genuinely canonical, previously admitted but never-intented original batch of at most10 items may use one empty current purpose bucket even when its full payload exceeds that bucket's new item setting. Record `carried_purpose_overage:true`, charge the **full** original item count and one current execution slot, retain its original admission, and admit no further work for that purpose in that turn. New batches and metadata-only unadmitted legacy batches cannot use this exception; they must respect the current item bound or remain named policy waits. The shared execution ceiling is unchanged. A turn-managed job cannot escape through the unmanaged legacy entry: saved replies remain reusable, but launching its carried work requires an active turn.

The signed-in subscription still has no fabricated cash invoice. A role-contract change requires new interpretation only when its accepted facts/meaning are affected; it does not authorise an automatic paid model. Original pending jobs survive turn/week rollover. A new fair turn can reserve new work, but cannot transfer or erase uncertain old admissions, actual subscription waits or metered financial commitments. Store genuine account/runner-route waits independently of programme/turn and check them before intent creation. Reaching a local turn limit returns an admission wait, **not** a provider outage/model-wide pause. Exact saved results still apply while either waits.

#### C. Actual entry-point wiring and initial finite defaults

`scripts/run_programme.py --tick --execute` currently selects one programme and calls `advance_programme` once. Implement its v2 control loop explicitly: select/resume a due programme → open/resume one durable turn → execute a bounded round → save/close that turn → release the existing programme writer → select the next ready programme/turn. Factor guard-held helpers instead of nesting `prepare_programme`/`advance_programme` writer locks. Recheck week admission/service state under the same existing writer before creating a genuinely new week. `due_programme` uses saved readiness plus a monotonic last-served turn sequence for fair ties, not an old global fixed-point requirement. A safe completed turn may yield while its programme still has useful work.

| Initial v2 tuning input | Exact meaning, restart and boundary |
|---|---|
| One bounded programme round per turn | Start with the current stage sequence once, not unbounded `max_cycles=None` inside a turn. Save globally monotonic `turn_sequence` across programmes, programme ID, input policy/hash, opened/closed state, counters and cursors as `work-turn-v1` in `batch_runs`; update the bounded current-turn projection under the same writer. A crash resumes the open turn; a completed turn closes with its reason and then permits another. |
|12 shared source callback slots | Discovery plus original/follow-on acquisition draw from the same durable turn, including any callback used for saved-only reconciliation. Base allocation is2 exploration callbacks, with1 exploration and1 backlog slot guaranteed when both are ready; donate unused exploration slots to backlog and unused backlog slots to permitted exploration. A callback is reserved before entry; missing local return consumes its reservation. Pure local saved-result application can continue within bounded stage item/commit sizes without a fresh-navigation slot; invoking another handler still needs a callback slot. |
| Stage item defaults | Retain CLI defaults20 query profiles (`C:product_query_profile`),10 fit reviews (`E:fit`),5 source-match reviews (`E:source_support`),5 supplier reviews (`F`) and10 photos per turn as fair work sizes, **shared across original and every follow-on scope**, not repeated for each child. Other C/E/B modes draw from the shared new-call ceiling, not an implicit reuse of each purpose's item limit. Source/API calls nested inside any stage still require their own ownership/admission. Shared items are counted once at their actual execution owner. |
| New model calls | Initial shared ceiling10 execution-service reservations per turn, including carried never-started original jobs, each retaining the existing maximum10 supplied items and256KiB payload. Stage item bounds and call bounds are different; preparation/reuse costs no new call. Rotate pending purpose/scope admission with a saved cursor; reserve one slot for an already-ready frozen report's G work when present, donating it if absent, so earlier continuously productive A/B/C cannot starve writing. A newly ready report can take its reserved turn next round. This is a tunable fairness/effort setting, not10 free dollars or a promised monthly usage ceiling. Finishing one call leaves its response applicable even if the turn has no new-call slots left. |
| Idle/owner stop | Continue immediately across safe turns while meaningful due work exists. If every remaining obligation has a genuine wait or no useful question, save the earliest trigger and exit cleanly for the existing15-minute/login task; do not spin new empty turns, sleep indefinitely or create another task. Owner pause prevents new turns/launches and keeps successful in-flight work. |

The `C:...`/`E:...` labels above are admission-purpose buckets, not replacement prompt-mode names. Retain current `product_query_profile`, `fit_assessment`/`fit_partial_review`, and `marketplace_relevance_only` contracts with their supported styles; new definition/update modes are explicitly versioned separately. Counters must not multiply when several purposes use E or several programme scopes share one runner.

Keep `--max-cycles` as an optional bound on completed programme rounds for this invocation, not an allowance-reset switch; persist each turn's counters regardless of process restarts. A manually named programme remains pinned to that programme; automatic ticks rotate programmes. Compute next readiness from dependencies: any independent ready work permits its next fair turn immediately; only all-waiting work gets the earliest real wait/change trigger. Do not carry the current unconditional default five-minute delay or one model's future retry into healthy free work. Do not create another Windows task or overwrite an arbitrary registration. Preserve the legacy command until the existing reviewed upgrade helper verifies the exact owner, action, triggers and settings, adds only the explicit `--cooperative-turns` option to that same task, and verifies its readback. Ordinary registration remains create-only; this named semantic upgrade is the narrow exception. Current task activation/timer evidence must nevertheless be rechecked after the changed runner is loaded; old proof is not proof of the new loop. Continuous productive work may keep the existing process alive, and finite turns still give due programmes fair service.

#### D. Selective invalidation, source waiting and report freshness

Record `dependency_kind`, actual input refs/hashes, affected meaning/criterion/offer fields and calculation/role contract on derived bindings. Keep a separate `fit_basis_hash` over policy/prompt and the actual applicable facts/inference fields: audience/benefit corrections can affect fit without changing functional meaning. `changed_dependency_targets(...)` in current projection/selection helpers compares those inputs locally; it is not an AI call or a new global event bus. A conservative legacy fallback can revalidate one affected assessment; it cannot cause a whole-history rerun. A new unreviewed listing does not automatically contradict an accepted fact: first accept its actual relationship and offer/type scope.

| Actual change | Reuse, recompute or review |
|---|---|
| Report title, extra citation or harmless wording | Keep identity/fit/source judgments; update exact presentation/provenance hashes and affected unpublished rendering. Raw original-brand judgments remain bound to source descriptions, not the cleaned report title. |
| Same-type new price, stock or date | Update the actual offer/source facts and code-owned comparisons/freshness. Keep A/B and unaffected fit criteria. New commercial conditions or ambiguous units may require scoped F; changed support can affect priority without changing identity. Review a benefit row only if its saved reasoning actually depended on the changed price/savings; unrelated price changes make zero fit calls. |
| Observed weight/material/size/age or corrected audience contradicts an estimate | Reopen only criteria/applicability/membership that relied on that estimate. Use existing `fit_review.prepare_fit_review/run_fit_reviews` and E's `fit_partial_review` with requested active IDs, the old reason, newly accepted facts and exact refs; extend its input binding for new scoped facts rather than invent a second criterion-review workflow. Deterministically validate all ten rows/counts after combining valid unaffected rows. A corrected audience can change criterion13 while identity stays stable. An exact heavy offer does not make all variants heavy; keep offer/type scope. A supported factual correction is neither failure by absence nor a requirement for exact packed weight. |
| Meaningful mechanism/use boundary changes | Review affected memberships, query scope, fit/source/supplier bindings and unprotected selections. Preserve original meanings/history. Different functional evidence is not merely presentation. |
| Parser changes with complete saved bytes | Reparse locally, compare actual resulting facts, reuse unchanged accepted role inputs; only changed relevant fields invalidate dependants. A parser version by itself cannot spend or invalidate every role. |

Unify discovery and validation access waits through existing typed source-check/progress records, not per-controller blind retries. Save a `wait_scope` describing actual host/provider, transport/session/access identity, affected account/location where relevant and reason. A shared Google access challenge can cover Google supplier/Maps reads through that same affected session; an Amazon host challenge can cover its three routes. One supplier company's host failure does not pause all supplier websites. A route-only selector/layout defect need not pause unrelated lists or a healthy different permitted reader. Never assume all transports are healthy alternatives, or evade a provider-wide restriction by changing wording/account. Saved-byte parsing/reconciliation remains allowed. All collectors consult this scope before launch; use actual Retry-After when provided, otherwise the existing bounded retry policy and named reconnect/parser/config/input trigger. One due recovery probe owns that scope; observing the same unchanged wait is not another attempt or useful loop progress. Diagnostics retain stage, source/request/attempt IDs, safe reason, original proof reference, affected dependencies and next trigger; no secret headers/bodies are printed.

Migrate the **actual receipts/adapters**, not only the wait reader: `create_marketplace_handlers` currently marks429 retryable but omits `failure_scope`, so `_envelope` defaults to a question-only failure. Carry native navigation status/challenge/scope/Retry-After through marketplace and discovery receipts to the shared wait. Keep local parse/import/save errors separate from website access refusal. Likewise join the QC platform guard and programme-local model pause to their appropriate shared scopes. Test first Amazon-search429 followed by list/discovery work and a healthy other site: zero premature same-scope navigation, continued independent work and one eventual due probe.

Freeze a report from its own dependency snapshot, **not all research**. Compute freshness at the full scheduled 09:00 India timestamp, not only `usable_until[:10]` or the rendering date. At activation, use actual availability `max(scheduled_at, server_activated_at)` so a late release cannot use expired support by pretending it appeared earlier. Freeze `priority_support_refs` and `support_valid_until` from the accepted actual one/two-site combination establishing its priority, using existing source-specific validity rules; Python and server recompute that same combination/expiry. Do not mint a new TTL here or omit contrary/stale results from the all-route panels. An optional photograph/old unrelated route does not expire every product. If essential support expires before release, refresh that affected question or select current inventory; never backdate a source. Facts remain honestly observed-at, not a current-stock promise. Before owner activation, validate the exact frozen selection/holds, intended server schedule, current alias/history root and actual authority. Already published immutable historical files remain downloadable when evidence later ages; freshness governs a new recommendation, not erasure of history. An explicitly authorised historical correction preserves its original as-of basis and must not be marketed as newly current; fixing presentation alone does not require recollection. Newly discovered contradictions create an owner-visible correction/replacement question, not automatic publication or silent mutation of an approved packet.

#### E. Choose explicit new packet versions and matched readers

New type/example packets use factual dataset `schema_version:3`, `recommendation_contract:'functional-type-examples-v1'`, existing `fit-10-v1` IDs, and delivery manifest `schema_version:2` with the same recommendation marker and `workbook_contract:'fit-checklist-examples-v2'`. Each product retains its existing concept ID and adds its meaning, exact definition/presentation refs and actual example-membership refs/hashes. Choose the declared fifth sheet **`Example products`** after `Shortlist`, `Evidence`, `Supplier leads`, `Checklist`. Example rows bind concept/example/source IDs, actual title/seller/public link, pack/qualified price, observed date and photo provenance where available; sample range totals are code-derived from compatible rows. Do not require two examples or duplicate supplier-only prices into retail examples.

Update `report_selection`, `report_source_facts`, allocation fingerprints, frozen `report_workflow` inputs, G contract selection, `live_outputs`, workbook/HTML/PDF checks, `delivery_package`, private staging/storage and **both** portal validators for this explicit tuple. G receives only frozen validated facts; it cannot select schemas or calculate totals. Legacy factual1/2, manifest1 and their genuine three/four-sheet contracts remain exact readable branches. Reject unknown/mixed versions instead of inferring schema from sheet count or accepting every layout. New output version is a factual-content change, not permission to redesign UI/UX or edit old packets. Release-index version is independent of packet version: v1/v2 archive migration must preserve actual packet contract and hashes.

#### F. Activation is dependent; code blocks are not six independent releases

1. **Reader-first S1:** enumerate kind/schema consumers, add typed legacy/new readers and local fixtures. Produce a read-only migration inventory with source head/policy hashes, supported mappings and unresolved IDs. Add new definition/question writers only after those consumers accept the mixed fixture; uncertain old IDs remain preserved.
2. **Joined S2/S3:** build physical ownership, bridges, turn/wait logic and AI admission together. Enable renewable discovery only when its actual A/B/C/E/F call paths all carry admission and shared waits. Preparation-only/local replay precedes bounded real-source tests. A disconnected successful extractor is not the fallback until its application adapter/import/provenance/recovery tests pass.
3. **Local S4 plus required S5 readers:** construct new packets in isolated local output namespaces, then add compatible private/server readers and release/history shard support. S4 depends on these S5 portions before new packet staging/activation can be enabled; S5 does not depend on publishing an S4 packet. Storage segmentation can be verified independently against retained canonical bytes. No circular “publish first to migrate history” dependency exists.
4. **Migrate and switch by existing one writer/conditional root:** save source head/financial and release-root generations, write new immutable segments/pages, verify old/new counts/heads/holds/exposure/approvals/withdrawals and exact packet-byte readback. Then switch the local active manifest or conditional remote root. Remote v1→v2 switches the **same authoritative root key**, comparing the exact verified source v1 ETag; a concurrent legacy withdrawal/release invalidates that comparison and must survive reconciliation/rebuilding. Fence legacy writes after the switch; dual-version readers are not two writable roots. Keep old originals readable. The migration receipt binds those inputs, code contracts, verification result and exact switched pointer. Lost switch reply reconciles that pointer first; content-hash orphan pages are harmless and do not grant availability.
5. **Reader compatibility on rollback:** before any switch, the old path remains authoritative. After authoritative v2/v3 writes, disable only the affected new writer on a defect and use compatible readers or repair/replay; do not run old code that ignores new kinds or reset the pointer/history to make it start. Reverting an unreferenced derived index is different from reverting published authority. No automatic retention deletion or duplicate legacy/v2 launch path.
6. **S6 proof:** focused tests cover all new seams and interruption points, then whole connected offline regression, bounded real source/model/restart/private-download checks and measured cohort costs. Distinguish those results. Preserve exact running-request ownership when loading changed code; do not start a second writer or kill a live paid/model job for deployment convenience. After implementation starts, ordinary in-scope repairs/tests continue through these blocks, not routine approval handbacks.

For exposure shards, retain actual activation/scheduled/deactivation times and immutable publication baselines. A future approved issue holds its types; it becomes actually exposed when `max(activated_at, scheduled_at)` has passed without earlier withdrawal, **without needing the local computer to run at09:00**. Once exposed it stays seen even after withdrawal. A future issue withdrawn before any availability may release its confirmed remote hold; loss of remote authority cannot. Alias/history shards and Python allocation use this same server-time rule, not a draft-upload date or an absent local timer event. This closes recurring novelty without promoting every staged draft into publication.

For an unrelated root update, first reconcile any original mutation, read fresh trusted authority and revalidate selected shards/approvals/schedule. If those facts are unchanged, rebase the same intended mutation to the fresh ETag without another fact revision, G call or owner approval merely for changed unrelated metadata. A changed selected hold/alias/status still requires its affected decision. Never blindly replay a possibly committed mutation.

Before accepting an alias/split projection affecting held or already remotely scheduled types, inspect the affected queued entries and their actual availability intervals. If it would let two issues autonomously show the same type as New, resolve those **remote** holds through the existing owner-authorised withdrawal/replacement flow before or with the root transition. Parking only the next local publication cannot stop an already queued release. A draft-only key cannot perform that repair. If owner authority is unavailable, retain the last authoritative mapping, record affected issue IDs and next availability time, and surface that precise queue-conflict action; do not claim the existing queue is safe or silently cancel it. Keep unrelated research/reports moving. Test alias discovery before either availability, between releases and after both; preserve all genuine past exposure rather than reclassify old files. No automatic quarantine/publication authority is inferred from this plan.

#### G. Saved types re-enter actual work and upcoming issues

Current `_unused_reporting_inventory` only carries presently eligible unused IDs; `_review_scopes` does not process reporting-only IDs, and `_register_discovered_products` treats them as already known. `_reporting_eligible` excludes published carryover, while `due_programme` normally excludes a finished origin. Do not keep those exclusions as the implementation of the rolling inventory. Finishing a bounded historical pass is not permanent retirement of its types.

Use direct `refresh_reporting_intake(...)` and `admit_due_inventory_work(...)` helpers in `programme`, with additive typed records in existing `batch_runs`/questions and rebuildable bounded due projections. Preserve the original programme binding, original156 scope, existing task/job IDs and original accounts; do not mutate `reporting_inventory_concept_ids` in an old frozen binding or copy a whole old pool into each new week.

| Handoff | Exact action, ownership and continuation |
|---|---|
| Current reporting intake | At a safe turn boundary, read committed changes since the saved inventory cursor and the actual upcoming unprotected slots. Admit eligible unused New types **and** already accepted meaningful Updated types. Each `reporting-intake-v1` binding stores client/concept/meaning, original owning scope, current eligibility/support dependencies, New or Updated, and the immutable publication baseline/update verdict for Updated. Intake is a non-authoritative projection: recheck source truth, aliases, exposure and holds before allocation. Newly ready older types can join this week; intake itself reserves nothing or launches research. |
| Specific due work | Translate actual pool `needed_fact_work`/`needed_market_work`, supplier-effort requirements, accepted changed dependencies and essential support expiry into one idempotent obligation per subject/purpose/basis. Record original scope, needed fields/refs, intended release, due reason/time and existing question/job/acquisition owner. Use the still-valid original scope where suitable; otherwise attach an explicitly linked narrow current follow-on scope. Reporting-only membership and rediscovery are not reasons to skip needed work. Reuse all unaffected answers. |
| Refresh of a completed source task | Add a due refresh generation linked to the original question/task and expired supporting refs; v2 dispatch calls `claim_source_acquisition` for that generation instead of returning through v1 `_FINAL`. Do not overwrite the completed task/capture or reset its tries/fees. A second consumer joins the pending refresh. An unanswered new useful question still uses normal admission, not a fabricated expiry. |
| Finished origins and fair selection | `due_programme` also consults bounded trigger-ready inventory obligations regardless of the origin's `research_pool_finished` label. Check stored deadlines independently of the committed-change cursor: time passing can make selected support due without another research event. Serve linked obligations through the current fair work turn without reopening old release slots. Waiting obligations retain original ownership/triggers. Rebuild a missing derived cursor from canonical scoped records; never treat missing projection as empty history. |
| Avoid perpetual stock maintenance | Finish an unused type's actual unresolved questions through fair backlog turns. Refresh an already completed type only for a currently intended selection, a bounded replenishment shortlist tied to the next actual release shortfall, or an explicit useful revisit/dependency-change reason. A backlog turn or expiry alone is not a new refresh reason. Do not maintain every unused/published type every seven days or preserve historical downloads through recollection. Retain dormant directions without promising freshness; exploration and repair continue to share fair turns. Updates are optional, actually substantiated and capped, not a target to fill. |

The intake cursor advances only after its canonical admissions/checkpoint are committed; restart applies the same change once. `_reporting_ids`, `_reporting_eligible`, owner summaries and allocation use this typed intake in addition to genuine legacy scopes. A newly conflicting hold/publication/meaning invalidates only its affected admission; protected packets remain unchanged. Keep eligible inventory, repair-needed types, held types and accepted updates as distinct counts, not four names for the same stock. S2/S3 implement this bridge before S4 relies on it.

#### H. Supplier conditions belong to an actual company, workflow and offer

Preserve URL-derived lead/page IDs and original F jobs. Extend accepted supplier projections with `scope_kind` (`company_workflow / product_offer / unclear`), derived `company_ref`/`workflow_ref`, actual `offer_ref`, purchase-unit/pack binding, source-page/quote refs and per-field observation dates. These are refs in existing supplier records, not another company database. A directory hostname or matching trading name is not company identity. Group directory/direct-site placements only when their actual saved evidence supports the same business; uncertain matches remain separate useful leads.

1. Code supplies F the real relevant offer sections and company/workflow sections with explicit ref choices. New scoped assessments use `supplier-scoped-terms-v2` with `research-f-v4`; retain current `supplier-terms-v1`/`research-f-v3` exact legacy readers and the independent `supplier-terms-v2` **display** marker. Do not confuse display and interpretation versions. F chooses scope/refs or Unclear, and explains short applicability/exceptions. It cannot invent company/offer IDs, confirm a neighbour's product or infer a global term from one offer. Update short-reference alias maps, prompt selection, result validation, source-interpretation currentness, supplier projections and packet validators together before new writes.
2. Code validates quote membership and scope/pack/reference relationships before deriving conditions. Replace `_suppliers`' single lead-level MOQ passed to every price with offer-local MOQ/dispatch/price derivation. An explicit applicable company rule can qualify an offer; a product restriction takes precedence or remains visibly conflicting. Keep fees separate from product prices and all meaningful offers inside grouped supplier presentation. Legacy mixed-scope rows retain dated raw statements but cannot acquire an invented offer binding.
3. `current_supplier_terms(...)` selects each accepted field by proved company/workflow or offer, relevant supplied content **including exceptions**, role/model/contract and actual source dates. Exact unchanged interpretation can be reused with an explicit application binding to a verified identical recapture; capture time is not part of the semantic F job key. Changed fee, restriction or offer facts reopen only dependent interpretation/derivation. Unknown company/scope or unverifiable content uses the existing bounded F path, not fuzzy automatic reuse.
4. Apply the existing30-day company-term rule from the actual supporting page capture, never F completion or report preparation. A day0 page interpreted on day40 remains old; a genuinely identical page fetched on day40 may reuse its interpretation with the new original receipt/date. Offer quotes keep their actual dates/conditions. Expired useful leads remain labelled dated leads with scoped follow-up; they do not reject an otherwise suitable type or pretend supply was just checked. Reporting, material-update comparison, both languages, workbook and private validators preserve these same scopes/dates.

No extra supplier-count gate, purchase, contact submission or paid search follows from an unknown field. Real bounded effort with no usable lead keeps the already agreed simple client sentence. Preparation-only failure still does not manufacture completed effort.

#### I. Photo reads, immutable report assets and whole-file size

Connect `product_images` to the physical acquisition owner and wait/recovery rules in26.14A/26.6. An image is supporting material, not a fifteenth market-vote route. The acquisition helper accepts the supporting `public_image_http` purpose without putting it into the14-route demand matrix. Its reusable physical fingerprint binds the actual public URL and byte-affecting options; the original acquisition owner separately tracks attempt generations and due/recovery reasons. Per-example relevance/observation/meaning remain separate application bindings. Save intent before GET, original bytes/receipt before canonical application, and content-hash-deduplicate verified storage. Sharing a URL/hash never proves that two examples are the same product. Consult actual host/access waits and preserve original fetch ownership.

Current `capture_product_image` returns an unchanged failed receipt indefinitely while `_photos` can repeatedly revisit it. Add explicit failure classification and a saved next trigger: retryable transient failure gets one linked due generation under the normal bounded policy; terminal404/invalid bytes parks until a new genuine URL/capture or proved repair. An unchanged tick does not fetch or spin. Saved-byte recovery and another genuinely relevant image remain useful alternatives. Lost known-free read recovery still needs the owned-process exit/saved-recovery proof; no blind concurrent request.

`freeze_report_assets(...)` runs before committing a new report's frozen facts. Verify selected source byte hashes, prepare display copies and copy required checked asset bytes into the issue/revision-owned immutable assets directory. A staged asset receipt binds original image/source/example IDs, original hash, display transform/output hash, exact relative asset ref and packet ownership. Its owner is issue ID + revision and its input is the path-independent asset-plan hash; it cannot recursively contain its own receipt or final packet hash. Source provenance is not rewritten to the report path. The packet's new frozen input/manifest binds that asset receipt. Both language editions use an allowlisted hash-bound resolver for the owned revision, not the continued existence of shared `MEDIA_ROOT`; reject symlinks/path escape/foreign revisions. Freeze the configured logo as a separate hash-bound brand asset too, instead of rereading mutable global `LOGO` during later rendering/stage validation. It is not a product photo or market evidence. Local validators, PDF inspection, backup selection and private staging validate the same contract. Embed verified display bytes in HTML/PDF; do not introduce unauthenticated remote images. Any separately staged asset must be explicitly enumerated/private-validated, not silently added to the client's download set.

In schema3 keep `image_provenance` as original source/example ownership and original byte hash; add a separate `display_asset` with owned relative ref, output hash/size, source hash, transform contract and asset-receipt hash. New local/portal validators must not compare resized display bytes to the original source hash or require the old shared absolute path. Legacy schema1/2 checks remain exact. Normal private delivery keeps its existing PDF(s), workbook, facts and delivery-manifest file set; display bytes travel embedded in the checked documents and receipt metadata travels in checked facts/manifest. No new public asset endpoint or mandatory extra client file is needed.

Use this exact new-packet order: provisional allocation without a hold → verified content-based asset plan/optional omissions → choose or reuse an unprotected revision from factual content + asset-plan hash → copy/resolve its checked owned asset refs → compute final selection/facts bindings → save and read back the exact immutable preparation input → recheck selected source/history/asset dependencies → atomically reserve/replace the exclusive hold → G/render. The saved preparation input alone is not execution or publication authority; the current exact hold is still required before writing. This order closes the real crash gap where a hold could survive without the facts/owned bytes needed to recover it. The asset-plan identity excludes final revision/path; `_report_revision` must not compare a freshly revision-bearing path and create another revision on every tick. A failed copy/optional omission before reserve recomputes that proposal's final bindings first. If facts changed, reconcile/replan only that proposal. An already reserved/frozen interrupted packet recovers its original hold/assets/input; it cannot silently omit an image or refreeze under the same revision. A local replacement atomically swaps the same issue's hold, preserving other issues' protection; uploaded/published authority is unchanged. Test unchanged second-turn reuse, interruption after asset copy/before input and after input/before hold, original legacy hold-without-input recovery, plus loss of the shared logo after its owned copy exists.

Prepare/copy assets with original staged payloads/receipts and a short local stage lock; no networking/model call inside that lock. Before freeze, unavailable optional bytes produce an honest no-photo treatment. After freeze, shared-source removal cannot break an intact owned copy. Missing/corrupt owned bytes first recover the **exact hash** from a verified retained copy/backup. Otherwise retain that revision and select one new unprotected revision with a genuinely bound alternative/no-photo treatment; do not mutate its frozen fields or accept tampering. `_current_report_selection` includes actual selected asset health so it can schedule this replacement rather than repeatedly validating a broken path. Protected uploaded/published packets retain existing owner correction authority; other issues/research continue. No new source/AI call is required solely to change a local asset path.

Individually valid images are not necessarily a valid packet:20×5MiB originals become about133.3MiB after base64 encoding, above the existing32MiB HTML/file limit. Use existing Pillow for a versioned **display-only** deterministic transform: honour orientation, keep full uncropped aspect ratio, handle transparency explicitly and preserve original evidence bytes. Initial tunable defaults: at most one embedded principal photo per selected type, long edge at most1600px, target at most256KiB per display copy, and at most8MiB **actual encoded** embedded images including the logo. Other useful photos retain example/source links and provenance. The target is not a minimum quality/photo requirement or a licence to alter what the product looks like.

Pin `report-display-image-v1` to three transform attempts: orientation-corrected long edge1600/quality85,1200/80, then960/75, never upscaling. Use JPEG for opaque RGB and alpha-preserving WebP for transparent images; if the existing Pillow build lacks that encoder, the same three size steps may use lossless PNG optimisation rather than flattening the product's transparency. Preserve the full image, including existing watermark/context; strip private metadata only from the display copy. Bind encoder/library contract and actual parameters/output hash; replay uses exact saved derivative bytes, not whichever encoder is installed today. Count **every embedded occurrence**, not just unique source hashes, against the aggregate budget. If no useful derivative fits, omit only that optional image before freeze and retain its limitation/source link. Before G, check actual asset/structured-fact/workbook sizes plus bounded allowed-prose headroom; check actual final HTML/workbook/PDF and manifest bounds again before promotion/private staging. Required facts/conditions are never silently truncated. A genuine remaining required-content size error records actual bytes/limit and the affected format-repair action, isolating that packet while other work continues. No uploaded oversized alias or second writing call is a size fallback.

#### J. Writing reuse follows the words' inputs, not every packet byte

`report_narration._frozen_hash` currently places the entire dataset and selection hash into every G context. `presentation_source_revision` deliberately requires exact product facts. Merely adding photo-aware replacement while keeping those cache rules would make the application rewrite every product. Retain that legacy path, but add explicit `research-g-v4` selection for new schema3 packets and `writing-input-v1` projections; unknown contract combinations still fail validation.

Each writing item binds its exact supplied title/benefit/market facts, ten criterion states/reasons/refs, protected limitation, material-update reason, language and source English where relevant, plus prompt/model/runner contract. Do not include images/paths, unrelated products, whole-packet hash, allocation revision or root generation **unless actually supplied as meaningful writing input**. Keep issue identity/output schema and allowed placeholders/refs in the projection. Packet/selection/asset hashes remain exact validation/audit links outside the semantic writing key; this is not weakening packet integrity. Give the generic title one stable per-issue/language/contract semantic key, with actual supplied title facts included if any, rather than a new key for every revision.

`reuse_narrative_items(...)` in the existing narration/workflow path first recovers original accepted response rows and revalidates them against the current identical writing projection/output contract. Save an explicit reuse receipt binding original job/item/accepted-row hash, projected-input hash and new packet/revision. Never relabel an original job as newly executed or copy a whole older narrative on trust. A legacy accepted row can be reused only through an explicit verified projection adapter with exactly comparable supplied facts/limitations/output slots; otherwise keep original legacy recovery and admit the genuinely needed new interpretation normally. A photograph-only replacement with unchanged writing inputs makes zero G calls.

Assemble all product/title rows against the new exact packet only after each is validated; then validate complete narratives and language parity under schema3. If one supplier limitation changes, only its affected writing item and dependent Hinglish translation reopen. Hinglish projection includes the corresponding accepted English row, not the entire English edition. A changed English row therefore invalidates its translation, while a photo/path-only change does not.

Implement that dependency at the actual entry points: new v4 `make_narrative_input` accepts a requested item subset plus verified original-job English-row refs, instead of requiring every English product. `generate_report_narratives` recovers validated English title/product rows from saved Jobs, admits missing English items and independently queues Hinglish items whose own English source is ready. A failed unrelated English row does not delay translation of healthy rows; it still prevents falsely declaring the entire English edition complete. Save/use original per-row acceptance refs before dependent translation, not a synthetic completed edition. Final manifests retain all required complete languages; later Hinglish-only delivery may use its internally accepted English sources without adding an unrequested client PDF. Keep legacy full-edition behavior in its exact reader branch.

All new/corrective G launches still pass shared job admission; malformed output uses the existing bounded one-item repair path extended explicitly to v4, not unlimited retries or invented template recommendations. Save/cache reuse and accepted siblings continue while new writing waits. Test one failed English item with all other English/Hinglish items completing and being reused on restart; the final package stays incomplete until the actual missing item and translation finish.

## 27. Current implementation and verification

Historical implementation checkpoints from9–10 October, retained for their foundation contracts, migrations and exact proof boundaries. References to “current”, “pending” or “remaining” inside this section are relative to those checkpoints, not today's position. Later operating changes/loading are recorded in the [implementation record](../../implementation_2026-10-11.md); the sole current completion summary is [Progress](../../../project/pj-tracker.md). Keep new execution narratives out of this specification.

### 27.1 Reader-first foundations and joined local execution

| Implemented seam / files | Actual behavior and upstream/downstream boundary |
|---|---|
| `research_records.py`; existing paid planner/dispatch/operations/coverage/summary readers | Positively recognise the real untyped paid-task contract and the separate new record kinds. A prepared v2 question, definition, example or scope verdict cannot accidentally become a paid request, legacy route feedback or market vote. Old paid uncertainty and actual baseline ownership remain readable. Unknown explicit kinds do not enter paid execution. |
| `functional_types.py` | Read explicit accepted functional meaning over the existing concept ID, or an honestly labelled legacy compatibility view. Acceptance freezes actual original observations and checks current meaning/revision at commit. Meaning and presentation hashes are separate: a title-only change does not create a new product type. Narrowing a legacy description does not automatically inherit every old example; actual child-level Same/Different/Unclear decisions bind unchanged source records. |
| Example membership and legacy correction boundary | Memberships retain actual child, original observation, date, seller/offer facts and accepted meaning; replay verifies the saved binding rather than silently trusting an occupied key. Existing old-schema merge correction cannot newly merge an affected accepted functional definition without its required review. Exact previously completed replay still works.
