Full versioned alias/report/history adoption is S4 work, not claimed complete by this boundary. |
| `research_questions.py` | Prepare actual concept-meaning or accepted catalogue-direction questions without collection. Direction questions do not invent a concept. Logical questions, full physical requests and saved-byte parse fingerprints are separate; programme/week/parser changes do not manufacture another physical request. Preserve actual wording, date, list node, location and request bounds. Parent-question and dependency bindings are validated again at commit. |
| `research_scope.py` | Explicit source-bound catalogue relationship and scoped evidence judgments bind genuine references, original request/date context, actual selected field values and correction history. Relationship, narrow/broad/offer scope and support are separate. This API does not grant a market vote merely because a review was saved. Existing market/fit/report consumers still need their planned new-contract adoption before production activation. |
| `source_acquisitions.py`; opt-in installed marketplace handlers | Global physical ownership joins actual discovery and validation for the four direct marketplace routes. Expand the full transmitted request before ownership/admission; adopt verified actual legacy intent/capture with its original ID, date and bounded artifact roots. Saved replay costs no callback; a proved never-entered original needs a new callback slot before service. Uncertain originals remain original-only. Ownership alone still says `launch_authority:false`; actual canonical caller intent and dispatch-stage verification supply execution permission. Legacy unknown fees are not relabelled zero until a verified direct capture provides separate proof. Other source classes, general shared waits, explicit overdue caller triggers and changed-parser revision remain pending; no live activation is claimed. |
| `work_turns.py`; `research_ai.Jobs.run` | Persist shared turn counters in existing `batch_runs`; reserve model origin/service and job metadata in one canonical commit before original intent/execution. Reuse/recover saved answers first. Full original carried payloads count under current-turn service; no unbound legacy escape for managed work. Local limits are not a provider outage. The original result files, job keys, execution uncertainty and signed-in subscription accounting remain unchanged. |
| `source_coverage.py`; `catalogue_discovery.py` | When explicitly bound to a turn, reserve callback and source intent together. Discovery and validation share the allowance; recovery callbacks count, pure saved-result application does not. A missing discovery reconciler preserves original uncertainty and blocks only that family's fallback rather than rewriting it as unstarted. Existing unmanaged v1 execution stays compatible. This does not yet install renewable waves or shared host waits. |
| `programme.advance_programme_turn(...)`; `advance_ready_programme_turn(...)`; existing CLI | The actual existing dispatcher executes one saved bounded round under one writer from selection through close. An interrupted turn resumes its counters; safe boundaries admit the latest actual due week and rotate by the last served sequence without cloning all old inventory. Advisory readiness cannot authorise execution. The existing command's explicit `--cooperative-turns` connects repeated ready rounds; unflagged invocation and installed task remain unchanged. Exact saved marketplace files and original A/B job ownership can wake waiting work; retry timestamps/diagnostics alone cannot spin rounds. Renewable waves and general all-route wake/host waits remain pending. |
| `product_images.py`; opt-in cooperative photo stage | Two consumers share one exact physical photo GET while retaining separate source/subject bindings. Canonical intent and actual photo allowance are saved together before a read. One atomic saved original receipt/body recovers a failed canonical save or missing shared media without another GET. Legacy verified success keeps its original date; old uncertain failures are not bypassed by a schema change. Actual HTTP failures retain their status. Destination links are checked before intent/GET. Existing v1 readers remain compatible; generation-based transient recovery, host waits and frozen report-owned display assets remain pending. |
| `scripts/inspect_functional_types.py`; `ResearchMemory.snapshot_with_provenance()` | Read verified research events and the accepted catalogue without rebuilding indexes, writing overlays, searching, analysing with AI or publishing. Pair exact state with its verified source head/event count and policy/catalogue hashes. Return actual mappings and unresolved records rather than treating an unreadable history as an empty inventory. |

### 27.2 Read-only inventory, not a new research result

The 9 October read-only migration inspection found **171 retained concept identities:164 active and7 inactive**. All164 active identities currently use their explicit legacy compatibility view; **zero newly accepted functional definitions** were written to production. The164 active identities comprise23 Baby,50 Home and Decor,64 Home and Kitchen and27 Travel and Outdoor. They are types already encountered, not all report-ready recommendations.

The effective accepted catalogue contained **260 usable research directions**. Those are places to look next, not260 searches already performed and not a guaranteed number of new products. The two counts are not multiplied into a runway. The inspection found333 resolved source children linked to active identities, plus219 unresolved source-child records, with zero missing referenced concept IDs and zero new catalogue-scope overlays. These two child counts are not asserted to exhaust all retained children. A source child is an interpretation of one encountered source item;219 such records are not219 unfinished products. No new production definition/question/scope migration or provider/model call occurred during this inspection.

### 27.3 Review findings addressed during implementation

| Concrete finding | Repair and required regression |
|---|---|
| New narrowed meaning could inherit legacy examples merely through shared concept ID. | Only unchanged legacy meaning or explicit hash-bound child judgments admit membership. Test narrowing, title-only replay, missing decisions and changed association. |
| Occupied membership keys and concurrent old merge/parent changes could be trusted or missed. | Verify current saved record/hash and recheck real dependencies inside the short commit. Preserve exact already-applied historical replay. Test actual concurrent association, meaning, parent and merge changes. |
| Shared limits could reset on restart, multiply per scope, lose accepted siblings or be labelled a model outage. | Canonical origin/service/callback/item counters, execution-leaf admission and per-item saved recovery. Test repeated scopes, restart, carried original batches, mixed reusable/waiting items and no fake programme pause. |
| Tiny tuned source allowances could reserve every slot for one lane and deadlock both. | At two slots give one to each ready lane; at one slot alternate by saved programme-turn sequence. At normal settings keep the planned exploration allocation and donation. |
| Missing discovery recovery callback changed original uncertainty into not-started, allowing duplicate launch or premature fallback. | Retain original attempt/dispatch proof, record a named recovery wait, stop only that family's fallback and continue other families. Test repeated advance and later original saved-only recovery. |
| Discovery's saved-result cache compared only words/department and could treat a10-card read as the full30-card request. | Compare the complete actual physical request; preserve distinct country/date/filter/bounds. Unsupported sparse old envelopes are retained, not silently declared equivalent. Four exact-scope regressions cover original reuse, changed requests, sparse history and stale/future/failed captures. |
| Turn creation/closure could fall outside the actual sole-writer window. | Factor `_advance_programme_locked` and retain the public legacy wrapper. The explicit turn entry owns one guard through open/round/close. Tests cover busy owner, interrupted dispatch/close, exact counter/result reuse and guard/context release after unexpected errors. |
| A copied old never-started receipt could clear later original execution uncertainty; parse could commit after original bytes changed. | Bind zero-process proof to the latest intent/sequence/ownership token. Verify original receipt/artifact/capture/stage dependencies again at short parse commit; unchanged metadata is checked without a large read under the financial lock. Independent failure-injection regressions exercise both cases before activation. |
| A damaged lookup fingerprint could hide an owner using a retained legacy acquisition ID and allow a second original. | Verify every positively typed owner's actual request/binding before trusting its lookup fingerprint to exclude it. The retained-legacy-ID regression confirms that the damaged record raises its named integrity error and creates no second owner. |
| A damaged definition/verdict subject could hide the latest correction, or a predecessor definition ID could be trusted without verification. | Verify the typed envelope hash and deterministic original binding before filtering by subject, and verify every selected predecessor. Unrelated opaque damaged records do not become automatic evidence. Independent routing-corruption tests cover both simple and self-consistently rehashed bad bindings. |
| Caller intent could save just before global dispatch-intent failure, stranding a definitely unentered marketplace read. | Resume the same reserved original only when zero actual dispatch stages prove the collector never entered; reserve a fresh caller slot and verify its exact canonical full request immediately before dispatch. No generation reset, refund or uncertain legacy adoption. |
| Completed-loop readiness missed an original marketplace file or an A reply owned by its listing ID rather than concept ID. | Bounded, non-authorising original-file hints and validated work-turn job admissions wake the actual owner. Regression tests recover each saved result with zero new source/model executions. |
| Retry-only journal changes could advertise useful work indefinitely. | Keep legacy behavior intact; cooperative semantic progress excludes retry/diagnostic-only fields and respects real future retries. Missing-original probes settle instead of spinning new rounds. |
| Photo bytes could save before a canonical result, or separate consumers could download the same address again. | One atomic original body/receipt plus a shared physical owner, with independent consumer commits and actual execution-leaf allowance. Restart and missing-media tests recover exact bytes; broken destination-link tests make zero reads and save no intent. |

#### Directional narrowing implementation closure

An explicit `functional-lineage-v1` record is a reviewed narrowing from an existing parent concept to an existing accepted child, not another registry, a same-type alias or a new market claim. `prepare_lineage_input` pins both actual accepted definition records and genuine associated grounding observations/associations. `accept_functional_lineage` requires the current bindings, reviewer, practical narrowing distinctions and a short-commit recheck. Literal equal use/mechanism supports the simple deterministic route; differently worded meaning requires an explicit operator judgment and reason, not a token-similarity assumption. A genuinely different function has normal new-type review, not a split exemption.

`lineage_projection` keeps equivalence members separate from directional ancestors. A child's history includes parent exposure; publishing only a child does not expose its parent or sibling automatically. `hold_overlap` blocks actual equivalent/ancestor/descendant queued collisions, not siblings merely sharing an ancestor. Original definitions/events/IDs/publication intervals and source/fit/supplier ownership remain unchanged. Original accepted lineage persists for novelty after later wording/definition revisions. Cycles, stale input, held collisions and mismatched original hashes have exact local outcomes; unrelated work continues. Allocation, report novelty, research novelty pages and portable private/server readers now implement these same directional rules locally. Current deployment and actual owner adoption remain separate proof layers.

## 28. Operating repair, recording and visible progress

### 28.1 Objective, precedence and evidence boundary

The 10 October request adds an explicit audit of recording, automatic health checking, the ability to follow an action back to its origin, and understandable whole-project progress. It also includes the known search-limit/ordering defect, nine rejected assessment answers, website-versus-local timing and quiet launch. These are part of the existing nonvisual programme, not another scheduler, database, paid monitoring project or reason to abandon unfinished research.

The [plain-language operating audit](../../../reviews/2026-10-10_programme_operation_audit.md) records the inspected evidence, eight-area/32-check progress calculation and estimates. It is an audit/status view; this section owns implementation. At that inspection 18/32 checks are met, not 56% of measured coding hours or a claim that unattended operation is ready. All N01–N25 items remain mapped and open items remain open. The final connected run is still required even when a substantial part of the code passes local tests.

The audit changed documentation only. The following seven repairs are **planned, not implemented by this audit**. Earlier section27 checkpoints remain dated evidence, not current completion claims. The existing code's `meaningful_progress` comparison occurs after a whole round returns; it does not currently detect the observed intra-stage preparation walk. The native Cloudflare spending warning is not a research-progress monitor.

The source stage has not returned, so later independent work and end-of-round duties cannot run. | `source_coverage.py`, `source_acquisitions.py`, `work_turns.py`, `programme.py`: distinguish a nonmutating saved-result/readiness inspection from acquisition reservation. ### 28.3 Minimum action record and plain-language view

Do not log every internal file read, copy complete public pages into several logs, or ask an LLM to narrate routine states. Log each meaningful action, decision, failure and continuation boundary. Original captures, prompts, replies and money records remain in their current private stores; diagnostics reference them.

The small diagnostics record should carry:

1. **Identity:** schema version, execution/run ID, exact process identity, loaded code version, programme/turn and action/task/job/attempt IDs, affected product/source/report and original evidence references where applicable. Machine-generated identities are code-owned.
2. **Timing:** actual UTC observation/start/end, monotonic elapsed duration, phase and last useful-result time. The owner's view formats India time. Old source observation dates remain old; a later parse does not make the source fresh.
3. **Outcome:** started/completed/empty/skipped/failed/deferred/uncertain; reason and plain-language explanation; expected/actual phase; confirmed or suspected cause. Missing root-cause evidence is recorded as unknown, not guessed to be a website refusal.
4. **Continuation:** what was saved, affected waiting item, next allowed retry/trigger, whether a permitted fallback was used, what runs next and whether independent work is available. Do not print “healthy work continues” unless it was actually reached or is identified as a future action.
5. **Money certainty:** direct/paid classification, actual proved dispatch and result state, original account/acquisition reference where needed, known charge/hold/unknown separately and existing AI usage receipt reference. A counter or token count is not an invoice.

Use an allowlist of bounded diagnostic fields. Never include credentials, authorization headers/cookies, private account data, client details, raw command environments or full arbitrary exception text. Link to public-research originals only within private operator context. Preserve original errors/results rather than patching them to look successful. Rotate/segment diagnostic files without silently deleting original evidence; any future deletion/retention policy needs its own owner authority. Keep the current status small and inexpensive; do not reread thousands of captures to update one sentence.

The owner-facing view should say things such as “Checking Amazon for sink caddies,” “The page did not answer in time; moving to the next product,” “The answer was saved, but could not yet be applied,” or “No useful result has finished since [time]; the program is still preparing searches.” It must not expose internal role letters, binding/hash language or unexplained denominators as its main explanation. Project progress uses the eight numbered areas and explicit acceptance checks; product/source counts stay separately named with their own denominator and observation time.

### 28.4 Continuation rules and practical limits

| Situation | Required behavior |
|---|---|
| One website refuses, times out or needs sign-in. | Save its actual phase/result. Use a previously approved, genuinely independent permitted route if available; otherwise defer that source and continue another ready source/product. Do not evade a provider-wide restriction. |
| The current search/model allowance is spent. | Do not prepare more chargeable work or refresh the allowance by restarting. Apply bounded saved work, move to other eligible stages, close the original finite turn normally and use the next genuine turn's policy. |
| One answer, parse or image fails. | Record the exact affected item and reason. Perform only an applicable bounded correction/recovery, then move on. Do not hold every report/product behind it. |
| A transport may have run but its reply is missing. | Reconcile that original action or defer it as uncertain. Other work continues; a second request is not the default fallback. |
| A report is short or its files fail validation. | Keep that issue private and incomplete, record the exact shortfall and continue independent research/other issues. Do not pad it or publish it as complete. |
| Status/log writing fails. | Do not recursively log the logger error, discard business originals or stop unrelated work. Use a bounded local fallback notice; mark the visibility gap on the next safe successful write. |
| Shared history cannot be trusted or a write cannot be saved. | Do not launch an action whose identity/funding cannot be recorded or publish untrusted results. Preserve the fault and any independent safe read-only/recovery work. Do not describe missing evidence as successful completion. |
| No eligible work is ready anywhere. | Save the precise waits and consumed allowances, exit the busy loop, and resume through the existing schedule on a real reply, deadline or input change. Waiting is not project completion or permission to fabricate progress. |

No blanket second-model checker, hosted monitoring stack, new paid API, global shutdown policy, second research loop or invented backup destination is required. The owner explicitly prefers continuing unaffected work. Built-in exact money/identity/privacy rules still govern only the actions they protect.

### 28.5 Implementation sequence and verification

The previously approved implementation remains continuous; the following are dependency blocks, not new approval stops:

1. Reproduce R01/R02 through the real shared resolver and installed callback paths using controlled local inputs. Add the small R04/R05 recording hooks first where they make the repair observable; preserve current research/accounting originals.
2. Repair allowance ordering and bounded return, website-versus-local timing, quiet native admission/output and durable status/errors together. Review actual process ownership before any necessary targeted reload; do not stop WSL or other projects, remove locks, clear counters, or repeatedly launch a new writer. A reload is not a completed research result.
3. Resolve the nine original answer cases through R03 and validate the revised input/output/renderer policy through fit, selection, workbook/PDF and compatible website readers. Future bad responses must use the same bounded mechanism; no manual corrections to delivered files.
4. Continue current research across the original and follow-on pools, retain exact source failures/no matches and finish relevant supplier/photo effort. Revisit stage fairness after the budget repair; do not impose an all-source-success gate or wait for globally idle research before creating a stable issue.
5. Build the first two updated bilingual private sets, inspect them, upload/download unchanged, and finish the incremental backup/restore and recorded-cost acceptance.
6. Demonstrate a scheduled wake during an existing run, interrupted restart with original allowances, the next fresh research round and an all-waiting round that returns cleanly. Update the same eight-area checklist only when its named evidence is met.

Verification must include the following connected failures, not merely separate helper tests:

| Case | Required result |
|---|---|
| Allowance exhausted with hundreds of fresh tasks followed by a saved result. | No new reservation/dispatch; bounded local recovery; later stages reached; cursor retained. |
| Shared source reservation exists but transport never began. | Exact original continuation when allowed, not deletion or a new paid identity. |
| Shared source reply arrives after an interruption. | Original saved reply applied once with its original date/cost; no repeat request. |
| Local save is slower than website deadline. | Local-save duration/cause distinguished from transport timeout; valid atomic evidence retained. |
| Actual website or child process hangs. | Owned transport deadline/cleanup recorded; next independent item executes. |
| Estimated label missing; supplied quantity or reasonable estimate present. | The new explicit policy and renderer handle the basis; no needless LLM call just to add a label. |
| Wrong description link, unsupported fact or foreign returned item. | Original preserved, precise item diagnosis and bounded current correction/review, never silent hash substitution. |
| One of nine corrections remains invalid. | It is individually deferred with a reason; the other eight and unrelated research continue. |
| Event count rises but no useful result completes. | Status reports activity without claiming useful progress; ordering/deadline violation produces a visible diagnostic. |
| Model is legitimately still running; remote execution uncertain. | Honest busy/uncertain status; no duplicate call or invented website refusal. |
| Second scheduled wake, stale status, missing logger destination or disabled Windows history. | Single writer retained, quiet launch and project-owned records; no dependence on global task-history settings. |
| Restart between report languages, upload, backup parts and next round. | Exact immutable files, original counters/provenance and incremental backup continuation retained. |

Focused new regressions precede the connected existing suites. Final code-version-stable local checks and actual normal-run/private-file observations remain separate proof. Do not rerun the synthetic2,000-issue experiment merely to display more test counts unless affected history/allocation behavior requires it.

Review the completed repair against the seven findings, all N01–N25 requirements and the plain-language owner view. The engineering-review skill supports this small extension to existing stores and task. Its generic additional monitor/automatic whole-program kill gates are deliberately not applied because they conflict with the approved continuation requirements. Record real remaining external/owner-only dependencies precisely; the current repair block has no need for a new provider, paid plan, client email or subjective layout approval.

### 28.6 Connected execution record — 11 October

The [connected implementation record](../../implementation_2026-10-11.md) now records the allowance/cursor, phase timing, practical-answer validation, code-owned functional response links, durable operations log, current-status and admission-before-warmup changes. Its local tests and original-answer readback are implementation evidence, not completion of actual research, the two updated private report sets or backup/restart acceptance. The seven repair findings above retain their required finish evidence. Do not reset originals or replace the installed launcher to resume this work.

**Newest owner verification boundary:** continue constructive implementation, delegated review and normal private research/report work without routine approval stops. Use focused checks only. Do not run local or hosted release gates, or another broad full-suite run, before regrouping. Earlier completed suite results remain dated evidence and are not proof for later changes. Opening the existing owner Google sign-in is approved; account settings, client publication and paid-upgrade boundaries remain unchanged.

**Fresh writing during the functional-report transition:** the connected cooperative programme must not spend new G executions on unfinished legacy packets merely because the updated selection has fewer than ten ready types. Preserve the old files and exact saved writing, and recover locally when a complete saved edition or a genuine newer checked hold is actionable. Direct fresh writing to the updated functional packets. This is a migration execution policy, not a change to old recommendation facts, prompt identities or published bytes. Standalone legacy package commands retain their existing default behavior.

Pass the explicit boolean `allow_fresh_legacy_model=False` from the cooperative report path; retain the actual runner and its identity. The shared pure `saved_legacy_narratives` collector validates original requests, item links, saved results/completions and any already-existing finite correction. It does not dispatch, create an intent/reservation, open a closed retry or write a new problem. Unsent old prose waits for new functional work; already-sent uncertain or failed work retains its exact diagnosis. Execution and readiness must use this same distinction. Exclude suppressed legacy G work from future-turn writing readiness and closed-allowance continuation, but do not rewrite an already-open turn's original readiness, policy or used/reserved counters.

Partial old rows alone are not a reason to wake. A complete cached English edition with missing Hinglish can be rendered locally once; after its recorded HTML/PDF sizes and hashes agree, it must settle instead of starting empty rounds. A genuine local workbook/render/file failure keeps its ordinary retry and exact reason even if Hinglish is still missing. Corrupt cached bytes remain actionable for local recovery. Functional G writing, genuine functional continuation and unrelated ready research keep their existing normal execution/admission paths. Focused real isolated job/turn/package fixtures and independent review must precede safe activation; this policy alone proves neither a new actual report nor a current private download.

**Known pending company originals must not spend fresh callback slots.** The installed company-only pure readiness hook may declare `company_original_wait` only for the exact canonical reconciling task, acquisition and request hash. It must verify that the original `company_inputs.json`, `company_pass.json` and `company_deadline.json` are all absent, and that there is no separately proved reserved-but-unstarted continuation. Normal execution checks this bound marker after current-target validation and staged-reply precedence, but before callback reservation. It records a waiting item with `actual_attempt=false`, preserves the original task, generation and counters, and continues to the next useful item. A generic `ready=false`, a custom hook or a foreign/unbound marker is not authority to suppress a handler. Integrity errors keep their existing treatment.

Exact outer-file metadata joins the existing leaf/native-original wake signal even when the pure hook receives an already-verified state. A real saved outer pass or deadline remains a local-recovery candidate; actual collection still validates its unchanged original contents and all bindings. A staged reply recovers with no new callback even at a spent allowance. A genuinely proved unstarted continuation still uses ordinary fresh admission and the same original acquisition. Mere input-file presence, a partial saved row, or a waiting original is not completed work. Do not create the missing input, force a retry, refund a previously spent slot or claim that an unchecked product was absent. The exact isolated missing-original, healthy-next-item, late-file, deadline, staged, changed-target, custom-handler and continuation cases must pass before activation.

**Avoid repeated pure preparation without hiding new state.** `resolve_query_profile` builds its current input once and passes it to a private current-profile validator in the same decision. The public saved-profile reader continues to build and validate its own input. One supplier-application read resolves the current wording lazily once after concept, meaning and description match, and individually validates every original application, search, response, question and effort. Nothing is persisted or shared across state reads, later invocations, launch admission or model calls. Changed meanings and damaged sibling originals must still be refused. These small reductions are not proof of a measured whole-round speedup.

**An unchanged incomplete supplier reply is not new work.** The installed supplier resolver must validate the exact original bytes, current parser, meaning/query-profile applications, caller request and current target descriptions before considering a no-op. After genuine due-access recovery has had priority, compare the fully normalized candidate envelope, including the union of all target evidence references, with that caller's validated latest canonical source receipt and applied result. Only an exact unchanged already-applied pending original may return a waiting result before the outer caller is rewritten. Preserve `supplier_original_pending`, `reconcile_required`, the original acquisition, counters and receipt; absence of accepted exit proof does not establish whether an old worker is running now. A different caller, changed target/profile/meaning, changed original/parser or genuine late completion follows normal saved-result application without starting another transport.

An unapplied staged reply must defeat this no-op explicitly: selecting a staged callback earlier in the controller does not by itself bypass the later resolver. Preserve a subsequent relevance refinement only when its exact canonical relevance record, task identity, current descriptions, original receipt references and actual evidence validate. Do not roll a finished relevance decision back to interpretation-pending merely by replaying unchanged partial bytes, and do not preserve an arbitrary unproved target edit. Isolated regressions must reproduce both the duplicate caller writes and actual refinement overwrite before repair, then cover unchanged replay, changed proofs/targets, staged recovery, late completion and due-access recovery. These are local implementation checks, not additional supplier searches or accepted recommendations.

A genuine parser upgrade must not collide with the first saved supplier-search check. Keep its original identity and bytes when the check is absent or exactly unchanged. Only after validating the same acquisition/original, both immutable parser files, a strictly newer current parser version, the exact historical response and its application binding, and the reconstructed original check may `_parser_search_check` produce a new identity from the original check ID and the current parser-proof hash. Validate the historical record/file fingerprints again when committing. Keep the original search, effort and application records; current supplier readers must accept the new checked application and skip its genuinely superseded response. A changed old check, changed parser bytes, an unrelated identity conflict or a parser downgrade is not upgrade authority. Regression proof includes authentic saved parser2 recovery under current parser3, a later parser upgrade, preservation of old bytes, tamper refusal and a settled no-op on the next unchanged replay. None of these local reparses start another website read or count as a newly confirmed supplier.

## 29. Owner desktop and tray launcher — 11 October

The owner's newest priority explicitly adds a small Windows graphical control, not report/website redesign. It must open through a desktop shortcut with a proper icon, minimize into the notification tray instead of remaining in the taskbar, restore on one left-click, expose Start/Stop/Exit, disable Start while on and Stop while off, and leave no command/PowerShell window running.

**Implemented and installed:** `scripts/windows/DropshipperDesktop.cs` builds a native .NET Framework Windows GUI executable, with `DropshipperDesktop.manifest` and the seven-size package/research icon embedded. `Build-Dropshipper.ps1` compiles locally without installing packages and replaces the executable only after successful compilation. `Install-Dropshipper.ps1` validates the exact existing owner task, preserves its original XML, updates only its action/working directory, compares every remaining setting on Windows readback, and creates the matching desktop shortcut. It does not add a task, buy credits or start research. Existing task flags are copied into the validated, non-secret sidecar settings rather than expanded.

| Connection | Exact behavior and failure rule |
|---|---|
| Desktop and tray | One per-owner mutex and restore event retain one window. Minimize and X hide; a single left-click restores. Right-click offers the same controls and logs. Exit requests Stop before removing the icon and closing. Windows owns whether the icon is in its overflow area. |
| Start and saved intent | `desktop_research_control.py start` uses the existing `set_programme_service_state`; no second on/off store exists. The native app starts its own invisible `--worker` entry, which invokes the existing programme with the preserved flags. |
| Stop and process ownership | The original owner pause is persisted before signalling. `/proc` matching requires this exact project cwd, actual Python script argument (including the launcher's `-u` prefix) and execution option. PID/start ticks and a kernel pidfd are rechecked before SIGINT. Shell snippets, other projects, old/reused PIDs and preparation-only commands are excluded. No blanket WSL/Python shutdown or spending reset. |
| Stop while waiting for a model | `run_programme.py` selects `CodexRunner(..., stop_on_interrupt=True)` without changing its saved job identity, request or model configuration. Owned-child cleanup and interruption records are retained, but KeyboardInterrupt/SystemExit propagate through `Jobs.run` to end this programme rather than being consumed as an ordinary recoverable item. A complete saved answer can be reused on restart; deadlines still retain their existing recovery behavior. Diagnostic-save or cleanup-proof failures cannot swallow the owner's interruption or fabricate a completed child-cleanup receipt. Other independent CLI callers retain the prior default policy. |
| Actual stopped state | Local status reads the small existing switch and actual owned processes, not research history. Running means a process exists, not useful progress. If a process has not exited, show Stopping and do not allow a conflicting Start or claim a successful Exit. A connection/validation failure shows an explicit control error. |
| Windows schedule | The same 15-minute/sign-in task now calls the GUI executable's invisible worker entry. `UseShellExecute=false` and `CreateNoWindow=true` prevent a console. The worker checks the saved pause and serializes admission before launching; an existing original research process is adopted, not duplicated. Scheduled checks while stopped return without research. |
| Records/recovery | Small local action receipts and drained worker stdout/stderr live in `data/operations/desktop`. Original evidence, open acquisition identities, funds and report revisions are preserved. Interrupted remote execution can remain uncertain and must follow original-receipt recovery. These launcher records are not the whole R04/R05 observability implementation. |

The controlled tests and final review exposed four real integration mistakes: gratuitously quoted WSL option switches, process identification that did not allow the launcher's Python `-u` argument, model-interruption handling that could consume an owner's Stop, and an external Windows close request being classified as `TaskManagerClosing` instead of `UserClosing`. All four were corrected and covered by regression checks before acceptance. Window-close requests hide; Windows shutdown and explicit application Exit remain real exits. The installed driver also uses the actual Windows accessibility action for these owner-drawn buttons, retains dated results with the exact executable fingerprint, and records an exact phase plus an attempted local Stop on test failure. It does not invent a successful test or leave deliberately test-started work running without a cleanup attempt.

The actual installed acceptance driver passed all 15 checks, including stopping the previous visible research run, actual Start/Stop, disabled buttons, tray/taskbar behavior, duplicate-shortcut restore, X, Exit, a real scheduled wake while stopped, icon assignment and no visible research command window. Independent fake-backend native tests exercise the tray click handler, control failure/recovery and console-free executable without provider calls. Python tests cover the saved switch, real cost-free child lifecycle, `-u` ownership, PID reuse, paid-option refusal and original-worker adoption. Existing programme/cooperative/schedule regressions remain distinct from whole-programme acceptance. Receipts are under `data/operations/desktop`; the [operator guide](../../windows_desktop_launcher.md) gives normal use, build and retest instructions.

The launcher's original controlled test left the owner window open and research stopped. That dated test state is not a later owner Stop; use [Progress](../../../project/pj-tracker.md) for the latest verified operation, and the [launcher guide](../../windows_desktop_launcher.md) for normal controls and retesting.

## 30. Current source routes after Plan R10 (15 October)

This section owns the current route set. It supersedes the route lists and route-specific requirements in sections 5, 19, 20, 22.3, 25 and 26.4 wherever they differ; the state dimensions of section 5 and the recovery rules of sections 21–25 still apply to every route below. Built on branch `r10` (commits `60fffb0`, `4707231`, `6630cbc`, `42be726`); loading and live proof are W13 of [Plan R10](../../../project/plan-2026-10-14-speed-sources-and-selection.md). Implemented details, wait codes and settings files are in the [implementation record, R10](../../implementation_2026-10-11.md#r10-plan-r10-sources-crowding-check-product-map-and-spend-watch-1415-october).

| Route id | Source group | Role now | Access and limits |
|---|---|---|---|
| `meta-ads` | meta | Heading discovery (the one search discovery route), per-type market check, and the advertiser-page and rising-query reads of wider discovery | Free direct Ad Library read in the owned hidden browser; no account. |
| `meta-ads-depth` | meta | Paid follow-up for a type whose free read left Meta Insufficient with an ad lacking advertiser identity or start date; counted inside the single Meta site and report row | Free monthly Apify allowance only; reserve-before-spend; US$0.50 protected buffer. |
| `amazon-search`, `amazon-bestsellers`, `flipkart-search` | marketplace | **Crowding check only** (D4): per-type reads whose reviewed relevant listings set the crowding level; no discovery. They still count as market sites in `opportunity-priority-v2` until the owner's ranking design (W6 selection, W7). | Free direct reads; parser v2 card facts from saved first pages. |
| `google-trends` | trends | Per-type market check; rising related queries feed discovery | Signed-in research browser only; 90 s spacing, 200 a day; proof gate after each sign-in. |
| `blinkit`, `zepto`, `instamart` | quickcommerce | Per-type paid check with admission (checklist passed, crowding Low or Medium); neutral context, never discovery | Provider API only (one credit per search); Mumbai first, Delhi NCR and Bengaluru after a relevant Mumbai result; 30-day no-repeat, 7-day refresh for issue types; free balance check before each batch. Requires `--funded-quickcommerce-fallback` with `--cooperative-turns`. |
| `deodap`, `baapstore` | dropship-platforms | Discovery from list items; per-type presence fact (not a market vote until the ranking design); supplier lead | Public Shopify feeds; 5 s spacing, 1,500 a day; full catalogue weekly, lists daily. |
| `dropdash` | dropship-platforms | Same roles as above, from a sample of the catalogue and list pages | Signed-in research browser; read-only allow-list; 30 s spacing, 300 a day; plan or membership walls wait. |
| `indiamart` | supplier | Supplier search; mapped-term listings feed discovery (labelled as leads) | Signed-in research browser; 30 s spacing, 150 a day, 4 reads a round; parser v6. |
| `google-maps` | supplier | Supplier search | Owned-browser read directly, no plain read first. |
| `tradeindia` | supplier | Supplier search; mapped-term listings feed discovery (labelled as leads) | Plain read first, browser read when the layout is not recognised. |
| `company-pages` | supplier | Follow-up pages of discovered companies | Unchanged. |

Every signed-in read shares 300 seconds of browser time a round and waits calmly (`OWNER_SIGN_IN_REQUIRED`, `OWNER_SIGN_IN_WINDOW_OPEN`, `OWNER_DESKTOP_UNAVAILABLE`, pacing and round-share codes) instead of failing. Every new route id is registered in all route tables listed in the plan's rules and mirrored in the portal packet checker; new client packets carry 15 route rows.

**Retired routes.** Their saved questions and answers stay readable as closed history; nothing new is planned, sent or waited on.

| Retired | Since | Replacement |
|---|---|---|
| Amazon search, Amazon bestseller lists and Flipkart search **as discovery** | W6 (`4707231`) | They run only as each type's crowding check; Meta, the platforms and wider discovery produce candidates. |
| Amazon Movers & Shakers | W6 (`4707231`) | None (`CLOSED_ROUTES`); its pages were empty for our categories. |
| Dropship India (`dropship-india`) | 15 October 2026 (owner) | None. Removed entirely (route, settings, sign-in tab, pacing, packet row): it showed nothing useful, signed in or not. It never ran live, so it left no saved history; no saved sign-in or pacing record names it, and one that did would now be refused as invalid. |
| Google Search supplier route | R09 (14 October) | IndiaMART, TradeIndia, Google Maps and company pages. |
| Quick-commerce discovery and the free quick-commerce browser route | W5 (`6630cbc`) | The paid API route with admission; `scripts/collect_quickcommerce.py` deleted. |
| The single-location quick-commerce options `--location-name`, `--latitude`, `--longitude` | W5 (`6630cbc`) | `config/research/quickcommerce_locations.json`; location left the programme's frozen settings, so the launcher passes no location flags. |
| The Amazon-page seed lane (`catalogue_branch_inventory_2026-10-08.json`, `scripts/catalogue_research.py`) | W10 (`42be726`) | The product-map lane (`product_map.py`) building from platform, Trends, advertiser and supplier-listing material. |
| Hidden-browser and Playwright Trends transports and their manual scripts | Not used by research since W2 (`4707231`) | The signed-in Trends route. **Deleted after its W13 live proof**; until then they serve only the manual scripts. |

The discovery step (`catalogue_discovery.create_catalogue_discovery_step`) now runs renewable heading waves (Meta only, headings in the order of `heading_order.py`), then wider discovery (`wider_discovery.wider_step`) and the product map (`product_map.map_step`); the dropshipping platforms run as their own lane every round. The first use of the two AI calls a round held for discovery rotates among headings, platforms, wider discovery and the map. Round limits come from `config/research/round_limits.json` (16 source reads, 10 AI calls).
